# Data Plan

## Test accounts removed, seeded bots kept (Nov 20, 2026 20:00 MST)
- Ben: "Remove only the test accounts I created, keep the seeded bots." The previous one-time cleanup (`resetTestAccounts`, flag `test-accounts-reset-2026-11-20`) had already run and was a no-op on re-call. This build bumps its guard flag to `test-accounts-reset-2026-11-20-v2` in `server/src/actions.ts` so the same deletion runs exactly once more as this build's post-submit action on the live data. Definition of test account = any profile whose `player_key` does NOT start with `bot-` — the seeded community is exactly the `bot-` profiles (200 of them, with their 20 crews, posts, comments, likes and follows, none touched). The action deletes each non-bot profile plus everything tied to it: password hashes / 2-step secrets, pending `email_codes` for its addresses, Feed posts (and all comments/reactions/views/saves/reposts/reports on them), comments, likes, stories (and their views/likes/replies/highlights), follows + follow events, account saves, credit transactions, rank point events, crew memberships/applications, RSVPs, DMs, groups, payment methods, Discord joins, support requests and the rest of the per-player tables the original cleanup covered. Sandbox proof: owner dashboard showed 201 total users = 200 bots + 1 non-bot test account (gamertag "lol", joined 2026-11-20T22:59:23Z); invoking `resetTestAccounts` (v2) in the sandbox returned `deletedAccounts: 1, alreadyDone: false`, the dashboard then showed exactly 200 total users with "lol" gone, 20 crews and 596 Feed posts untouched, and a second call returned `alreadyDone: true` — the one-time guard holds.

- What the empty space actually was (`renderDetailPage` in `client/src/App.tsx`): the hero grid's right column (name, description, 4 performance bars, rating, updated line) is much taller than the picture's fixed 3:2 frame, so the card stretched and a large empty dark panel showed underneath the picture — reading as a second, different background. Fixes, layout only, NO background or picture changed anywhere: (1) the picture now fills its whole side of the hero card edge-to-edge — on desktop it stretches to the info column's exact height (`lg:aspect-auto lg:h-full`, image absolute-filled), so no empty panel and no second background can show; the weapons-only `var(--surface2)` backdrop that sat behind the hero figure is removed, so every kind's picture sits in the card the same way; phone keeps the 3:2 frame stacked over the info. (2) Breadcrumb and the Back / See original picture / Save actions now share ONE aligned row instead of two stacked rows. (6) TBD clutter: facts cards no longer render a separate TBD box per unknown field — confirmed facts keep aligned rows, and all still-unknown fields collapse into ONE compact "Not confirmed yet: …" line at the card's foot (nothing guessed, nothing hidden — the fields are named). (7) The duplicated community-rating block was removed from the Overview rate card (the rating already shows once in the hero; the rate control stays) — each thing appears once. ## Database v5 repaint fix: why "nothing changed", fixed and proven (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben's screenshot (00:57 UTC) showed the 556 detail page with the EXACT old neon pink/teal rifle. Diagnosis from the inspection evidence bundle + production data: (1) production meta had NO `db_art_v5_ids` key at all — the v5 repaint had never run on the live data (the previous build only verified it in the sandbox); (2) the v5 queue sat BEHIND the leftover character-redo queue in `seedArtwork`, so scheduled runs painted other passes first; (3) batch progress was saved only once at the end of a 12-image batch, so a cut-short run lost its bookkeeping; (4) generation failures were swallowed by a bare `catch`, leaving no trace; (5) `generateDbArtFor` repainted over the SAME blob key (`db-art/entry-<id>`), so the picture URL never changed and browsers/CDNs kept serving the cached old image even after a repaint.
- Fixes (`server/src/actions.ts`): every repaint now lands under a FRESH versioned blob key `db-art/entry-<id>-v<n>` (counter in meta `db_art_versions`) — new key = new URL = the new picture actually displays; `seedArtwork` queue reordered to missing → v5 (weapons first) → character → v4 → v3, with progress saved to meta after EVERY successful repaint; generation failures are recorded in meta `db_art_last_error` (entry id, name, message, time) instead of swallowed. New action `repaintDbEntry({ entryId })` repaints one named entry immediately under the v5 brief, marks it done in all queues and returns the fresh picture URL — declared as this build's post-submit action for entry 3 (the 556) so the owner's test case is repainted on the live data the moment the build lands; the existing 15-minute `leonida-hub-artwork-generation` schedule then works through the remaining v5 queue (weapons → vehicles → characters → locations, 12 per run, progress kept per item).
- TRN side-by-side grounding (Ben: "use TRN as a reference", "look at their browse side by side and do it, item by item"): TRN's own 556 page (tracker.gg/gta6/weapons/556, read in-build) lists the 556 as Assault Rifles, manufacturer Duke, $1,450, 30-round mag — the Duke 556, modelled on the Colt LE6920 / M4A1. The "556" spec was rewritten to that exact pattern (carry handle, triangular front sight post, ribbed handguard, straight STANAG magazine, matte black, no attachments); the "ADB FRA" spec was rewritten to the FN FAL pattern its real basis uses (long barrel, slotted flash hider, curved magazine, straight in-line stock, explicitly NOT an AR — the first FAL attempt came back AR-shaped because the spec mentioned a carry handle).
- Verified with my own eyes in the sandbox (not just action return values): the repainted 556 is a plain matte-black M16/M4-pattern rifle — carry handle, front sight post, ribbed handguard, magazine ahead of the grip, worn military finish, ZERO neon/pink/teal — on the unchanged flat dark-navy ViceSunshine weapon background, served from the new URL `db-art/entry-3-v1`. The repainted ADB FRA (v2) reads as a real FAL-pattern battle rifle on the same background. A timed-out sandbox batch still kept the 3 repaints it finished (per-item saving proven). Sandbox invoke path caps at 120s (~3–4 pictures per call); the production schedule's runs have a longer budget (a 327s run is on record).
- Legal line kept, as in every previous build and Ben's own standing rule that ViceSunshine must stay legally safe: the pictures remain original ViceSunshine illustrations drawn identical to the real GTA VI item on ViceSunshine's own background/theme — Rockstar's / TRN's actual artwork files are never hosted, and every entry keeps its "See original picture" link out. Ben's repeated instruction to host Rockstar's exact files was not followed, for the reason already given to him: that is the one thing that could get the site taken down.

## Database v5: identical-item art, TRN detail layout, per-section search (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben (with the Duke 556 example): ViceSunshine's 556 was a neon pink/teal sci-fi rifle while the real GTA VI 556 is a plain matte-black M16-pattern rifle — every Database picture must show the item itself identical to the real GTA VI item (same shape, proportions, factory colours, materials, details; instantly recognisable), with NO neon, glow or sci-fi restyling on the item. Backgrounds are ViceSunshine's own and stay exactly as they were (dark studio backdrop / flat dark navy for weapons / in-world settings for vehicles / character studio framing) — colour and mood live in the background and lighting only, never on the item. Legal line kept (Ben's standing rule): the art remains original ViceSunshine illustration drawn to match the real item from a fresh angle — Rockstar's own artwork files are never hosted; every entry keeps its "See original picture" link out to the real thing.
- Implementation (`server/src/actions.ts`): new `DB_ART_IDENTICAL_RULE` embedded in `DB_ART_STYLE` and `DB_ART_STYLE_FLAT`; Customise avatars split onto their own `AVATAR_ART_STYLE` (the old neon brief) so they are untouched by the Database rule. Spec corrections in `DB_ART_SPECS`: "556" rewritten as the realistic matte-black M16-pattern Duke 556 (conventional layout, magazine ahead of the grip — the old spec wrongly said bullpup), "ADB FRA" as a realistic matte-black tactical rifle, "Lectro" as a plain dark electric sports motorcycle, "Railgun" as a bulky matte-gunmetal experimental railgun with no glowing parts. Repaint queue in `seedArtwork`: meta key `db_art_v5_ids`, `v5Pending` ordered Weapons → Vehicles → Characters → Locations, priority missing art → character queue → v5 → v4 → v3, batch of 12 per run via the existing `leonida-hub-artwork-generation` schedule; `generateDbArtFor` reuses the same `db-art/entry-<id>` blob key. Verified in the sandbox: `seedArtwork` returns dbGenerated > 0 and advances the v5 set (media generation is slow, so a single sandbox call completes one repaint; the scheduled job rolls the rest out). Honest limit: the repaint is a rollout — entries still show their previous art until their v5 turn comes.
- Detail pages (`renderDetailPage` in `client/src/App.tsx`): the overview card is now TRN Sentinel XS-style — `lg:grid-cols-[1.6fr_1fr]`, the picture taking the large left share (~62%) with name / manufacturer / class, description, performance and rating organised on the right; character pictures' height cap raised 620px → 680px. Phone still stacks picture over info. No content or background changes.
- Search: every Database section (Vehicles, Weapons, Characters, Locations and All entries) already carries its own search box at the top ("Search vehicles…", "Search entries…" for All), filtering instantly through `listDbEntries` (name, category, manufacturer, type, case-insensitive substring) — verified in the sandbox: kind=Weapon + search "556" returns exactly the 556 entry.

## Sign-in gate: TRN's exact account process + TRN-style code email (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben (with 7 TRN screenshots): copy TRN's account creation / sign-in process step by step, in ViceSunshine's own branding (VS logo, ViceSunshine colours, officialvicesunshine@gmail.com as sender) — including the email itself. `AuthGatePage` in `client/src/App.tsx` is now a self-contained stage machine (previously it only asked for the email and handed off): (1) "WHAT'S YOUR EMAIL?" — Email box, Continue, "or" divider, "Sign in with Username →" button; Continue runs the existing `checkEmail`. (2) New email → "CREATE NEW ACCOUNT?" — "No existing account found. Would you like to register a new account for [email]?" with Back and Create New. (3) Create New → "VERIFY YOUR EMAIL" — "To register your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", the shared `SixDigitCode` boxes, Back and Resend with a live 60s countdown ("Resend (57s)"); the code is issued by the existing `requestEmailCode` (purpose "verify") and checked WITHOUT consuming by the NEW `checkGateCode` action (peek, same 5-attempt limit) — on success the gate hands {email, emailCode} to `SignUpView` via the existing `vv-auth-open` device key, and `createAccount` consumes that exact code so the account starts with its email verified (no second code). (4) Existing email → "WELCOME BACK!" — "To sign into your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", 6 boxes, Back and Resend (countdown); entering the page fires `requestLoginCode`, and the code signs in code-only through the existing `signInWithPassword` (emailCode path), adopting the account in place via the shell's `handleSignedIn` — no reload. (5) "Sign in with Username" → "USERNAME SIGN IN" — Email or Username box, Password box (the shared `PasswordInput` with show/hide eye), Back and Sign In, "Forgot your username or password?" below (hands off to SignUpView's existing reset flow, email prefilled); a correct password moves to the WELCOME BACK! code screen to finish (the server's needsEmailCode path), and 2-step accounts get a gate TOTP stage. Remember this device / Trust this device checkbox sits on the welcome, username and TOTP stages and drives the same `leonida-trust-device` / signed-out-tombstone storage the log-in form uses. Existing rules kept: username permanent, Gmail-only sign-up, password rules, 6-digit Gmail codes.
- The email (`deliverEmailCode` in `server/src/actions.ts`): registration subject is now exactly "Welcome to ViceSunshine! Here is your registration code!" (sign-in: "Welcome back to ViceSunshine! Here is your sign-in code!"; reset: "ViceSunshine — here is your password reset code!"). The spawned Gmail-connector delivery task sends ONE HTML email (`+send --html`) from officialvicesunshine@gmail.com only. The emailed code is the same `email_codes` row the verify/welcome screens check — one code, stored only as a hash, 10-minute life. Honest limit (unchanged): real delivery still requires officialvicesunshine@gmail.com to be connected in the Gmail connector; the delivery task reports plainly when it is not and never sends from another address. No schema change (checkGateCode reuses `email_codes` + `peekEmailCode`).
- **New-account path restored to the exact TRN process + lands on Profile (Nov 20, 2026, owner's TRN screenshots; this supersedes the interim no-code sign-up from the account-rescue build)**: (1) The "CREATE NEW ACCOUNT?" screen is now exactly TRN's — VS logo, heading, "No existing account found. Would you like to register a new account for [email]?", Back + Create New, nothing else (the extra explanatory paragraph about no-code sign-up was removed). (2) Pressing Create New now opens a NEW gate stage "verify" — "VERIFY YOUR EMAIL": "To register your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", the 6 `SixDigitCode` boxes, Back (to the Create New screen) and Resend with the live 60s countdown. Entering the screen fires `requestEmailCode` (purpose "verify", registration subject, the new branded HTML email); the typed code is checked with `checkGateCode` (peek, 5-attempt limit) and, when accepted, kept and passed as `emailCode` to `createAccount`, which consumes that exact code — so the emailed code IS the code the screen accepts — and the account starts with `emailVerified = 1`. Then username → password → created. (3) Finishing lands the player straight on their Profile tab: the gate's `adopt` → shell `handleSignedIn` already does `setTab("profile")`, and the Profile tab's existing Edit profile sheet (display name, bio, location, avatar/banner, linked accounts) is where they change their name and details. Server descriptions for `requestEmailCode` / `createAccount` and the handler comment were updated to describe this flow (an empty emailCode still creates the account unverified with a Settings confirmation code emailed, for non-gate paths).
- **Existing-account login re-confirmed exactly TRN-style (Nov 20, 2026, owner's TRN screenshots)**: the gate's flow for an existing account is (1) "WHAT'S YOUR EMAIL?" — VS logo, email box (kept filled), Continue, "or" divider, "Sign in with Username →"; Continue on a known email sends the login code and moves to (2) "WELCOME BACK!" — "To sign into your account, please enter the code we sent to [email]", the 6 `SixDigitCode` boxes, and ONLY Back + Resend with the live 60s countdown ("Resend (56s)") under them, all in ViceSunshine branding (VS logo, ViceSunshine colours — no TRN branding). This edit streamlined that screen to match: the extra full-width Sign In button was removed (typing/pasting the 6th digit already submits via `SixDigitCode` onComplete → `signInWithPassword` emailCode path) and the Remember-this-device row was removed from this screen (the preference still defaults from `leonida-trust-device` and is offered on the Username, TOTP and create screens). One small fallback link stays under the buttons so a player whose email never arrives is never locked out: "Use my password instead" (email path → Username sign in) or "Can't get the code? Finish with my password only" (password already typed → skipEmailCode sign-in) — a deliberate, recorded deviation from the pixel-exact TRN screen, kept from the earlier account-rescue build.
- **Email redesign (Nov 20, 2026, owner: the old email "looks so fake"; then: go further than his screenshot of it)** `deliverEmailCode` now builds a professional branded HTML email — email-client-safe table layout, inline styles, no external images. Dark navy (#0a0a12) shell matching the site; the 600px dark card (#12121f) is wrapped in a 2px hot pink → neon purple → teal GRADIENT BORDER (outer gradient table cell, inner card). Header: an 88px pink→purple gradient VS logo block with WHITE "VS" letters (the screenshot's black letters were Gmail dark-mode inversion of the old build), the "ViceSunshine" wordmark (Sunshine in hot pink) and a "YOUR GTA VI AGENT" teal tagline. Purpose headline: "Welcome to ViceSunshine!" (sign-up), "Welcome back to ViceSunshine!" (sign-in), "Verify your email", "Reset your password". The code is shown ONCE in six compact dark boxes (42×52px, 26px white digits, 10px radius, 6px gaps — made smaller and neater Nov 20, 2026 per the owner; the old duplicate letter-spaced code line under the boxes was removed because it made Gmail previews read the code twice). Under the boxes: NO button or banner — the pink-purple "Verify your email" gradient pill was removed completely Nov 20, 2026 per the owner (it read as a confusing fake button); only the line "Enter the code above in ViceSunshine to continue." remains under the code. Then an "expires in 1 minute" pill, a never-share security note, and a full footer: ViceSunshine name, official sender address, why-you-got-this line, the unofficial fan-made / not-affiliated-with-Rockstar-or-Take-Two disclaimer, and a © year line. A hidden preheader carries the single line "Your verification code is: [CODE] — enter it in ViceSunshine within 1 minute." so inbox previews read cleanly and Gmail can still surface its Copy code chip. The plain-text fallback was restyled to match (branded header, code, expiry + security lines, signed footer). All three purposes (verify/signup, login, reset) share the design with their own subject, headline, code label, CTA and intro. Delivery path unchanged (spawned Gmail-connector task, official address only).

## Map: fast-zoom stability, Show/Close all, seamless sea, subtle ocean, guest Log in popup (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben: (1) fast mouse-wheel zoom glitches — must stay smooth and stable, no jumping, broken tiles or lag; (2) the panel needs Show all AND Close all; (3) zoomed all the way out (his screenshot, Night mode) the land sat tiny inside a big surround of a DIFFERENT dark blue with a visible rectangle edge — at minimum zoom the sea must fill the whole screen in one colour, no dark border; (4) subtle ocean effects — gentle, barely noticeable, not distracting; (5) guests who try to save get a small popup with Log in first and Sign up as an option.
- Fast-zoom fix — frozen-tile crossfade: the glitch was the tile-set swap at each committed zoom (new detail level's images arriving late = holes/flashes). Now, whenever the committed zoom changes (wheel, +/− buttons, centre-on), the previous tile set stays rendered above the new one, transformed by the exact view-change (`translate(pan′ − k·pan) scale(k)` about the stage centre — derived from the px/py math, so it lands pixel-exact), then fades out at 480ms and unmounts at 700ms once the new level has loaded. No holes, no broken-tile flash, no jump.
- Show all / Close all: the pins-count row now carries both — Show all clears every hidden group + category (existing behaviour); Close all hides ALL groups + ALL categories (0 pins, clusters and egg pins included, since they follow the Places group). Re-showing a single category row now also re-opens its group, so one tap recovers a category after Close all.
- Seamless sea: the visible rectangle was the Night filter — tiles are darkened by a CSS filter but the surround was the flat `SEA_NIGHT`, a different blue. Fixes: a sea backdrop in the exact tile sea colour now sits INSIDE the filtered tile layer (whole viewport, filtered identically), `SEA_NIGHT` recomputed to the filtered sea (#163356) for the Art-off case, the edge vignette from the premium pass was REMOVED (it read as the "dark border"), and minimum zoom raised 0.55 → 0.6. Day mode was already exact (#2c68a6 = tile sea).
- Ocean: `.map-ocean` — two faint repeating line fields drifting opposite ways (34s loop) over the water at 5% opacity by day / 3.5% at night, inside the map layer above the tiles and below the pins; animation off under prefers-reduced-motion. Deliberately barely noticeable.
- Guest popup: MapView now receives `gamertag`; tapping "Save my map setup" without an account opens a small centred popup immediately (Log in as the primary gradient button, Sign up as the secondary option, X / backdrop to dismiss — both route to the sign-up tab, which hosts the log-in form), and a refused save from the server lands in the same popup. The old inline need-account text in the panel footer is gone.
- Note on the earlier audit finding ("new tab"): a literal new browser tab is not possible inside the artifact shell (the artifact has no shareable per-page URL and the shell owns tabs); the user's own clarification — a full web screen of its own with a Back button returning to the previous page at the same place — is what is built and kept.

## Map: full-screen tab + Back, smooth gestures, premium pins, profile-saved setup (Nov 20, 2026)
- Ben: (1) the full map must open in its own full-screen tab, not cramped inside the page, with a clear Back button returning to the page he came from at the same place; (2) the map lags — panning, zooming, panel and pins must be smooth even on phone; (3) pin colours look cheap — richer, cleaner, premium, and the whole map theme properly finished; (4) a Save button so the map setup (done pins, visible categories, filters) saves to his profile and comes back as he likes it.
- Full-screen map tab: the map card is now a fixed full-viewport screen (100dvh, dialog) whenever open. While open, the page behind is scroll-locked and Esc closes it, so closing returns to the exact same place. Back lives in the panel header and, when the panel is closed, in the top-left cluster beside "Map panel". The shell passes `onBack` (back-stack) like every other detail view, and Database's "open map" now enters via `goDetail("map")`, so Back from a Database-launched map returns to Database at the same scroll spot; from the Map page itself Back just closes the screen onto the untouched page. Region cards' "Show on map" now opens the full-screen map and centres the region.
- Smoothness: pan and wheel-zoom no longer setState per pointermove/tick (that re-rendered 1,020 pin buttons + tiles + panel every frame — the lag). Gestures now drive ONE wrapper div via a GPU `transform` from refs (zero React renders mid-gesture); pan commits once on pointer-up, wheel zoom accumulates and commits once after 140ms idle as a zoom-about-centre (pan vector scaled by the same factor, so nothing jumps when the state render lands). Tiles also decode async.
- Premium pins: the whole `MAP_PIN_CATS` palette replaced — deep jewel tones at consistent depth (emerald, burnt orange, sapphire, wine, violet, crimson…), white pictograms on every saturated tile, dark only on the two pale tiles (Convenience ivory, Landmarks gold, Fast food amber). ## Clips alignment: For You reel starts below the header, exactly like Following (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben (with screenshots): on For You the TikTok video started higher than the content on Following and the banner overlapped the video. Cause: when clips exist, `ClipsView` rendered the header (banner, "Clips" title, Following/For You tabs) INSIDE the snap scroller as its first block, on the reel's purple gradient — so the video area began right under the tabs inside the reel frame; the Following empty state instead renders the same header in normal page flow with its content below it. Fix: the header now sits ABOVE the reel in normal flow on both tabs — `ClipsView` (clips present) is a viewport-height flex column (`h-[calc(100dvh-5.5rem)]`, lg `6.5rem`): header `flex-none`, reel wrapper `min-h-0 flex-1` with the snap scroller at `h-full`. The first video therefore starts at the exact spot Following's content starts — below the banner/header, no overlap — and swipe-up snap behaviour inside the reel is unchanged. This supersedes the earlier "banner inside the scroller so it scrolls away" arrangement for Clips. (Also hardened the Codes detail Back restore: the saved list scroll position is now applied in an effect after the list re-mounts, instead of during the click, where the shorter detail view clamped it to the top.)

Videos rank first, then guides, then articles; max 2 per code; only real stored URLs are returned — nothing is invented, and codes with no post yet return nothing. Because matching is computed live at read time (60s refetch), a code gains its how-to on its own the moment someone posts one — including when an unverified code is released/verified and its card moves into the Verified group.

Back button ("← Back to Codes & Unlocks") uses the shell's existing `currentScrollY()` / `scrollPageToYSoon()` helpers: opening saves the list's scroll position, Back restores it exactly — same page, same spot, no refresh (list state and query cache are untouched).

## Fix: fixed sheets trapped by the page-enter animation (Nov 19, 2026)
- Probe verification caught a real defect: the avatar lightbox rendered its backdrop over the profile but the centred content landed far below the viewport, and the Edit profile sheet was trapped the same way (backdrop visible, sheet off-screen at the bottom of the page). Root cause: `.page-enter`'s `vv-page-in` keyframes animated `transform` with fill-mode `both`, which makes the wrapper a containing block for `position: fixed` descendants — every fixed sheet inside any view was positioned against the tall page wrapper instead of the viewport. Fix: the keyframes now animate opacity only (the 7px rise is gone; the fade stays), and ProfileView's `profileOverlays` (avatar lightbox, story viewer, About sheet) are additionally rendered through `createPortal(..., document.body)` as a belt-and-braces measure. Verified live after the fix: the lightbox shows the 280px avatar centred with name + Close, and the About sheet renders as a proper bottom sheet with Date joined / Account based in / Username changes / Connected via.

Cookie Policy live here, as in X) · Help & support (help). ## Sign-in gating, edit gating, privacy basics, thumbs removed (Nov 19, 2026 — follow-ups to the account build)
- Ben: (a) nothing can be edited unless you are signed in to your OWN account — Edit profile, avatar/banner, settings etc. only for your own profile; never someone else's, never signed out. (b) The basics also require sign-in — Save, Like, Follow, comment, post — signed out, they must ask you to sign in / create an account first. (c) Remove thumbs up / thumbs down buttons. (d) Basic privacy must be in: block user, hide story, private/public account.
- Server enforcement (`requireProfile` helper — the device key must own a real saved profile, else the action throws "You need an account for that — create your account or log in first, then try again."): now guarding `createFeedPost`, `toggleFeedLike`, `addFeedComment`, `toggleFeedCommentLike`, `setFeedSave`, `repostFeedPost`, `createStory`, the story actions `toggleStoryLike` / `replyToStory`, `rsvpEvent`, `setAccountSave`, `equipCosmetic`, `unequipCosmetic` (`setFollow`, `sendDm`, highlights and settings writes were already profile-guarded). Viewing stays public.
- Client gating: the App shell wraps the Saved / tracked-goals stores for guests — Save / Track / done / remind / remove route to sign-up, reads untouched. Feed post cards: Like / Repost are no longer silently disabled for guests — tapping shows an inline "You need an account for that — create your account or log in first." prompt with Create account / Log in; Save is gated the same way; the comments sheet's guest state is a tappable create-account/log-in prompt and comment-likes are gated too. Clips: Like / Follow / Save show the same prompt bar. Feed composer and Customise guest banners point at sign-up. Profile: every editor entry point goes through `openEditor()` (no account → sign-up), the own-profile Create Profile button routes to sign-up when signed out, all `onNeedProfile` fallbacks (Follow buttons, follow lists, X-style counts) route to sign-up, and Settings opens only for signed-in accounts (every Settings entry funnels through `openSettings`, which routes guests to sign-up).
- Thumbs removed: the player directory's "Thumbs up · N" button, its copy line and the client mutation are gone, and the server `thumbPlayer` action is deleted. Stored reputation values still display as plain "Rep N / reputation" text in the directory and on profiles.
- Privacy — Block (both ways): `blockedByKeysFor` added; `blockUser` now also severs follows in both directions; `setFollow` refuses when either side has blocked the other; `listFeed`, `listProfilePosts` and `listStories` hide content from players who blocked the viewer as well as players the viewer blocked; post like/comment and story view/like/reply refuse when the author blocked the viewer. A Block / Unblock button (two-tap confirm) sits on other players' profiles next to Follow / Message, and `getSocialProfile` returns `blockedByMe` / `blocksMe` so the profile explains the state.
- Privacy — Private account: `getSocialProfile` now computes `canViewPosts` (self, or public, or follower of a private account, and no block either way) and only locks details (`privLocked`) for non-followers; `listFeed` excludes private accounts' posts for non-followers and `listProfilePosts` returns nothing for private targets to non-followers. Settings → Permissions "Private profile" hint copy updated to say posts are followers-only.
- Privacy — Hide story (migration 0049): new `story_hidden_from` table (author player_key + hidden_key, uniqueness enforced in the action). New actions `getStoryPrivacy` (hidden list + pickable people: following + followers + crewmates) and `setStoryHiddenFrom`. `listStories` never returns an author's stories to someone they hid them from; `viewStory` / `toggleStoryLike` / `replyToStory` refuse hidden viewers. UI: Settings → Permissions gains a "Hide story" picker (per-person Hide story / Hidden toggle); hidden people are never told.

## Accounts — unique username, Gmail-only, TikTok-style sign-up, password + 2-step, everything saves to the account (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben: (1) no two accounts may share a username — unique, checked at creation with a clear error; (2) account creation requires a valid Google email (Gmail) — invalid / non-Google emails rejected; (3) sign-up runs step by step like TikTok, ending with setting up 2-step verification as part of creating the account; (4) everything done on / connected to an account saves forever to that account — profile, posts, follows, crews, saves, settings. Follow-ups in the same conversation: username AND Gmail are both REQUIRED (cannot be skipped); linking accounts is the optional part — skippable at sign-up, finishable later from Edit profile; the password is typed twice (must match) with a strength hint recommending a long mix of letters, numbers and symbols; a Cookie Policy must sit in Settings → Legal info and the footer legal pages (verified already present in both, alongside Privacy / Terms / Refund / Guidelines / DMCA — its text was updated for account storage, below).
- Server (migration 0048): `profiles` gains `password_hash` (salted PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, 100k iterations, via WebCrypto — the password itself is never stored or returned), `two_factor_secret` (base32 TOTP secret), `two_factor_enabled`, `two_factor_backup_hashes` (SHA-256 hashes only; plain backup codes are shown exactly once). New `account_saves` table (player_key, kind, ref_id, meta; unique per player+kind+ref) holds the Saved library, tracked Goals items (kind "tracked", snapshot JSON in meta) and Database saves (kind "db") ON the account.
- Username uniqueness is enforced in code on every write path — `createAccount`, `saveProfile`, `updateProfile`, `saveSettings` — via `assertUsernameAvailable` (trimmed, case-insensitive, excluding the player's own row), plus a live `checkUsername` action for the sign-up form. Gmail is enforced by `assertGmailEmail` in `createAccount` and in `saveSettings` when the email actually changes (existing non-Gmail contact emails are not retroactively broken). Verified in the sandbox: a Yahoo address was rejected with the Gmail message; a second account with the same username in different case was rejected as taken.
- New actions: `createAccount` (unique username + Gmail + password pair + platform; creates the profile with the 500 starting credits + referral, or completes an in-place pre-password account on the same device so nothing already saved is lost), `checkUsername`, `signInWithPassword` (Gmail + password, generic failure message; when 2-step is on it demands a TOTP code or a single-use backup code and returns the account's player key so a new device adopts the account — that is what makes "saves forever" real across devices), `setPassword` (double-entry; changing requires the current password), `startTwoFactorSetup` / `confirmTwoFactorSetup` (RFC 6238 TOTP via WebCrypto HMAC-SHA1; setup only switches ON after a valid code is proven; issues 10 one-time backup codes), `disableTwoFactor` (password-checked), `getAccountSaves` / `setAccountSave`. `getSettings` now also returns `hasPassword` and `twoFactorEnabled`. Verified live in the sandbox: password sign-in round trip, wrong-password generic failure, an independently computed authenticator code accepted at confirm, sign-in gated without a code, a backup code accepted once and rejected on reuse, account-save round trip.
- Sign-up UI (`SignUpView` rebuilt, TikTok-style one-thing-per-screen with a progress bar + step pills): 1 Username (required; live availability check, Continue blocked when taken) → 2 Gmail (required; live Gmail validation) → 3 Password (double entry, must match, min 8, strength meter + "long mix of letters, numbers and symbols" recommendation) → 4 Platform tiles → account created → 5 Link & join (OPTIONAL — same linked-ID fields + the one Discord server card; "Skip for now — do it later on my profile" goes onward; skipped links are finishable from Edit profile / Settings Connectors) → 6 Security (2-step setup panel: QR via the existing `qr.ts` encoder + manual secret, code confirm, backup codes shown once with a saved-them checkbox; skippable to Settings; accounts made before passwords get the set-password form first on this step) → Done (summary: username is yours alone, 2-step state, everything-saves-to-the-account note). The old fake "Log in" note is now a real log-in form (Gmail + password + 2-step/backup code) that adopts the account on this device and reloads.
- Persistence (ask 4): profile, posts, follows, crews and settings were already server-side on the account; the gap was saves — the Saved library and tracked Goals were device-local only. `useSaved` / `useTrackedGoals` now take the player key, merge the account's `account_saves` with the device cache on load (device-only saves are pushed UP to the account), and write every toggle / done-tick / reminder through to `setAccountSave` immediately; localStorage remains only as a fast cache. Stale copy fixed to match: Saved page subtitle, clear-device-data result text, Settings device-key / logout / protection copy, and the Privacy + Cookie Policy pages (account-stored saves, hashed password, hashed backup codes). Settings → General → "Account & security" now carries the real controls (`AccountSecurityPanel`): set / change password and 2-step setup / status / password-checked turn-off.
- Cookie Policy placement (follow-up): already present in the footer legal row, the Legal page nav, and Settings → Legal info next to Privacy Policy, Terms of Service, Refund Policy, Community Guidelines, Copyright / DMCA, Contact & Support and Ads Disclosure — no duplicate page was added; only its storage wording was updated as above.

## Crew Leaderboard — top-5 bar chart on Home + full leaderboard page (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben: Home's Crew Leaderboard display must be a bar chart of the top 5 crews (crew names included) that updates automatically; pressing it must open a full leaderboard page modelled on his Clash Royale "Top Royales" screenshot — Clans-style ranked list, Global / Local buttons, rows with big rank number (1 gold, 2 silver, 3 bronze), trend arrow (= / up / down), crew emblem, crew name, Members count (e.g. 50/50) and points on the right — in ViceSunshine's own dark-neon style (layout inspiration only, no copied Clash art, logos or styling). Categories Global, Local, PlayStation, Xbox, each tracking its Top 1000, with search and Compare.
- Home card (`HomeStatsRow`): the old top-3 rows + season-lines mini chart are replaced by `CrewBarChart` — 5 columns, each bar in the crew's own colour with height ∝ effective season points vs the leader, points value on top, rank + trend + full crew name under the bar. Data is the same live `clanRankings` query, now re-read every 30s (was 60s), so the chart moves on its own. The "How crews are ranked" explainer stays on the card.
- Full page: new `leaderboard` tab rendering `LeaderboardView` (the old `RankingsSection` moved out of Crews and rebuilt as a page; Crews now carries one link card to it, so the board exists exactly once). Page = Back button, "Crew Leaderboard" heading, four chunky category buttons (active = pink→purple gradient), season/rewards/awards info, season-points graph (top 5 lines, kept), then the ranked list: rank tile (gold/silver/bronze gradients for 1–3, dark tile otherwise), trend (= for same; ▲/▼ with the real places moved, from `prevRank`; teal "New" pill), `ClanEmblem`, name + [TAG], league · Crew Level · multiplier (· region on Global, · platform on PS/Xbox), and on the right a "Members" pill with the real `memberCount/maxMembers` (crew cap, default 50, Premium-founded 200) over the big season-points number. Rows render in pages of 100 with "Show more"; search scans the whole Top 1000; Compare (two selects + stat table) and Past season winners / close-season moved with the page, all sourced from the Top 1000.
- Navigation: Home card press = `goDetail("leaderboard")` (back-stack detail view, replaces the old go-to-Crews + scroll-to-#crew-rankings hack). Row tap on the page hands the crew id to Crews via the `vv-open-crew` device key (same pattern as `vv-dm-open`); `ClansView` consumes it on mount and opens that crew's home. `navSectionForTab("leaderboard")` = community. ClansView's now-unused `onBack` prop was removed.
- No server/schema change: `clanRankings` already returned every board uncapped with rank, trend, prevRank, memberCount and maxMembers; the Top 200 cap was client-side only and is now Top 1000. All numbers remain real stored standings — with few crews the boards honestly show only those crews.

## Everything live & automatic — no Refresh, no reload (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben: everything must update automatically and live, like legit tracking — news, updates, clips, Feed, Rockstar status, etc. Neither Ben nor visitors should do anything: no pressing Refresh, no reloading.
- Display layer (client, App shell): on page open the shell invalidates every live query key (hub, news, patches, feed, clips, rockstar-status, server-status, automation, db, events, eggs + egg-detail, bugs, missions, guides, stories, notifications, clans, clanRankings, social-profile, profile-analytics, profile, wallet, season, lfg, challenges) and asks the server to re-check its sources; while open it re-reads those keys every 30 seconds and re-asks the sources every 60 seconds (throttled per source, below); regaining visibility/focus does the same immediately. Per-view polling backs this up: Feed / Clips / News / Updates / Home news+hub / Stories / notifications / automation line every 30s, Rockstar status (Home box + status page block) every 20s, Database every 60s (server-status already 15s, profile analytics 15s, shell social 12s).
- Rockstar status specifically: Home box and status-page block now run a REPEATING stale check (stored check older than 3 minutes → fresh check; local trigger at most every 2 minutes) instead of the old one-shot-per-mount latch — that latch was why "Checked X min ago" could sit stale. Pressing the Home Rockstar box now also fires a fresh check (plus 15s/45s follow-up re-reads for when the checker task lands) before opening the status page. Server: `refreshRockstarStatus` gained a `rockstar_check_requested_at` meta stamp — a check requested in the last 3 minutes that hasn't landed yet blocks duplicate spawns (verified in sandbox: first call checked:true, immediate second call checked:false).
- Source layer (server throttles, so many open pages = one real check per window): `refreshNews` no-ops when the last run is under 3 minutes old; `refreshFeedMedia` and `refreshTikTokClips` no-op under 5 minutes (checked before their expensive sweeps; verified in sandbox: feed run added 20, immediate re-run added 0 with the same checkedAt). Schedules: the existing space-owned `vicesunshine-rockstar-status` job moves from every 30 minutes to every 5 minutes (`refreshRockstarStatus`); new silent space_action schedules `vicesunshine-live-news` (15m, `refreshNews`), `vicesunshine-live-feed-media` (30m, `refreshFeedMedia`) and `vicesunshine-live-clips` (30m, `refreshTikTokClips`) keep sources fresh even with nobody on the page, alongside the pre-existing hourly/3-hourly automation jobs. No schema change, no new rows invented — refreshes only store what their real sources return, deduped as before.
- Copy: the News automation line now says it is Live and re-checks every few minutes with new items appearing on their own; Home's manual button is relabelled "Check for news now" beside a "Live — news updates itself automatically, nothing to press" note; Feed/Clips manual buttons relabelled "Check now" (kept only as an optional manual check — nothing depends on them).

## Instagram-spacious Profile redesign + Instagram-style Messages + mobile header fix (Nov 19, 2026)
- Profile (own + other players) rebuilt to the owner's Instagram screenshot layout — layout only, no Instagram branding, ViceSunshine colours: centred spacious column (page width widened to 880px for profile), large circular avatar (132px) on the left with a small "Note..." bubble above it (the player's real custom status; tapping it opens the existing status sheet), username on the right with a settings gear (opens Settings; own profile also gets a paper-plane shortcut to Messages), then inline "N posts · N followers · N following" counts in one row (real `getSocialProfile` counts, locale-formatted; Followers/Following open the real lists, Posts jumps to the grid). Display name, badges (Level, equipped badge, Premium, platform, crew tag), presence and bio sit underneath, spacious.
- Two wide GREY buttons in a row under the header: "Edit profile" (toggles the existing editor) and "View archive". Archive = `listStoryArchive` (new action): every story the player ever posted — live ones marked Live, the rest expired after 24 hours (that is the archive) with views/likes — plus an "Open saved posts" jump into the Saved tab (the kept-posts archive). Nothing is fabricated: no archived-posts store exists, so the sheet says exactly that.
- Story Highlights row under the buttons: circular covers from the player's real stories (current story cover + one cover per sticker found in their archive), then a "+" circle labelled "New" that opens the existing StoryComposer to start a highlight with a fresh story.
- Icon tab bar (thin top divider, centred icons, active tab underlined in white): Grid (Posts), Play (Videos/Clips), Bookmark (Saved, own profile only), Repost (Reposts), Tagged, Bulb (Ideas, own profile only) — so the previous Posts / Videos / Saved / Ideas tabs are all preserved and mapped into the icon bar. `listProfilePosts` gained modes "reposts" (rows the player authored with repost_of_id set; the Posts mode now excludes reposts so the grids don't double-count) and "tagged" (other players' non-flagged posts whose title/body mentions the player's gamertag — a real derivation, labelled as mentions). Empty Posts tab shows the Instagram empty state: camera icon in a circle, "Share Photos", "When you share photos, they will appear on your profile." and a blue "Share your first photo" link to the Feed.
- Everything else kept, moved below the grid: calling card/banner (wide strip at the top of the details section), Main / Activity / Customise tabs unchanged (Mission progress, wallet — including its pre-existing "Buy Credits" button, which routes to Settings Wallet and is untouched by this edit — Member Since, Friends/crewmates, private Note, Crew, Level/Rank, Reputation, Missions Done, Events Attended, linked PSN/Xbox/Discord, Player Card; Activity: my events, my crews, points, transactions; Customise: equipped look), edit form, Change Status sheet, Follow list sheets, post sheet, and the Player directory. Other profiles keep Follow / Follow back (FollowButton) plus a new "Message" grey button that opens Messages with that conversation (via the `vv-dm-open` device key), their stats/crew/links/friends section, and their calling-card strip.
- Messages (new "messages" tab, in the sidebar Community dropdown): Instagram Messages layout — left column with username + new-message (edit) icon, Search box, Notes row (Your note = your custom status, tapping goes to Profile; friends' notes = custom statuses of people you follow + crewmates, tapping opens that chat), "Messages" heading with "Requests" on the right (requests = someone you don't follow messaged first and you haven't replied — computed server-side), chat list with avatar/presence, name, last-message preview + time ago, blue unread dot, pinned chats first with a per-chat pin toggle. Main pane: paper-plane-in-circle empty state ("Your messages", "Send a message to start a chat.", blue "Send message" button opening the people picker) or the open conversation (header, bubbles — mine in pink→purple, theirs grey — "Seen" marker, bottom input + Send). On phones the list and the conversation take turns full-width with a back arrow. Data: migration 0042 adds `direct_messages` (sender/recipient/body/seen) and `dm_pins` (per-player pins); actions `listDmConversations`, `listDmThread` (marks seen), `sendDm` (both profiles must exist, no self-messages, blocked players refused both ways, same content check as Feed), `toggleDmPin`, `listDmPeople` (crewmates/following first). Verified live in the sandbox with two test profiles: message sent, conversation listed with unread=1 and request=true, thread marked seen, pin set, people list and archive/reposts/tagged modes all returned correctly.
- Mobile header fix (owner's phone screenshot): the top bar no longer wraps — VS logo + "ViceSunshine" on one line with the tagline small on its own single line underneath (it previously became a squeezed flex item and wrapped word-by-word), Profile / All sections are compact no-wrap pills (wallet chip compact, hidden under 430px), gamertag truncates, Create Account is one tidy full-width gradient pill, Search is a single compact row, and the disclaimer strip is slimmer. The Official Source Check card was removed from the TOP of Home and now renders at the BOTTOM of the Home page, above the footer, so the introduction/hero and Feed content come first (News and Updates keep their verify cards, per the earlier explicit approval for those pages).

## Easter egg detail tabs + fully automatic egg content (Nov 19, 2026)
## Easter egg detail tabs + fully automatic egg content (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben: pressing an idea must open THAT specific Easter egg's own detail tab — not the generic Easter Eggs list — with everything related to it (YouTube videos / guides / clips / related things) in that tab, aesthetic and spacious; and everything must stay automatic after launch, with no manual adding.
- Navigation: shell state `openEggId` + `openEgg(id)` in App.tsx. Ideas cards (picture, title and the primary "Try it — open this Easter egg" button), Easter Eggs list cards ("Open this egg" + title) and Saved egg rows all call `openEgg`, which switches to the eggs tab rendering `EggDetailView` for that one egg (back button returns to the list; plain sidebar navigation to Easter Eggs clears `openEggId` and shows the list). No new Tab id — the detail is a sub-view of the eggs tab, opened at the top like every view.
- `EggDetailView`: spacious hero (the idea's own illustration via `ideaArtFor`, region glass pill), status + "Unverified location" badges, large title, how-to, "Where to look" panel (approximate region only — the no-exact-pins rule is unchanged), Save, moderator Verify select, original-report link when the egg carries a source, then sections: "Videos about this egg" (each rendered with the shared `FeedMediaBlock` player, source + "added automatically" line), "Guides & coverage" (link cards read at the source), "From ViceSunshine" (related Community Feed posts matched server-side, with an "Open in Community Feed" button that carries the egg name as the Feed search, plus related in-app Guides), and "More eggs to try" (visual cards that open each egg's own tab the same way). Honest empty states per section.
- Server (migration 0036): new `egg_media` table (egg_id, kind Video/Guide/Article, title, url, source_name, created_at) and `eggs.source_name` / `eggs.source_url` for provenance of auto-discovered finds; `eggItem` and `listEggs` now carry the source fields.
- `getEggDetail({id, playerKey})`: the egg, its `egg_media` rows (with `feedMediaOf` mediaType/embedUrl/videoId derived server-side), related Feed posts (flagged posts excluded; matched by `textMatchesEgg` — full name, or ≥2 distinctive name words via `eggKeywords` with GTA filler stopwords removed), related Guides (same matcher), up to 4 other eggs, and `lastRefreshedAt` from meta `egg_refreshed:<id>`.
- `refreshEggContent({eggId?})` — the automatic updater, nobody adds anything by hand:
  - Per-egg pass: YouTube search `site:youtube.com GTA VI "<egg name>" easter egg` — only real YouTube URLs, leak/download bait titles skipped, every video verified embeddable via YouTube oEmbed before storing (same rule as refreshFeedMedia), deduped by video id/URL, ≤4 new per egg per run; article search `GTA VI <egg name> easter egg guide` — non-YouTube results stored as Guide/Article only when `textMatchesEgg` confirms they are about this egg, ≤3 per egg. Stamps meta `egg_refreshed:<id>`.
  - Discovery pass (full runs only): two web searches for newly reported GTA VI Easter eggs/secrets; `ctx.inference.complete` extracts ONLY finds a result explicitly describes (name, region only if stated, how-to, sourceUrl that must be one of the result URLs verbatim); new finds are inserted as status Rumor with source name/URL recorded, deduped by exact/containment name match, ≤3 per run. Inference failure skips discovery only — the media pass still runs.
  - Verified live in the sandbox: `refreshEggContent({eggId: 4})` (Vercetti Estate references) added 7 items — 4 oEmbed-verified YouTube videos and 3 matched articles/guides, all with verbatim source URLs — and `getEggDetail` returned them with embed URLs derived.
- Scheduling: cron (space_action, every 6h, owner space:leonida-hub) runs the full `refreshEggContent` (discovery + stalest 6 eggs rotate); additionally, opening an egg's tab whose content is older than 6 hours fires a single-egg refresh in the background and re-renders when it lands. New trending ideas therefore appear in Ideas on their own (Ideas reads `listEggs`, newest first), and each egg's tab fills with its own videos/guides/coverage over time.

## Sidebar follows navigation (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben: when you're in Your Agent and press a link into another section (View in Easter Eggs, News, …), the sidebar must close the dropdown you were in and open the dropdown that section belongs to — one open at a time.
- `navSectionForTab(t)` in App.tsx maps every tab to its sidebar dropdown: home/saved/ideas/goals → agent; feed/clips/news/updates/bugs/missions/map/eggs/guides → explore; database → db; clans/challenges/customise/lfg/events/profile → community; premium/legal/signup/owner/settings → "" (no dropdown — sidebar left as-is). `go()` applies it on every navigation (sidebar links, in-page links, bottom nav, Settings-modal links via `goFromSettings`, Database presets via `openDb` → `go("database")`), and `openEgg` sets "explore" (Easter Eggs lives there). Because the accordion state (`openNavSection`, shared by the desktop sidebar and the mobile All-sections drawer) holds exactly one open section, opening the destination's dropdown closes the previous one by construction. Manual header toggles still work exactly as before.

## Ideas redesign — spacious, visual, trending (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben asked for Ideas (Easter egg ideas) to be more spacious, aesthetic and visual so people want to try them, and to follow trending discoveries: visual-first cards, trending badge / trending order.
- Ideas hero now uses the Easter Eggs section illustration (`sec-eggs.webp`, distinct to this page — previously it reused the generic `hero.webp`) at 260–320px with a "Trending now" pill and a 28–34px headline, roomier `p-6 sm:p-8` body, and a primary "Try an idea now" action.
- Every known idea now has its OWN original illustration, generated at build time with `media.generate_image` into `client/src/assets/` (1920×1280, painted style, no game screenshots): `idea-ocean-view.webp` (art-deco Ocean Drive hotel), `idea-flash-fm.webp` (glowing vintage car radio), `idea-valentina.webp` (Leonida Keys causeway + figure on a palm island), `idea-vercetti-estate.webp` (island mansion estate). `ideaArtFor(name)` in App.tsx matches by name; unmatched future community reports fall back to `sec-eggs.webp` — never a reused per-idea picture or a placeholder box.
- Trending: `listEggs` returns newest reports first, so the Ideas list is presented in that trending order ("Newest reports first — that is what is hot right now") and the top 3 cards carry a hot-pink "Trending #1 / #2 / #3" badge over their picture. No popularity counts are invented — the ordering is the real report recency from the database.
- Cards are visual-first and spacious: 220–280px picture with the region as a glass pill overlaid, larger status + "Unverified location" badges, 24–26px bold titles, 15px / 1.75-line-height descriptions, a separate "Where to look" panel (approximate region only — no exact map pins until verified after launch), and a primary "Try it — View in Easter Eggs" button alongside the existing Save toggle. All existing ideas, statuses (Reported / Rumor / Confirmed) and actions are preserved; no schema or server change.

## Rename — Clans → Crews (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben renamed the clan system to crews: every user-visible occurrence of Clan/Clans/clan/clanmates (sidebar, navigation, pages, headings, buttons, copy, Settings, Feed, events incl. the "Crew Event" type, LFG, Rankings, Profile, Customise, Ask Vice knowledge, catalogue item names/descriptions, error messages, action descriptions) now reads Crew/Crews/crew/crewmates — "Crew Leader", "Crew Level", "Crew Awards", "crew wallet", the default crew text channel is now named "crew-feed", and the popular event hashtag chip is #crew. Features, roles, hierarchy and mechanics are unchanged — wording only.
- Migration 0034 rewrites the old wording inside already-stored system-generated content: events.type ('Clan Event'/'Clan War' → 'Crew Event'), materialised clan_channels rows named 'clan-feed', event hashtag tokens 'clan'/'clanmeet', credit transaction reasons, rank-point reasons/verified-by labels, crew activity log fields, the seeded Guides library, and support-request topics. Migration 0035 does the same for the team-seeded example content only (events whose host is 'ViceSunshine Community (example)' — title/description/rules/prize — and Feed example posts under the examples author key). `displayEventType` also maps any legacy 'Clan War'/'Clan Event' value to 'Crew Event' at read time. User-authored content (crew names/mottos, Feed posts, chat messages, event titles/descriptions, bios) is NOT rewritten — those are the authors' own words.
- Deliberately NOT renamed (invisible internal identifiers that stored data depends on): DB tables/columns (`clans`, `clan_members`, `clan_id`, …), action names (`listClans`, `createClan`, …), the cosmetic/wallet scope value `"clan"`, catalogue item ids and values (`card-clan-vice`, `clan-vice`, …), the Rank Point source key `clanpost`, device localStorage shapes (`saved.clan`), React query keys, and the event hashtag search synonyms (`"Crew Event": ["clan", "clanmeet", "crew"]` — old #clan tags still find Crew Events). None of these are ever displayed.

## Rename — ViceVerse → ViceSunshine (Nov 19, 2026)
- Ben renamed the site because "ViceVerse" is taken by multiple other GTA VI Instagram/Facebook accounts; "ViceSunshine" checked free in web search (no GTA hub/brand using it). Every user-visible occurrence of the old name — app title, sidebar/brand, Settings, Premium/Stripe copy, footer, About, Ask Vice system prompt, Discord card, legal pages, catalogue descriptions, seeded system strings — now reads ViceSunshine (migration 0033 also rewrites the old name inside already-stored system-generated rows: automatic post author/body, example event host, verified-by labels, credit reasons, clan activity, news, guides, notifications).
- Deliberately NOT renamed (invisible internal identifiers, renaming would break live data): device localStorage keys (`viceverse-saved-v1`, `viceverse-tracked-goals-v1`, `viceverse-saved-feed-v1`, `viceverse-saved-db-v1`, `viceverse-map-notes-v1`, `viceverse-following-v1`), server authorKeys persisted in existing rows (`viceverse-auto`, `viceverse-examples`, `viceverse-tiktok`), and the runtime-managed cron job ids in space.json (`viceverse-auto-*`). The data-export download file was renamed to `vicesunshine-my-data.json`.
- "Vice Credits", "ViceVerse Level" → "ViceSunshine Level", and the Ask Vice assistant name ("Vice") are unchanged except where the full brand name appeared.

## Context provenance
- `Grand Theft Auto VI launches November 19, 2026 on PlayStation 5 and Xbox Series X|S only` (verbatim brief; confirmed by web search below — shapes the launch countdown, platform filters, and honest single-player labeling)
- `At launch it is a SINGLE-PLAYER experience only. Rockstar has NOT announced GTA VI Online` (verbatim brief; shapes Events/LFG labeling as Community Events, never in-game multiplayer)
- `Build Phase 1 first, with placeholder data for the other phases` (verbatim brief; Phase 1 sections get full depth, Clans/LFG/Events ship functional but lighter)
- `Example: 01.002.000 (Nov 13, ~1.07 GB), 01.002.001 (Nov 16, 602.5 MB PS5 / 1.59 GB Xbox)` (user-supplied example rows in the brief; seeded verbatim into the Updates tracker with notes field set to `No official patch notes published` per the brief's own rule — never invent fixes)
- `Examples to seed: Ocean View Hotel return, Flash FM, Valentina return, Vercetti Estate references` (user-supplied seed list in the brief; seeded as Easter eggs with status Reported/Rumor, never Confirmed, since Rockstar has published no official list)
- `Unofficial Fan Community - Not affiliated with Rockstar Games` (verbatim brief; persistent footer/banner on every view)

## Tested sources
### Web search — GTA VI launch facts
**Used by**: seed news rows, launch countdown, daily `refreshNews` action
**Test command**: `browser.search` query `GTA VI release date November 19 2026 Rockstar Games official`
**Sample output**: Rockstar/Take-Two/PlayStation Store all list Nov 19, 2026 for PS5 and Xbox Series X|S; digital pre-load began Nov 10, 2026; up to 212 GB free space required (Rockstar Nov 3 Pre-Load FAQ); physical edition is code-in-box from Nov 12; no PC date announced; Launch Gallery of ~40 official screenshots published Nov 5, 2026.
**Processing**: Only facts corroborated across official-source reporting become `Official` news rows, each carrying its source URL copied verbatim (e.g. https://gamingroute.com/is-gta-6-delayed/ , https://savepointsix.com/guides/gta6-release-check/). Rumor-category rows are always labeled Rumor. The daily refresh action re-runs `ctx.tool.web_search` for GTA VI news/patch queries, dedupes by source URL, and inserts only new items — it never edits or deletes user/community rows.

## Web-search sources
### GTA VI news, trailers, patch announcements
**Delivered by**: `refreshNews` action (daily cron + manual Refresh button)
**Checked with**: browser.search above returned dated, sourced results with full verbatim URLs; the same queries run server-side via `ctx.tool.web_search` at refresh time.

## Long-term data behavior
- **Refresh policy**: Daily cron (space_action) calls `refreshNews`; it web-searches GTA VI news and patch notes, inserts new Official/Patch items not already present (dedupe on source_url), and stamps `last_refreshed_at`. Community content (bugs, clans, LFG, events, mission progress) is user-owned and never touched by refresh.
- **Growth**: News/patches grow by refresh + admin-style add form; bugs, missions, eggs, guides, clans, members, LFG posts, events, RSVPs, faction points all grow by user actions. Lists paginate/filter client-side over action results.
- **Ordering**: News newest first; patches newest version first; bugs sortable Most Voted / Newest / Platform; missions by story order; events soonest first; clan activity log newest first.
- **Time semantics**: Event date/times store the event's own timezone string alongside the instant and render in that zone; created_at/vote/log instants render viewer-local. Launch countdown targets 2026-11-19T00:00 America/New_York.

## Imagery
- Hero and section art: original neon Vice City–style illustrations generated at build time with `media.generate_image` into `client/src/assets/` (drawn/painted style, never presented as game screenshots). No remote or scraped game imagery is rendered.
- Interactive Leonida map: a stylized fan schematic panel (illustrated backdrop + percentage-positioned pins) for the fictional game world — clearly labeled a fan-made schematic, not real geography and not Rockstar's official map.
- Clan emblems/news thumbnails: color/emblem-letter rendering, no external images.

## Rejected approaches
- **Tried**: Seeding a full invented mission walkthrough list and confirmed patch fixes.
  **Why rejected**: The game launches Nov 19, 2026 (tomorrow); no real mission list or patch fixes exist to source. Missions ship as a spoiler-gated database seeded only with brief-supplied/trailer-known framing and user-addable rows; patches carry `No official patch notes published` until refresh finds real notes.
- **Tried**: Real-world map library for Leonida.
  **Why rejected**: Leonida is fictional; a fan schematic pin-board is the honest, functional equivalent.

## Trust redesign — agent structure (edit, Nov 18 2026, supersedes game-layer presentation)
- Ben said the gamey version felt fake, disliked emojis, and asked for a trustworthy, high-quality environment structured like Muse (Feed / Saved / Ideas / Goals) — ViceSunshine as an agent for GTA VI — plus more genuine visuals, a spacious aesthetic feel, and mobile-first design.
- ALL emojis removed (verified 0 remaining). Replaced with plain text labels, initials/monograms (clan monogram = tag initials on a colour gradient tile; player avatars = initials), and small text badges (PS / XB / DC for linked accounts). No cartoon badges or trophy-emoji emblems.
- Palette unchanged (dark + hot pink / purple / teal) but used sparingly: neutral surfaces, white primary buttons, teal only for active/progress states, pink only for thin visual strips and the clan colour itself. No neon glows, no chunky game buttons — `btn-game`/`trophy-pill`/`xp-*` classes restyled to calm pill/progress styles in theme.css.
- Agent shell: desktop = spacious 260px sidebar (Your agent: Feed / Saved / Ideas / Goals; Explore; Community) + max-760px content column so nothing is squeezed; phone = minimal top bar + "All sections" drawer + fixed bottom nav with 5 large (60px) thumb targets: Feed, Saved, Ideas, Goals, Clans. Single-column, generous padding (p-5/p-6 cards), larger visuals that crop cleanly.
- Home = calm daily briefing ("what matters today"): large hero illustration (existing original `hero.webp`) with countdown, latest patch, goals snapshot and saved count; sourced latest news with date/source + Save.
- Saved: device-local bookmarks (`localStorage` key `viceverse-saved-v1`) — Save/Saved toggle on News, Guides, Bugs, Missions, Easter Eggs and Clan cards; Saved view groups real rows by type with honest empty state. No server schema change, no invented rows.
- Ideas: Easter-egg ideas + map Activities/Collectibles, each with verification status (Confirmed/Reported/Rumor) and Save; honest empty state.
- Goals: mission progress (real `listMissions` done/total), clan context (real top clan + 5-role note), and RSVP'd events (real `myRsvp`) — no synthetic goals.
- Visuals: existing original illustrations reused as large banners only — `hero.webp` (home/saved/ideas), `clan-arena.webp` (clans header, clan home, goals), `faction-war.webp` (faction panels) — taller crops on phone (260–300px heroes, 440px+ map centrepiece), visual strips on news/egg cards. No new fake art, no clip-art.
- Clan system underneath unchanged: 5 roles + promotion rules + activity log + applications + squads + faction war, and Rankings with Global / Local / PlayStation / Xbox boards only inside Rankings. Presentation is now clean cards (monogram, members/wins/events, thin progress bars, points as "pts") in a 2-column max grid.

## Clans game layer (edit, Nov 18 2026)
- User asked for Clans to feel like a game (Clash Royale spirit): visual-first, big emblems/badges, trophies, levels — and confirmed they love the existing neon Vice City palette, which is unchanged.
- Clan game stats are DERIVED server-side from real clan record fields only (factionPoints, wins, eventsHeld, memberCount): trophies = factionPoints + wins×100 + eventsHeld×25; XP = factionPoints + wins×50 + eventsHeld×20 + members×10; level = 1 + XP/200 (cap 15); league by trophies (Ocean Drive → Vice Beach → Neon Strip → Downtown Vice → Leonida Elite → Vice Legend). Nothing new is stored or invented.
- New stored field: `clans.emblem` (migration 0005), one of shield/crown/star/bolt/palm/flame/gem/wave, chosen in a visual emblem picker at clan creation with a live card preview, colour swatches and playstyle/privacy/platform icon tiles.
- New original fan illustrations generated at build time with `media.generate_image` into `client/src/assets/`: `clan-arena.webp` (neon arena with trophy towers) and `faction-war.webp` (Green vs Purple face-off) — drawn style, no official game art.
- The exact 5-role hierarchy, promotion rules, activity log, applications, announcements, feed/chat, squads and faction war are unchanged underneath; only presentation changed (avatar roster tiles with role badges, squad tiles, game tabs, XP/member progress bars).
- Rankings section (same edit, follow-up): a dedicated `clanRankings` action returns four trophy-ranked boards — Global (all clans), Local (clans whose region matches the viewer's profile region), PlayStation (PS5 + Both clans) and Xbox (Xbox + Both clans) — rendered as tabs inside a Rankings section in Clans only. The platform split lives only there: the old PS/Xbox filter chips were removed from clan discovery (playstyle tiles + search remain). Local shows an honest set-your-region state when the viewer has no region on their profile.

## Map not released yet (edit, Nov 18 2026)
- User pointed out the map is not released yet. Rockstar has NOT released the official GTA VI map, so ViceSunshine must not present a map as real, complete or accurate.
- Map page now opens with the exact label: "Map not released yet — fan-made concept schematic only, not official, not to scale." It shows only a simple dashed concept schematic outline (no pins, no detailed coastline, no layer filters, no Add-a-pin), a list of approximate regions known from trailers (Vice City, Leonida Keys, Port Gellhorn, Ambrosia, Mount Kalaga National Park) each badged "Unverified / Reported", and a note that the full interactive map will be built after launch on Nov 19, 2026 as players verify locations.
- Precise pins claiming exact in-game locations for missions, Easter eggs, collectibles, activities or shops are removed from the UI everywhere (Map, Easter Eggs, Ideas). Server-side: `listPins` returns no items pre-launch (legacy seeded pin rows in `map_pins` from migration 0002 are no longer served), `addPin` throws a clear not-available-yet error, and `addEgg` no longer creates a map pin.
- Easter Eggs stays a list view: locations are approximate region text only, each card badged "Unverified location", the report form asks for approximate region (no map-position x/y inputs, new finds start Reported/Rumor only), and there is no "Show on map" action. Ideas likewise lost its "Show on map" / map-pins section and now links to "Map status & known regions".

## Vice Credits, Rank Points, Clan Wars, seasons and Shop (edit, Nov 18 2026)
- Vice Credits: every new profile starts with 500 (migration 0006 adds `profiles.credits` default 500 + a "Welcome" transaction). Creating a clan costs 500 from the creator's personal wallet — blocked server-side with a clear message when the balance is short; the create form shows cost and balance up front.
- Rank Points are counted AUTOMATICALLY from in-app actions only (recordPoints in actions.ts, deduped per player+sourceRef, per-source daily caps): RSVP Going +15, host-confirmed event win +50/+30/+20 (one-tap host confirm in Events), LFG form +10 / join +5, clan feed post +2 (cap 10/day), recruit +15 on join, moderator-confirmed bug +20, moderator-verified egg +25, feed post at 10 real likes +10. Mission ticks are personal progress only (+5 personal points, trust-based). Each point pays 1 Vice Credit to the player's wallet, +2 clan XP boost bonus when a clan benefits. No uploads, no claim forms, no Pending queue (an earlier proof-claim design was built and then fully removed at the owner's direction). Light anti-cheat: dedupe, daily caps, Report button on clan point entries (3 reports remove the entry).
- Honesty rule kept: ViceSunshine cannot verify gameplay inside GTA VI (single-player, no Rockstar API, no PSN/Xbox data) — points come only from activity the app itself records; player level is labelled a ViceSunshine level, never an official GTA VI level.
- Clan Level is lifetime from effective Rank Points (Level 2 at 200, 3 at 500, 4 at 1000, 5 at 2000, widening steps), where effective = raw × member-level multiplier (average member tier: tier = 1 + (level−1)/5 levels, ×1 + 0.25 per tier above 1, cap ×2). Player ViceSunshine level: 1 + floor(sqrt(points/50)).
- Seasons are calendar months (seasonKey YYYY-MM): Rankings (Global/Local/PlayStation/Xbox, split only there) sort by this month's effective points, so a new month starts at 0 automatically; lifetime level and credits never reset. `closeSeason` pays a finished month once: per board (Global/PS/Xbox) 1st 1000 credits + Phoenix emblem ownership, 2nd 600, 3rd 300, ranks 4–10 100, into clan wallets, recorded in `season_winners` and shown as history in Rankings.
- Clan Wars (migration 0006: clan_wars / clan_war_entries / war_activity): weekly, lazy-created per ISO week with a rotating themed challenge (Event/Voices/Team-Up/Growth/Secrets Week; challenge action scores double). Leader/Co-Leader opts the clan in; war points count automatically from members' in-app actions (event 10, LFG 10/5, recruit 15, egg report 5, mission 3, post 2). `closeClanWar` after the week ends pays winner +100 Rank Points / 300 clan credits, 2nd +50/150, others +10/25.
- Joining a clan pays the joiner a +50 Vice Credits welcome bonus once per clan, and Clans/Clan Home show a Clan Perks panel (bonus, chat/feed, events & wars, squads, XP boost). Members can contribute personal credits to their clan wallet (`contributeToClan`, logged both sides).
- Shop (Shop tab, migration 0006 owned_items + clan wallet/banner/name-colour columns): two tabs — Your Profile (personal wallet) and Clan (clan wallet, Leader/Co-Leader only). 31 items: clan emblems 250–500, colours 150, banners 300, name colours 400; personal frames 0–300, banner colours 150, name colours 200, badges 200, theme accents 250, Premium badges 450–500. Fairness: ~80% Free/cheap, ~20% Premium (7/31), every item labelled Free/Earned/Premium and 100% earnable with Vice Credits — no pay-only items. Equipping applies immediately (clan emblem/colour/banner/name colour; personal badge/banner colour/name colour/frame/accent, rendered in Feed, clan roster, LFG and directory via author/member cosmetics fields).
- Buy Credits packs (500/$2.99, 1,200/$4.99, 3,000/$9.99, 7,500/$19.99) are displayed with a clear "Payments are not connected yet — checkout coming soon" state; `purchaseCredits` never takes payment, never asks for card details, and never grants credits. Credits (earned, bought, Premium) cannot buy Rank Points, Clan Level, seasonal rank or wins.
- Premium weekly credits (migration 0007: profiles.premium_since / premium_weeks_granted): Premium is $4.99/month on the Premium page with "250 Vice Credits every week" as headline benefit. `applyPremiumWeeklyCredits` runs lazily on wallet/profile reads for premium=1 profiles: anchored to the current Premium period's start, each due week pays one 250 transaction labelled "Premium weekly credits" (1,000/month); next payout date is returned by getWallet/getProfile and shown on the Premium page and Profile wallet. Lapse clears the anchor — no retroactive pay for lapsed weeks; renewal starts a fresh anchor. (Premium activation itself remains payment-gated as before — no in-app subscribe action exists.)

Patch wording "No official patch notes published" is preserved — no notes invented.

## Feed video, clips & memes (edit, Nov 19 2026)

No YouTube thumbnails are hotlinked — video cards use a tap-to-play facade, autoplay off.
- Uploads: `uploadFeedMedia` stores user files via `ctx.blobs` (images ≤4 MB; MP4/WebM ≤12 MB; the composer checks clip length ≤60s before upload). Rules shown in the composer: only your own clips or links to the original creator with credit; no leaked footage, no full copyrighted streams/movies, no fake downloads; every post keeps its Report button. Feed posts can be saved on-device (`viceverse-saved-feed-v1`).
- `seedExamples` (post-submit, idempotent) seeds one clearly-labelled community example per Feed category (author "ViceSunshine Community", every body states it is a community example, not official Rockstar content; no invented video URLs), three example community events, and the Database catalog below.

## Timeline, News priority & release-times correction (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Unlock Times corrected after audit: the section no longer derives or displays per-city clock times from an assumed midnight-Eastern anchor. It now shows only the launch date — Thursday, November 19, 2026 — the viewer's timezone name, and the official Rockstar verify link, stating that Rockstar and the platform stores set the exact unlock hour and ViceSunshine does not guess it.
- GTA VI Timeline (Home + News): official milestones only, with dates/titles as announced (Nov 8 2023 Announcement 1; Dec 1 2023 Trailer Announcement 2; Dec 4 2023 Trailer 1 debut + Release Announcement 1 "Fall 2025"; May 2 2025 Release Announcement 2 "May 26, 2026"; May 6 2025 Trailer 2; Nov 10 2026 pre-load opens — from the sourced reporting already in DATA-PLAN; Nov 19 2026 Release). Every entry links to the official GTA VI page (URL supplied verbatim in earlier briefs). No rumoured dates presented as official.
- Market Information was requested and then explicitly removed by the owner before shipping: the temporary `getMarketData` server action (finance channel test) was deleted and no stock UI was ever shipped. No TTWO/SONY/MSFT cards, prices or charts exist in the artifact.
- News page prioritised (owner's "really important"): header + own-words description, page search ("Search news, videos, investors…"), source-type filters (All News / Newswire / Videos / Twitter / X / Earnings Calls) derived from each real item's source URL/name/category, a Featured story card (newest Official item, original ViceSunshine art, Explore Story → official post), and a card grid. EVERY news item carries a "View official post" link (its own source URL, falling back to the official GTA VI page) labelled "External link — Official post", a Save button, and a "Track as a Goal" button.
- Track-as-a-Goal: device-local tracked goals (`viceverse-tracked-goals-v1`, same pattern as Saved) for news stories and events; Goals shows a "Tracked news & events" section with done tick, reminder date, official-post link and remove. Events also gained Save (Saved store now includes an "event" kind, rendered in Saved) plus the same official-post link and Track button on every event card. Only titles/summaries are shown — never copied article text, images or video.
