## Shop redesign, Database likeness + original-picture link, Vice AI helper (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Database illustrations (owner's confirmed "safe option"): every entry's picture is an ORIGINAL ViceSunshine illustration drawn to look almost the same as the real item (same shape/colour/look — per-name specs sharpened with signature details: Mustang-shaped Dominator with tri-bar tail lights, Charger-shaped Dukes, Jason's and Lucia's trailer looks, AK-pattern Kozlov Hind, G36-pattern VF-G1), generated server-side via `seedDbArt`/`seedArtwork` into owned blobs. No copied screenshots/stills and no hotlinked photos anywhere. Each detail page also has a "See original picture" link (entry's source URL, falling back to the official GTA VI page) labelled "External link — Official source" — the original photo is only ever viewed on the official site.
- Artwork completion is automated: cron `leonida-hub-artwork-generation` (space_action, every 15 min) runs `seedArtwork`, which generates up to 3 missing Database illustrations plus any missing Shop avatar illustrations per run until everything has art (then it is a no-op). Community-suggested entries get artwork the same way via the generic per-kind spec fallback.
- Shop redesign (owner: "looks dead, too text-heavy"): visual-first card grid — every item is a large card with a big animated visual stage (h-44), tier badge and price pill, minimal text. Personal tab opens with a live "This is how you'll look" preview (banner colour, animated avatar, frame, name colour, badge) that updates when you tap an avatar to preview it. Clan tab opens with a "Your clan's look" preview (emblem + equipped effect animating).
- Avatars (migration 0013: profiles.avatar_id): 8 original ViceSunshine characters (Street, Crew free; Driver, Surfer 200; Racer, Mechanic 250; Neon DJ 450 Premium; Detective 500 Premium), art generated by `seedAvatarArt` into owned blobs (meta `avatar-art:<id>`). The equipped avatar renders animated (bob + pulsing glow ring, reduced-motion safe) via CosmeticAvatar in the Feed, clan roster, LFG, player directory and profile. Same fairness rule: 10 of 46 items Premium (~22%), 100% earnable, no pay-to-win.
- Emblem effects (migration 0013: clans.emblem_effect): Neon 300, Lightning / Fire / Ice 350, Poison 400, Shadow 450, Gold 600 Premium, plus free "No effect". Effects are pure CSS animations (flicker, shimmer, bubbles, shine sweep; reduced-motion safe) rendered by ClanEmblem's overlay on clan cards, clan home, war entries and Rankings.
- Vice — AI helper + support (migration 0014: support_requests): floating "Ask Vice" button (desktop bottom-right; phone via a bottom-nav button) opening a chat panel labelled as an AI helper, not a person and not Rockstar support. `askVice` grounds answers in ViceSunshine's own rows (news/guides/db/missions/events/latest patch matched by keyword) plus the app's exact rules for credits, Clan Wars, seasons, Shop and Premium, and the signed-in player's own wallet/clan facts for support questions; answers return page links (validated tab ids) and the official Rockstar link where relevant. Hard rules in the prompt: never invent GTA VI facts, label Rumor/Unverified, single-player at launch / no GTA Online announced, never promise refunds/fixes, never ask for passwords/card details/codes. When a problem needs a human it sets needsHuman and the panel opens a support form (topic, details, username) → `createSupportRequest` (which also rejects details containing passwords/card data). Quick chips include the five help questions plus support chips ("I lost my credits", "My clan won't create", "How do I report someone?", "Premium isn't working", "Contact support"). If inference is unavailable, askVice falls back to a grounded summary of matched content with links.

## Voice chat — community voice channels (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Discord used as a layout guide only (server-style channels, live occupants, bottom call bar, roles/permissions) — no Discord logo, branding, artwork or icons copied; all design is original ViceSunshine neon pink / purple / teal.
- Model (migration 0015): `voice_participants` (presence rows with 3s heartbeats, pruned after 25s stale; scope clan/event/lfg + channel key + mute/deafen/speaking/force-muted state), `voice_signals` (WebRTC offer/answer/ICE mailbox, deleted on poll, 2-min TTL), `voice_reports`, and `lfg_members` (joinLfg/createLfg now record team membership — previously only a spot counter — so team voice can enforce "that team only").
- Channels: every clan gets General / Clan War / Car Meet / LFG voice channels beside its text channels (Clan Home → Voice tab, server layout: text channels on top, voice channels below, call area right); each community event gets an Event Lobby channel; each LFG post gets a Team Voice channel.
- Access enforced server-side in `voiceAccess`: clan voice = clan members only; event voice = RSVP Going only; LFG voice = poster + recorded team members. Non-members see live counts only, never occupant names. Moderation: clan Leader / Co-Leader, event host, or LFG poster can server-mute (force_muted, member cannot unmute) or remove; anyone in a call can report a user (`voice_reports`).
- Audio is peer-to-peer WebRTC (mesh, deterministic initiator per pair, signals relayed via the actions above); the server never receives or stores audio — calls are not recorded, stated in the UI. Microphone (`getUserMedia`) is requested only on the Join Voice Call tap; denial joins listen-only/muted with a clear notice. Speaking indicator comes from each client's own mic level, reported through the heartbeat; mute/deafen state syncs the same way. Deafen also silences remote audio locally.
- "In a call" badge: live presence is joined into clan roster members, event attendees, the player directory and the profile header (`inVoice` fields).
- Fallback: event cards show the host's external Discord / PS Party link (the existing Going-only `lobby_link`) inside the voice panel, labelled as a fallback — the main call stays in ViceSunshine.
- Honesty labelling everywhere the panel renders: "ViceSunshine community voice chat — not official Rockstar / GTA VI in-game voice chat. Calls are never recorded." Ask Vice's grounded rules gained a matching voice-chat fact.

## Automatic Feed media, Database art v2, per-section pictures, sidebar Database (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Automatic Feed media (migration 0016: `feed_posts.auto`, `source_name`, `source_url`): the `refreshFeedMedia` action runs nine `ctx.tool.web_search` queries (one per Feed category: trailer/News, news, patch/Clip, Streamer Clip, Tutorial, Gameplay, Car Meet, Meme, Funny) and stores only results whose URL is a real YouTube watch/shorts/embed link (video id extracted, deduped by id and title, leak/download bait titles skipped, max 3 per query / 18 per run). Posts are authored "ViceSunshine Automatic", embed the original via the existing YouTube facade (never re-uploaded), and carry the verbatim source URL as both media and source link. Search-result shape note: the server search tool nests results under `content.results` with the text in `snippet` — both `refreshFeedMedia` and `refreshNews` parse that shape (the older top-level `results` parse silently returned nothing; fixed in this edit). Found text is cleaned (crawler "Last Updated / Last Crawl" boilerplate stripped; emoji stripped from auto-post titles per the owner's no-emoji rule). Client: Feed source filter All / Automatic / Community, sectioned rendering under "Automatic — official & trending" + "From the community", Auto badge + source line + Watch original link, Track as a Goal works on auto posts (tracked kind "feed"). Daily schedule: cron `leonida-hub-daily-feed-media` (space_action, daily) runs `refreshFeedMedia`; a manual "Refresh automatic posts" button in the Feed does the same on demand.
- Database artwork v2: `generateDbArtFor` now pins the framing Ben approved for Weapons onto Vehicles and Characters — Vehicles: side-profile studio portrait, whole car centred and fully in frame, plain dark studio backdrop with neon rim light, no street scene/cityscape/other cars/people; Characters: head-and-shoulders portrait, face centred and fully in frame, plain backdrop, no skyline. `seedArtwork` batch raised 3 → 6 per run and prioritises Vehicles, then Characters, then Weapons; a one-time v2 pass (meta `db_art_v2_ids`) regenerates Vehicle/Character art that was painted under the old street-scene framing. The artwork cron (every 15 min) therefore finishes all 40 entries and the v2 repaints over the following hours.
- Interim Database tile: entries whose own illustration is not painted yet no longer show the shared per-kind generic image or a "?" box — list rows, detail panel, Recently Updated and the Home strip render a per-entry monogram tile (initials on a hue derived from the entry name; detail version explains the illustration is being painted and will appear automatically).
- Status badge truncation fixed: Database list rows now wrap (`flex-wrap`), and `DbStatusBadge` caps at `min(230px, 100%)` with wrapping text, so "CONFIRMED IN TRAILER" / "RETURNING FROM GTA V — NOT CONFIRMED FOR GTA VI" are never clipped by the screen edge or the Ask Vice button.
- Per-section pictures: 14 new original build-time illustrations (`client/src/assets/sec-*.webp`, `db-*.webp`, generated with the media tool, painted style, neon palette, no text/logos) — one per main section (Feed, News, Updates, Bugs, Missions, Map, Easter Eggs, Guides, Events, Shop, Profile) rendered by a shared `SectionBanner`, plus one per Database tab (Vehicles = muscle-car studio portrait, Weapons = rifle studio portrait, Characters = duo portrait) shown above the Database tab list. No image is reused across sections or tabs; Clans keeps `clan-arena.webp`, Home keeps `hero.webp`.
- Sidebar Database section: an expandable "GTA VI Database" nav in the desktop sidebar (and the mobile All-sections drawer) deep-links into the Database with kind + status presets — Vehicles / Weapons / Characters / All entries / Confirmed / Reported-Rumor / Unverified / Returning from GTA V. The Database gained matching status filter chips (All statuses / Confirmed / Reported-Rumor / Unverified / Returning from GTA V). Badges are generated from each entry's stored `status` by `DbStatusBadge` everywhere (list, detail, Recently Updated, Home strip), so a status change in the data updates every badge and the filter together; no entry is hidden by default (All statuses is the default view).

## Feed on top, Reposts + Quote Reposts, notifications, Voice Calls hub, Feed redesign (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Feed first everywhere: the home page's first big section is now "Community Feed — clips & videos" (above Featured and Official Rockstar news); the sidebar Explore list starts with Community Feed above News; the phone bottom nav is Feed, Calls, Today, Saved, Goals, Clans.
- Inline playback is the default: YouTube posts play inside the Feed card via the official youtube-nocookie embed (tap to play, full-screen allowed, creator credited, "Watch on YouTube" only a small secondary link); uploaded MP4/WebM clips play in a native inline player. Nothing routes users out to YouTube to watch.
- Reposts (migration 0017: `feed_posts.repost_of_id`, `feed_reposts`): EVERY Feed post (user, automatic, text, clip) can be reposted via `repostFeedPost` — one repost per player per original, never your own post, flagged posts blocked; reposts of reposts resolve to and credit the ROOT original. Simple reposts link the original media (never re-upload); counts are always counted against the root, and `listFeed` sort "Top" (the Feed's default; "Latest" is the alternative) ranks by reposts, then likes, then newest.
- Quote Reposts: `repostFeedPost` accepts an optional comment (≤500 chars, leak/download bait rejected). A quote stores the comment as the body with no copied media; the card renders the comment on top and the original boxed below it (author, kind, title, its own inline media, body excerpt, "Original post" source link) via the extended `repostOf` payload in `listFeed`.
- Notifications (migration 0018: `notifications`): reposting or quote-reposting a real member's post notifies the original poster (`listNotifications` / `markNotificationsRead`); the Feed shows a Notifications card with unread count and mark-all-read. Automatic/example posts have no inbox.
- Share from outside: `shareExternalPost` takes a pasted link (YouTube / X / Rockstar Newswire / any https page; download/leak patterns rejected), stores it as a Feed post with `source_name` derived from the host (YouTube / X / Rockstar Newswire / hostname) and `source_url` set; the card labels it "Shared from [source]", credits the original creator, plays YouTube inline, and links to the original. Nothing is downloaded or re-uploaded.
- Voice Calls hub (new "calls" tab): sidebar Community nav and the phone bottom nav both have a Voice Calls / Calls entry. `listMyVoiceScopes` returns the player's clans, Going-RSVP events and LFG teams; the hub renders a full VoicePanel per scope (live occupants + Join). Clan Voice tab keeps the Discord-style rail — "Text channels" on top, "Voice channels" below with a speaker icon and occupants listed under each channel name.
- Feed redesign (Muse's own Feed as the guide, per the owner's screenshot): FeedPostCard is now a flat, spacious article — avatar left, bold title with time-ago ("50m ago" via the existing timeAgo helper) on the right, author/kind/chips meta line, body text with URLs linkified in teal, large full-width media (rounded-2xl) below the text, then a minimal primary action row (heart Like, Discuss, Repost) with Share / Save / Track / Report / Hide / Delete as one quiet secondary row. No emojis in the card UI (heart/comment/repost are inline SVG). Posts are separated by hairlines with generous padding instead of boxed cards; the same layout applies to user, automatic, shared, repost and quote-repost posts. Posts carry a single media item (the data model has one media URL per post), so the multi-thumbnail row from the reference only appears when a post actually has media — no fabricated galleries.

## Party Calls, clan channel structure, Feed player fix, ads prep (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Party Calls (owner's simplification, supersedes any separate 1-on-1 design): ONE call type for everyone outside clan/event/LFG channels. Migration 0019: `party_calls` (name, host, invite_code, max_members ≤ 20, visibility public/private, ended) + `party_members`. Anyone can create one from the Calls page; the creator is host and can mute / deafen / remove people, make it Public or Private, and end it for everyone (`endPartyCall` removes all participants and blocks rejoining). Join by invite code (`joinPartyCall`, code or public-party id) or by invite link (`#party=CODE` fragment, parsed on the Calls page) — clan membership never required. Public parties are listed on the Calls page with live counts (e.g. 3 / 20) via `listMyVoiceScopes.parties/publicParties`. A 2-person Party Call IS the 1-on-1: the Call button on player profiles (directory), clan roster cards and LFG posts calls `callPlayer`, which creates a private Party Call with that player, adds them, and notifies them with the code. There is no direct-call table or scope (an early draft was removed before ever building).
- Clan channel structure (migration 0019: `clan_channels` + `clans.elders_manage_channels` + `voice_participants.force_deafened`): each clan's text/voice channels are rows; the previous fixed channels (announcements / clan-feed / chat + General / Clan War / Car Meet / LFG) are materialised lazily as defaults (`clanChannelsOf`) so existing clans keep working and every channel becomes manageable. Leader / Co-Leader manage channels (`createClanChannel` / `updateClanChannel` / `deleteClanChannel` / `reorderClanChannels`); Elders only when the Leader flips `setClanChannelSettings` on. Channels are Public or Private: private channels are filtered OUT of `getVoice`/`getClan` responses for members not on the allow-list (roles and/or specific members; Leader/Co-Leader always allowed), render with a lock icon, and `joinVoice`/`clanPost` re-check visibility server-side. Voice channels carry a member limit enforced live in `joinVoice` (Party Calls enforce their ≤20 cap the same way). Moderation gained server-deafen (`moderateVoice` deafen/undeafen + `force_deafened`, synced via heartbeat — a mod-deafened member's client silences remote audio and cannot un-deafen). `clan_posts.channel` is now a free channel key (was a 3-value enum); `getClan` returns visible `channels` + per-channel `channelPosts`, and the clan Chat tab is a dynamic channel switcher.
- Feed video fix (owner reported still seeing YouTube links): the player is now a real thumbnail facade — the video's own YouTube poster (`i.ytimg.com/vi/<id>/hqdefault.jpg`, poster only, never a copied video) with a big play button; tapping swaps in the youtube-nocookie iframe (autoplay, full controls, full-screen) inside the card on the Feed page, the home Feed section and the home videos carousel (`YoutubePlayer`, shared). Server: `feedMediaOf` returns `videoId`; `listFeed` strips any YouTube URL (and the post's own media URL) out of the displayed body for video posts, including quote-repost originals, so a raw watch link can never render as text; `createFeedPost` extracts a pasted YouTube URL from the body into the player automatically. Client belt-and-braces: `FeedMediaBlock` turns any YouTube media URL into the player even if a stored row's type says "link". The ONLY remaining YouTube link is one tiny "Watch on YouTube" text link under the player on every video post type (user / automatic / repost / quote / shared) — the prominent "Watch original ↗" auto-post link was removed. If a creator disabled embedding, YouTube's own in-player message shows inside the frame; the post is never degraded to a plain link.
- Ads preparation (no real ads): `ADSENSE_PUBLISHER_ID = ""` in App.tsx with the full policy in code comments — while empty, `AdSlot` renders only a small dashed "Ad space — reserved" placeholder, loads no external script, and renders NOTHING for Premium members (ad-free is a Premium benefit, already promised on the Premium page). Slots: home top banner, Feed top banner, in-feed after every 4 posts, desktop sidebar — each marked in code as an ad slot with its future AdSense unit name (`data-ad-slot`). Permanent exclusions (also in Terms + About): no crypto, gambling, or account-selling ads, ever. No AdSense connection, no ID requested anywhere in the app.
- Legal pages (new "legal" tab, footer links everywhere): Privacy Policy (what's stored; voice presence only, audio peer-to-peer and never recorded; on-device storage; youtube-nocookie embeds; ads/cookies posture), Terms (unofficial fan community, community rules, accuracy labels, credits/Premium honesty, ads policy), About & Contact (what ViceSunshine is, contact via Ask Vice → human support request, ads & affiliate disclosure, not-affiliated disclosure). The unofficial-fan-community line also remains in the footer and sidebar.

## Sidebar dedupe + mission / Rewards & Levels (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Sidebar: removed the plain "Database" entry from `EXPLORE_NAV` — the sidebar (and mobile All sections) now has ONE Database section, the expandable "GTA VI Database" with Vehicles / Weapons / Characters + status filters inside it.
- Mission (owner's wording in substance): "Play GTA VI. Level up in ViceSunshine. Earn rewards here." — now the lead of the About page ("Our mission") and the page footer line.
- Rewards & Levels (About page): Rank Points from in-app activity only (missions ticked in the app, event RSVPs/attendance, Feed/clan posts, recruiting, LFG teams, Clan War points) raise the ViceSunshine level; milestones pay Vice Credits once, automatically: L5 +100, L10 +250, L15 +400, L20 +600, L25 +800, L30 +1,000, L40 +1,500, L50 +2,500 (`LEVEL_MILESTONES` + `applyLevelMilestoneCredits` in actions.ts, paid lazily on getWallet/getProfile, idempotent via the transaction log; `getWallet` returns `levelMilestones` and the About table renders from it, with the player's own level/points when they have a profile). Copy states plainly: the ViceSunshine level is a community level, NOT an in-game GTA VI level; rewards are never based on a self-reported in-game level and no user-facing copy mentions an API (two older honesty strings that said "no Rockstar API" were reworded to "ViceSunshine can't see your game").

## Feed black-screen fix (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner report: clips stuck on a black screen. Cause: tapping play swapped the thumbnail facade for a bare `youtube-nocookie` iframe on a black background — no loading state, no failure state, no way back — and some views (in-chat preview / app webviews) block third-party iframes entirely, while the nocookie host stalls in webviews waiting on a cookie-consent flow. In those views the video was a permanent black box.
- Fix, following the social-embeds posture (embed = enhancement layered on a link-out card, never the only content): server `feedMediaOf` now returns the standard `https://www.youtube.com/embed/<id>` endpoint; `YoutubePlayer` shows a "Loading video…" state, detects a frame that never loads (6s), retries on the alternate host (youtube.com ⇄ youtube-nocookie), then shows a plain "isn't loading here" panel with Try again + Watch on YouTube. While playing, a control row under the player always offers Stop video (back to thumbnail) and Watch on YouTube, plus a black-screen hint. Iframe carries the standard sandbox token set + `playsinline`. Uploaded MP4/WebM clips got the same treatment via `NativeClip` (error → "Open the clip" link instead of dead black controls). Privacy copy updated to match (no longer claims nocookie-only).

## Map tools upgrade (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Map page rebuilt with the tool layout of big community maps (inspired by map.stateofleonida.net's tools only — no map image, pin data, clusters or counts copied): "ViceSunshine Map" header, left toolbar (Pins, Art, Find/search, zoom +/−, 3D tilt, Ruler/measure, Note, Undo, Reset, Clear), right Setup (grid/labels/art/pins toggles) and People (known-regions) panels, bottom-left live coordinates readout (X: 0.000, Y: 0.000 in schematic units), drag-pan + wheel/button zoom, and clustered pins.
- The map underneath is unchanged in honesty: ViceSunshine's own fan-made concept schematic (dashed outline, radial landmass shading, optional grid), still labelled "Map not released yet — fan-made concept schematic only, not official, not to scale". Pins are ONLY the five approximate trailer-known regions (Vice City, Leonida Keys, Port Gellhorn, Ambrosia, Mount Kalaga National Park) at schematic positions, each Unverified / Reported; clusters are computed from those five pins in schematic space (radius shrinks with zoom, so bubbles split into pins) — counts are 2–3, never copied from any other site.
- Measure shows distance in "schematic units — not to scale". Notes are the player's own, stored on-device only (`viceverse-map-notes-v1`), labelled "not a verified location"; Undo removes the last note/measure point, Clear wipes notes after a confirm tap, Reset restores the view. No server pin-adding exists pre-launch (addPin still blocked server-side); player-verified pins open after launch on Nov 19, 2026.

## Stripe-ready payments (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Prices are CAD: Premium CA$4.99/month recurring; credit packs CA$2.99 / 4.99 / 9.99 / 19.99 for 500 / 1,200 / 3,000 / 7,500 credits (`CREDIT_PACKS.priceCad`). No USD conversions are printed — the provider shows the live-rate total at checkout.
- `subscribePremium` and `purchaseCredits` both return `payments_not_connected` and never take money, never activate Premium, never grant credits. Credits are granted only after a successful payment, which cannot happen until the owner connects Stripe (card / Apple Pay / Google Pay) on his own account. Payouts will go to the owner's Canadian bank (Scotia / Wealthsimple Business Chequing per Ben's memory note) — Stripe-side setup; the app never collects bank details.
- Cancellation: `cancelPremium` sets `profiles.premium_ends_at` (migration 0020) to the end of the current paid month (monthly anniversaries of `premium_since`). Premium and weekly credits stay active until then; `applyPremiumWeeklyCredits` lapses the membership lazily on the first read after that date and never grants retroactively. `resumePremium` undoes a cancellation. `getWallet` exposes premiumSince / premiumEndsAt / premiumNextBilling / premiumPriceCad / paymentsConnected=false / paymentHistory (honestly empty until Stripe exists; weekly grants already show in wallet transactions).
- Refunds: Ask Vice's rules forbid promising a refund; an "I need a refund" support chip routes members to a human-reviewed support request, and the Premium page + Terms state the same.

## Two separate reward systems — labelled, never mixed (Nov 19, 2026)
- **Clan Awards — earned, never for sale** (server `AWARD_ITEMS` / `CLAN_AWARDS` / `applyClanAwards` in actions.ts): exclusive clan cosmetics granted to the WHOLE clan at milestones — 10 members → Founders banner style; Clan Level 5 → Storm Champion (Lightning) emblem effect; Clan Level 10 → Champion Gold emblem effect; season #1 on any board → Phoenix emblem. Award items are NOT in `SHOP_ITEMS`: `buyItem` rejects them explicitly, they cannot be bought with Vice Credits or real money or owned individually. `equipItem` resolves them via `itemDef` (shop ∪ awards) so a Leader / Co-Leader can equip an earned award from the new "Clan Awards — earned, never for sale" section on the clan page (getClan returns `awards[]` with earned/equipped state; grants are lazy + idempotent via owned_items, logged in clan activity). The Phoenix emblem was REMOVED from the Shop (was 400 credits, tier premium) and from createClan's starting-emblem enum to keep it award-only; existing owners keep their owned_items rows.
- **Event / War Rewards — Vice Credits for the winning clan** (separate system): Clan Wars already paid 300 / 150 / 25 participation to clan wallets. NEW: `hostConfirmEvent` now also pays event credits once per event (guarded by `[event:<id>:…]` tags in credit_transactions reasons): winner's clan 150, 2nd 75, 3rd 50, +10 participation to every other clan with a Going RSVP; response gained `clanCreditsPaid`. Season finishes keep paying credits per board (1000/600/300/100) alongside the Phoenix Clan Award.
- Labelling: Rank season box, About page and Shop fairness note all carry the two exact headings — "Clan Awards — earned, never for sale" vs "Event / War Rewards — Vice Credits for the winning clan" — and Ask Vice has matching knowledge lines.

## Market prices (Nov 19, 2026, Ben approved)
- Premium stays CA$4.99/month (`PREMIUM_PRICE_CAD`, unchanged).
- Credit packs replaced in the single source `CREDIT_PACKS` (server actions.ts; Shop Buy Credits grid, purchaseCredits validation and Ask Vice all read from it): 100 / CA$0.99 (`pack-100`), 500 / CA$2.99 (`pack-500`), 1,000 / CA$4.99 (`pack-1000`), 2,500 / CA$9.99 (`pack-2500`), 5,000 / CA$19.99 (`pack-5000`), 12,000 / CA$49.99 (`pack-12000`). Old packs pack-1200 / pack-3000 / pack-7500 removed — purchaseCredits now rejects them as non-existent. Bigger packs give more credits per dollar, per market convention.

## Structure rule (Nov 19, 2026 — dedupe + autoplay + settings build)
- One home per content type: Feed posts render only in FeedView, clips only in ClipsView, news only in NewsView, database entries only in DatabaseView, patches only in UpdatesView. Home is a gateway (hero, countdown, release times, counts, links) and never re-lists section content; the old Home carousels/strips that duplicated Feed/News/Database content were deleted, components included, so they cannot quietly come back.
- Videos autoplay muted in view (YouTube embed with autoplay=1&mute=1 + IFrame API play/pause on IntersectionObserver; native clips the same). The thumbnail stays until the player confirms it is alive; a blocked embed shows a thumbnail panel with Watch on YouTube — never a black box. No `sandbox` attribute on the YouTube iframe (it stalls black in app webviews).
- Party Call QR codes are generated in-app (client/src/qr.ts, ISO QR, ECC M) encoding the same `#party=CODE` fragment as invite links; verified 38/38 decodes with ZXing. Join accepts code, pasted link, or QR text — one path.
- Settings is one page (getSettings/saveSettings) covering profile, contact email (double-entry confirm; there is NO password in ViceSunshine — identity is the device player key), linked accounts + visibility, privacy (private profile, clan visibility, blocked users), notifications, locale (language/currency/timezone/region; currency auto-detects from timezone, CAD default for Canada, prices shown approx-converted but charged in CAD), appearance accent, Premium summary, and Refer a Friend (VV-XXXXXX codes; both sides bank a 10% discount, one use per player, never stacks; applied via saveProfile referralCode on signup, #ref= arrival, or applyReferral in Settings).
- Legal pages live in the single LegalView with nine sections (privacy, terms, cookies, refunds, guidelines, copyright, contact, ads, about) — one policy page each, linked from the footer and Settings, disclaimer on every section.

## Settings panel redesign + saved payment methods (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Settings rebuilt to match the Muse Settings screenshot Ben shared (layout/aesthetic only — no Muse branding or text copied): one centred `.settings-panel` with a left navigation list (General, Connectors, Wallet, Secure store, Permissions, Messaging channels, Devices, Data controls, Help & support, Legal info, Log out pinned at the bottom of the nav column) and a right detail panel using soft grey `.set-row` cards on near-black, teal as the accent. On phone the nav wraps above the detail panel — no horizontal scroll, no squeezed columns.
- All pre-existing Settings capability moved, none dropped: General holds the account row, Usage (Free/Premium plan + a progress bar of real ViceSunshine Level progress from getWallet: vicePoints between (level−1)²·50 and level²·50), Redeem invite code (the existing applyReferral), Refer a Friend (code, copy link, counts, banked 10%), profile fields, contact email with double-entry confirm, Language, currency (auto-detect unchanged) and timezone, and Appearance (Mode row: Dark by design; functional accent swatches). Connectors holds PSN/Xbox/Discord IDs + Connected chips + the link-confirmation card. Permissions holds private-profile + all visibility toggles + blocked users. Messaging channels holds the two notification toggles plus where notifications land. Devices honestly shows only THIS device (device-key sign-in, masked key, detected timezone/currency) and states ViceSunshine does not track other devices. Data controls: real JSON data export (profile, wallet history, blocked list, masked saved cards), clear on-device data (Saved/tracked/map-notes localStorage keys), deletion via human support request. Help & support routes to Ask Vice / support / refunds honestly. Legal info opens the existing nine LegalView sections. Log out removes the device player key from localStorage and reloads (profile/data stay server-side; the copy says this browser starts fresh).
- Saved payment methods (migration 0022, `payment_methods` table): brand (enum Visa/Mastercard/Amex/Discover/Interac/Other card), last4 (schema regex ^\d{4}$ — the column and the request can physically hold only 4 digits), exp month/year, label, is_default. Actions: listPaymentMethods / addPaymentMethod (profile required, expiry must be future, max 5, no duplicates, first becomes default) / removePaymentMethod (promotes oldest remaining to default) / setDefaultPaymentMethod. NO full card number, CVC or bank detail is ever requested, sent or stored — the add form has no field that could hold one, and the copy says Stripe's secure form collects the card when Live opens and hands back only brand/last4/expiry. Stripe remains test-mode only (owner has not activated Live or added payout bank info), so listPaymentMethods returns paymentsConnected=false and every purchase still returns payments_not_connected with no charge and no credits.
- One-tap wiring: purchaseCredits and subscribePremium accept an optional paymentMethodId (validated as belonging to the caller); when given, the not-connected message names the saved card ("Visa •••• 4242 … one tap — no re-entering your card"). Settings > Wallet shows one-tap Subscribe + all six CREDIT_PACKS with "Buy with •••• last4" buttons using the default method; Premium page subscribe button and Shop Buy Credits buttons do the same when a default card exists, and link to Settings > Wallet to manage cards. Wallet also shows balances, payment history (honestly empty until Stripe Live) and a note that payout bank details live in the owner's Stripe Dashboard, never in the app.
- Secure store (Settings section): lists exactly what ViceSunshine keeps — saved cards (masked), contact email, linked gaming IDs, masked device key, referral code — with manage links, the protection rules (Stripe holds card/bank details; ViceSunshine never asks for passwords/full card numbers/codes), and a confirmed "Remove all saved cards" action.
- Privacy + Cookie policies gained a "Saved payment methods" paragraph matching the storage above.

## Profile — Discord-style structure (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Profile page rebuilt to the structure of Ben's Discord profile screenshot (layout only — no Discord logo, branding, artwork or text copied; original ViceSunshine neon pink / purple / teal design): banner strip (player's banner colour, or the Profile section illustration), large avatar (96px) overlapping the banner with a status dot (teal = in a voice call now, green = online in ViceSunshine, from real voice presence), a bio/playstyle bubble beside the avatar on wider screens, display name (Settings displayName, falling back to gamertag) with username (gamertag) below, and a badges row under the name (Level pill, equipped Shop badge, Premium pill, platform mark PS/XB, clan tag) — all from real profile data, text pills only, no emojis.
- Big full-width Edit Profile button (accent) toggles the existing edit form (gamertag / platform / region / playstyle / linked accounts + visibility) — the form is hidden until tapped when a profile exists, and shown by default for profile creation. No capability was dropped: wallet history, points, Player Card copy, Contribute-to-clan and the player directory all still exist (see tabs below).
- Tabs are exactly Main / Activity / Wishlist (underline tab bar, teal active). Main holds "all my related stuff" as clean Discord-style cards: Vice Credits Balance (real wallet balance + Buy Credits → Shop; the Orbs Balance analogue, with the Try/Buy role played by Buy Credits), Member Since (the profile row's real created_at date, with a platform mark), Friends (clanmates only — derived from real clan_members rows for the player's clan, overlapping avatar row of the first 5, tap expands the full list with role/platform, in-call dot and a Party Call button per friend; honest empty state when clanless), Note (only visible to you), Clan card, Level / Rank + Reputation + Missions Done + Events Attended stat cards (all server-counted from ViceSunshine records), and Linked accounts (PSN / Xbox / Discord + other links with linked/hidden/not-linked states and copyable visible links). Activity holds My events (RSVPs), My clans + contribute form, Recent points and Transaction history. Player Card copy lives at the bottom of Main.
- Private note (migration 0023: `profiles.private_note`): saved via `saveProfileNote` (≤500 chars) and returned ONLY by `getProfile` for the requesting player's own key — it is never in listPlayers, clan rosters or any other player's payload, and the UI labels it "(only visible to you)".
- Wishlist (migration 0023: `profiles.wishlist`, JSON array of SHOP_ITEMS ids): `toggleWishlistItem` validates the item exists in SHOP_ITEMS; the Wishlist tab resolves ids against the real Shop catalogue (getShop), shows price/owned state with Remove + View in Shop, and offers one-tap Add for unowned personal items. Wishlist never buys anything by itself.
- `getProfile` extended (same action, no new source): displayName, bio, memberSince (created_at), privateNote, wishlistIds, clanId/clanTag/clanRole/clanColor/clanEmblem, and friends[] (gamertag, role, platform, avatar frame/art, name colour, inVoice).

## Shop — Discord-style storefront, original collections & bundles (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Shop rebuilt to the STRUCTURE of Ben's Discord Shop screenshots (layout only — no Discord logo/branding, and NO Marvel / Dead by Daylight / Hello Kitty / Spider-Man / Zenless Zone Zero names, art or items anywhere): top bar (Shop title, Vice Credits balance pill → Vice Credits Shop, wishlist heart with count badge, hamburger opening a Shop Menu dropdown with Featured ✓ / Shop All / Vice Credits Shop), a big animated hero banner, a horizontal featured-bundle row, full-width collection banners, a "Find your style" section with a Shop All button, and 2-per-row (phone) bundle/item cards with a large visual, a heart top-right, name, and CAD price with a green discount % on bundles.
- Original ViceSunshine collections (server `SHOP_COLLECTIONS`, creative and collectible, all original): Sunset Strip, Neon Nights, Street Racer, Leonida Keys, Haunted Leonida (limited — Halloween season), Frost Vice (limited — Winter season), Vice Essentials, Clan Vault. 23 new items (server SHOP_ITEMS with collection + cad fields; older items fall back via `collectionOf`/`cadOf`): avatar frames (Sunset Glow, Chromawave, Checkered Racer, Palm Shade, Haunted Vines, Frostbite — new frame values styled in CosmeticAvatar with an animated pulse on premium frames), profile effects (accent kind: Neon Pink), profile banner colours, name colours, badges, and a Sunset clan emblem effect (new CSS `fx-sunset`, joining Lightning / Fire / Ice / Poison / Neon / Shadow / Gold).
- Collection pages open as a full-screen themed view: the collection's own high-resolution original painted illustration (7 new build-time artworks in `client/src/assets/shop-*.webp`, 2352–2736px wide, verified crisp, no text/logos — hero boutique, sunset Ocean Drive, neon street, street race, Keys causeway, bloodmoon swamp, frost beach) blurred behind a tinted veil, back arrow top-left, collection title centred at top, limited-drop chip, then a grid of that collection's bundles + single items. Art is object-cover at fixed heights — never stretched.
- Bundles (server `SHOP_BUNDLES`): 8 original 3pc/4pc sets (Sunset 3pc, Neon Nights 4pc, Street Racer 3pc, Leonida Keys 4pc, Haunted 3pc, Frost 4pc, Vice Essentials 4pc, Clan Neon 3pc). Discount % is COMPUTED from real prices (bundle price vs sum of parts: −11% to −14%, matching the screenshot pattern) and CAD bundle prices are derived from the parts' CAD prices at the same rate. `buyBundle` grants every missing part and never charges twice for owned parts (price reduced pro-rata at the bundle rate); clan bundles spend the clan wallet (Leader/Co-Leader).
- Sell / trade back (`sellItem`): any owned Shop item (personal or clan) trades back for 50% of its Vice Credits price to the same wallet, logged as "Shop trade-back"; an equipped item is unequipped first and its look reverts (profile fields cleared / clan emblem back to shield, colour to pink, effect/banner/name-colour cleared). Clan Awards are explicitly rejected — earned, never sold. Free items trade back for 0.
- Prices: every item carries a display CAD shelf price (`cadOf`: explicit per-item cad, else a VC→CAD ladder 150→5.99 … 600→14.99; singles mostly CAD 5.99–12.99 like the screenshots' CAD 8.99 singles). In-app buying always spends Vice Credits; real-money purchase of items/packs stays gated behind `payments_not_connected` (Stripe test-mode only) with a saved-card one-tap label when a default card exists — no charge is ever taken yet. Wishlist hearts are server-backed everywhere via the existing `toggleWishlistItem` (extended to accept bundle ids), shared with the Profile Wishlist tab; the Shop's heart view is the same wishlist.
- Animation & quality pass (Shop + Profile): subtle GPU-friendly animation only (transform/opacity/background-position) — animated avatar frames (pulse) and the existing avatar bob/glow ring, emblem effects (flicker/shimmer/bubbles/shine per effect), bundle-card hover lift + tap press, heart pop on tap, hero background slow pan, and a 0.28s fade-up (`shop-enter`) on every Shop view/collection/tab switch; Profile tabs and cards keep the same language. All animations are disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`. Visual quality rule: only the verified high-resolution original illustrations are used as large visuals; item visuals are the existing crisp CSS/avatar renders, fully in frame at fixed stage heights.

## Shop organised by type (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner asked for one section per item type ("avatar one section, emblem one section etc"). The Shop is now organised BY TYPE everywhere items are browsed: `SHOP_TYPE_SECTIONS` in App.tsx is the single ordering source — Bundles (own section, first), Avatars, Clan Emblems, Avatar Frames, Profile Banners (bannerColor), Clan Banners (banner), Name Colours, Profile Effects (accent), Emblem Effects (effect), Badges, Clan Colours (color). Each section renders as a headed `<section>` (title + item count + one-line blurb, divider) with its own 2-per-row phone / 3-per-row desktop grid; empty types are skipped, nothing is mixed across types.
- Shop All no longer groups by collection: it renders the scope filter (Everything / For You / For Clan), a by-type jump nav (chips with counts that smooth-scroll to the section), then the type sections. The themed collections are unchanged as an entry point — collection banners on Featured and the full-screen collection pages still exist, and each collection page now ALSO renders its bundles + items through the same type sections (dark variant over the blurred art) with its own jump nav, instead of one mixed grid.
- Featured keeps the Discord-style layout (hero, featured-bundle row — bundles only, now with a visible "Featured bundles" heading, look preview, collection banners); its old mixed "Find your style" grid (bundles + premium items together) was replaced by the by-type jump nav: tapping a type switches to Shop All and scrolls straight to that type's section (pendingSection state, jump delayed past scrollPageToTopSoon's top re-asserts). The wishlist view renders through the same type sections + jump nav too.
- Nothing else changed: same items/bundles/collections data from `getShop`, same CAD prices with computed bundle discounts, wishlist hearts, buy / equip / sell-back at 50%, animations (`shop-enter`, animated frames/effects) and artwork. No server or schema change was needed — the organisation is purely how the existing catalogue is grouped client-side.

## Sidebar navigation fixes (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Collapsible sidebar sections (desktop sidebar + phone All-sections drawer share the same state): Your agent starts OPEN; Explore, GTA VI Database and Community start CLOSED on first load (useState defaults true/false/false/false). Headers are real toggle buttons with aria-expanded and ▾/› chevrons.
- Database selected state: tapping a GTA VI Database item (Vehicles / Weapons / Characters / All entries / Confirmed / Reported-Rumor / Unverified / Returning from GTA V) records dbActiveLabel; that item renders selected — teal-tinted background, teal border, bold white text, pink inset bar, teal dot, a "Selected" tag, aria-pressed + aria-current — in both the desktop sidebar and the phone drawer. Other sidebar items keep the existing aria-pressed selected style.
- Scroll-to-top on every navigation: `go()` now calls a scrollToTop helper that resets window, documentElement, body and the <main> column (different webviews scroll different elements), and a useEffect on tab does the same. Because the reset runs in the click handler itself, re-tapping the section or Database item you are already on also scrolls back to the top — it never stays mid-scroll.

## Voice calls removed — Discord server instead (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner direction: remove voice calls / Party Calls completely and add a Discord server instead. Deleted from the client: the Calls tab (sidebar Community nav, phone bottom nav, tab render), VoiceCallsView, VoicePanel, CallButton, InCallBadge, PartySharePanel, QrCodeSvg (and the now-unused qr.ts import + sec-calls banner use), clan Voice tab, event lobby VoicePanel, LFG team VoicePanel, profile/directory/roster Call buttons and "In a call" badges, Settings Party Call toggles/rows, and Party notification kinds in the Feed. Clan channels are text-only now (ClanChannelManager creates/lists text channels only, rendered inside the clan Chat tab).

## Discord card not visible — two pages called "Feed" (fix, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner report: "the Discord server card is not visible on the Community Feed page where it was supposed to be at the top". Diagnosis from the inspection evidence (audit 2026-11-19T09-53-41Z): the card itself was working — the audit's own tap on the phone bottom-nav "Feed" (act-001.png) shows it rendered at the top of the Community Feed with its sunset art and "Where the crew hangs out" heading. The real cause was a naming collision: TWO different pages were both labelled "Feed". The "Your agent" sidebar item labelled "Feed" (open and selected by default, desktop and phone All-sections) actually opens tab "home" — the Today briefing (HomeView), which has no Discord card. The real Community Feed (tab "feed", with the card) was hidden under the closed Explore dropdown (desktop) / behind the bottom-nav Feed (phone). The landing screenshots in the same audit show the selected "Feed" item displaying the Today briefing with no Discord card — exactly what the owner saw.
- Fix 1 — one name per page: the Your agent item for tab "home" is relabelled "Today" (AGENT_NAV), matching the bottom nav, which already called that page "Today" and the real feed "Feed". "Feed" now names exactly one page everywhere: the Community Feed. No tab ids, views or routes changed.
- Fix 2 — card literally first: in FeedView, DiscordServerCard now renders BEFORE the "Community Feed" heading and intro paragraph, so it is the first element on the page the moment the tab opens, on phone and desktop. It still renders exactly once in the whole app, still does not depend on any query loading (no invite set → the coming-soon placeholder shows), and its design/art/invite behaviour are unchanged.
- How to reach it now (accurate directions): phone — bottom-nav "Feed"; desktop — open "Explore" in the sidebar, tap "Community Feed" (first item, above News). The card is the first thing on that page.
