## Feed video fix, Muse-style Feed, TikTok Clips, Sign Up (Nov 19 2026)

- Diagnosis for "no videos play at all": the stored automatic videos
  are real — 53 of 54 sampled YouTube IDs returned 200 from YouTube's
  oEmbed endpoint (one, Cz6cNsAvnRY, is gone: 404). The player code
  was the failure: it mounted the embed at opacity 0 until an iframe
  load-event "ready" guess, and its JS-API handshake used the video
  id as the player id (YouTube ignores it), so play/pause commands
  raced and state never came back; blocked views sat on black.
- New YoutubePlayer: thumbnail facade first (hq→sd→mq chain), embed
  mounts in view and autoplays muted, messages matched by iframe
  window source, onReady reveals the frame, real playing state hides
  the facade, onError or 7s of silence retries the alternate host
  once then falls back to thumbnail + Watch on YouTube. One video
  plays at a time page-wide. `refreshFeedMedia` now verifies each
  found video via YouTube oEmbed (https://www.youtube.com/oembed)
  before storing it — gone / embedding-disabled videos are skipped.
- Feed rebuilt Muse-style: "Feed" heading, big gradient Create Post
  composer (category chips, title, text, YouTube link or upload),
  borderless posts (avatar, bold title, time ago, … menu, Like /
  Discuss / Repost / Save). New post switches the Feed to Latest so
  it appears at the top immediately.
- Clips rebuilt TikTok-style: vertical snap-scroll full-screen
  slides, active slide plays muted, right rail (Follow avatar, Like,
  Comment sheet, Share, Save), creator + caption bottom-left.
- Discord card moved: it now renders exactly once, on the Create
  Account page's final step (was: top of the Community Feed). Ask
  Vice's directions and the Settings / LFG / Events copy updated.
- Create Account: new `signup` tab — gradient button under the
  ViceSunshine logo top-left (sidebar + phone header) and a second one
  at the bottom of the page footer, both hidden once an account
  exists. Step 1: username + email + platform (saveProfile +
  saveSettings email). No password field: ViceSunshine sign-in is
  device-based by design (see Settings/legal copy) and the page says
  so. Step 2: link PSN / Xbox / Discord + the Discord card.
- Sidebar: fixed h-screen, no self-scroll (compact spacing, DB list
  caps at 30vh internally), profile/login bar fixed at bottom left.

- Missions Database enrichment (same edit): missions gained `source_url` (official
  source link) and `guide_url` (YouTube guide) columns (migration 0025).
  `listMissions` derives mediaType/embedUrl/videoId from guide_url via
  feedMediaOf, so the guide plays in-app through the shared facade player
  (thumbnail + Watch on YouTube fallback). The mission detail panel shows
  Region, Playable (Jason / Lucia), Type, numbered Steps (walkthrough split
  by line), Rewards, Tips, the Official source link, the Video guide, and
  Related clips — real Feed posts whose title/text names the mission,
  linking into Clips. Everything stays behind the spoiler shield until
  revealed. No mission rows were invented: Rockstar publishes no official
  mission list, so the database still fills only from verified entries
  (the Add mission form now collects the official link + guide link too).

- Trending news + editorial pictures + dead-video scrub (same edit):
  refreshFeedMedia gained a trending-news pass — the day's GTA VI news
  stories (e.g. TalkEsport, "N3on Finishes GTA 6 Story in 7 Hours,
  First to Complete It", https://www.talkesport.com/news/n3on-first-finish-gta-6-story-7-hours/)
  are added as Automatic News posts: bold headline, search-snippet
  summary, source name + link to the original article, deduped by
  title/URL, per-query caps so streamer news is not crowded out.
  Ben approved (Nov 19, 2026) real pictures for Feed news stories:
  the refresh fetches each article's og:image, stores the bytes as an
  owned blob (feed_posts.image_blob_key, migration 0026), and the
  card shows it large with a "Picture from {source} — editorial news
  use only" credit. Database / Customise / Profile art stays original
  illustration. The refresh also re-verifies EVERY Automatic video in
  the Feed on each run (oEmbed 404/401 = definitively dead): a dead
  video loses its player permanently and becomes a text post with a
  working Watch on YouTube link in the body — verified live on the
  previously dead video Cz6cNsAvnRY. Full sweep Nov 19, 2026: all 78
  unique video ids in the Feed returned oEmbed 200 (exist +
  embeddable) and all 78 hqdefault thumbnails returned 200.

## Feed/Clips video black-screen fix v3 (Nov 19 2026)

- Owner report (again): every video black on his real device; asked
  whether it is because ViceSunshine is "not online yet". Answer: no —
  YouTube embeds do not need the site to be publicly published; they
  play from any page. The preview inside Muse runs in an app webview
  whose origin/cookie handling differs from a normal browser tab, and
  the previous players depended on exactly the things that webview
  handles worst. Publishing to his own domain would, if anything,
  make embeds MORE reliable, but the player must not depend on it.
- Confirmed root causes in the v2 player (source-verified):
  (1) iframe held at opacity 0 until a postMessage onReady handshake
  that is unreliable in the app webview — frame invisible/frozen;
  (2) players never unmounted once scrolled past, so dozens of live
  YouTube iframes accumulated until the webview exhausted media
  resources and all players went black; (3) in Clips the player root
  had no height, so the h-full frame collapsed onto the slide's black
  background; (4) a restrictive referrerPolicy on the embed (missing
  Referer is a known cause of YouTube's black "error 153" player).
  The stored videos themselves are fine (oEmbed 200 sweep above).
- v3 player (`YoutubePlayer`, shared by Feed, Clips, Missions guides,
  home videos): nothing waits on postMessage — the iframe renders
  fully visible and autoplay is requested only via the embed URL
  (autoplay=1&mute=1&playsinline=1). The thumbnail facade sits OVER
  the mounting frame and fades on the plain iframe load event + a
  short settle, so no black box is exposed while it spins up. The
  iframe UNMOUNTS when scrolled out of view / its Clips slide goes
  inactive (at most ~1–2 live players). Clips vertical root is
  h-full w-full. No referrerPolicy restriction. The per-frame
  "listening" registration is still sent on load, but only as a
  best-effort subscription for onError/state messages: onError settles
  the card on the thumbnail fallback (Watch on YouTube + Reload
  player, which remounts on the alternate host, then in tap-to-play
  mode). A watchdog covers the case the messages never arrive: if no
  real playing signal shows within ~6s of reveal, a dismissible
  "Video staying black?" bar appears over the frame with Reload /
  Watch on YouTube — never a bare black box left on screen.
- Verification limit (honest): the audit browser blocks YouTube by
  design, so real playback cannot be captured in-audit; Ben's tap on
  a real device is the deciding check.

## Feed/Clips video black-screen fix v4 (Nov 19 2026)

- Owner report (again, after v3): videos still do not play at all on
  his real device — not one clip, every video. Asked again whether
  it is because the site is "not online yet": no (see v3 note).
- Evidence check before touching code: the isolated inspection copy
  of the database holds 71 YouTube posts (watch + shorts URLs); a
  live sample of stored ids (4jX2ibx6Qsw, sOcQ7zp2FLk, e42-sTApIhU,
  BFWiBMxce6E, GS0jS1g82sg, cpABTNIOD-A) all returned oEmbed 200 and
  the embed page itself returned 200. The videos were never the
  problem; the player was.
- Root causes in v3 (source-verified in the shipped component):
  (1) the thumbnail facade OVERLAID the iframe until a load+settle
  "reveal", and the facade's full-area play button only flipped a
  React state — a tap never reached YouTube's own player, so when
  autoplay was refused in the app webview the video could never be
  started by tapping; (2) the "staying black?" watchdog was gated on
  `revealed`, so a frame whose load stalled — the exact failure it
  existed for — never armed it: no host retry, no fallback, a
  permanent dead player; (3) the embed still carried enablejsapi=1
  with a hand-rolled "listening" registration under a made-up
  player id, and no explicit referrerPolicy (a missing Referer is a
  known cause of YouTube's black error-153 player; the hosting
  shell's page-level policy is outside ViceSunshine's control).
- v4 player (`YoutubePlayer`, shared by Feed, Clips, Missions
  guides): the standard lite-embed pattern — machinery removed, not
  added. Thumbnail facade with a real play button and NO iframe
  until the video should play; on scroll-into-view / active Clips
  slide / tap, the facade is REPLACED by the iframe (never overlaid,
  so nothing intercepts taps or hides the frame). Autoplay is
  requested only via the embed URL (autoplay=1&mute=1&playsinline=1
  &controls=1&rel=0); if autoplay is refused, YouTube's own poster +
  play button shows in the frame and taps land directly on it. No
  JS API at all (no enablejsapi, no postMessage, no handshake);
  "Tap for sound" remounts the embed with mute=0 off the tap.
  Primary host is youtube-nocookie (built for embedded/webview
  contexts, no cookie-consent dependency); the iframe carries
  referrerPolicy="strict-origin-when-cross-origin" so an Origin is
  always sent. A load watchdog armed AT MOUNT (8s, gated on nothing
  else) retries once on youtube.com, then settles on the thumbnail
  with working Reload / Watch on YouTube — never a bare black box.
  Iframes still unmount off-view so players cannot pile up.
- Probe verification (audit browser blocks YouTube by design, so
  real playback still cannot be captured in-audit — Ben's tap on a
  real device remains the deciding check): Feed video cards render
  thumbnail facades with "Play video" buttons; tapping one replaced
  the facade with the iframe (frame load fired, "Tap for sound"
  chip appeared, nothing overlaying the frame); the Clips active
  slide auto-mounted its player full-height with the TikTok rail
  and caption intact. No console errors.

## Clips is YouTube-free and playable-only (edit, Nov 19 2026 — supersedes Clips playback in all earlier sections)
- Owner correction, in two steps: (1) YouTube CAN remain as a link / Feed player outside Clips — e.g. a "Watch on YouTube" secondary link for a Feed news story, a guide, or a mission video guide (Missions keep their YouTube guide field and Feed player). (2) Clips must NOT use YouTube at all, and must contain ONLY clips that can actually be played in-app.
- Clips rule (single source, client `isNativePlayableClip` + server `isClipsPlayableMedia` / `listFeed.clipsPlayable`): a post enters Clips only when it is an uploaded MP4/WebM file — `mediaType === "video"`, direct `.mp4`/`.webm` URL, server `clipsPlayable === true`, and no YouTube URL/source anywhere on the post. YouTube (`mediaType "youtube"`), TikTok, X, Instagram, Vimeo, Twitch, plain links, images and text are ALL excluded from Clips, however they arrived (composer link, Share-from-elsewhere, automatic refresh, repost). Those posts stay in the Feed only.
- Clips rendering: ClipsView renders ONLY `NativeClip` (ViceSunshine's own `<video>` player) in vertical mode — there is no YoutubePlayer branch, no platform embed, no `i.ytimg.com` thumbnail and no "Watch on YouTube" link anywhere in the Clips view. The caption source line reads "Uploaded in ViceSunshine · plays here" plus, only when a non-YouTube source URL exists, a credited original link. MissionRelatedClips (which links into Clips) uses the same filter and no longer shows YouTube thumbnails.
- No black / dead clips: NativeClip now sits on a ViceSunshine gradient (pink→purple→teal) instead of black, stays transparent until `onCanPlay`/`onLoadedData` fires (with a play-disc + "Loading clip…" placeholder, never a black frame), autoplays muted when its slide is active, pauses off-slide, and "Tap for sound" appears only once playable. On file error it calls `onUnplayable`, and ClipsView adds that id to `unplayableIds` and removes the slide entirely for the session — an unplayable clip is not left in Clips in any form. Feed keeps a non-black gradient fallback with an "Open the clip" link for the same error outside Clips.
- Other platforms in the Feed (not Clips): server `feedMediaOf` now recognises TikTok (`tiktok.com/embed/v2/<id>`), Instagram (`instagram.com/<p|reel>/<code>/embed/`), X (`platform.twitter.com/embed/Tweet.html?id=<id>`) and Vimeo (`player.vimeo.com/video/<id>`) alongside YouTube, returning `mediaType` tiktok/instagram/x/vimeo + `embedUrl`/`videoId`. Client `FeedMediaBlock` routes those to the new `PlatformEmbedPlayer` (Feed only): gradient placeholder behind the frame, 9s load watchdog, and on failure a gradient link card ("Watch on TikTok/X/Instagram/Vimeo ↗", creator credited at the source) — never a black box. `shareExternalPost` now labels TikTok/Instagram/Vimeo sources by name. Composer and Share copy state the rule plainly: uploaded clips appear in Clips; linked videos stay in the Feed only.
- Legal copy updated to match: Privacy "Videos and external sites" and Cookies "Video platforms" now say platform embeds/links are Feed-only and Clips uses only the native player with no platform cookies.
- Automatic Feed refresh is unchanged in sourcing (YouTube videos for the Feed/News, with the existing dead-video sweep) — those automatic posts are Feed posts; under the rule above they can never enter Clips.

## YouTube link-only everywhere (fix, Nov 19 2026 — supersedes the Feed-player wording in the section above)
- The audit correction was confirmed against the owner's two messages: the original ask banned YouTube embeds/players everywhere; the follow-up re-allowed YouTube ONLY as a plain outbound link outside Clips ("a 'Watch on YouTube' link for a news story, guide, or mission video") — it did not re-allow an embedded player anywhere. "Keep every earlier ask it doesn't cancel" applies.
- Shipped state now: `YoutubePlayer` is deleted from the client (no YouTube iframe, no nocookie host, no YouTube thumbnail image loads anywhere). Server `feedMediaOf` returns `embedUrl: ""` for YouTube, so no embed can be built from its data. A YouTube post in the Feed / a mission guide renders ONLY as `YoutubeLinkCard`: a ViceSunshine gradient panel with a working "Watch on YouTube ↗" outbound link, credited to the source. TikTok / X / Instagram / Vimeo Feed embeds (PlatformEmbedPlayer) are unchanged — the owner named those platforms as embed sources. Clips is unchanged and stricter: no YouTube embed, thumbnail OR link, native playable uploads only, unplayable files removed via onUnplayable.
- Privacy/Cookies copy updated to state YouTube is never embedded and appears only as a link outside Clips.

## TikTok Clips + sidebar fixes (edit, Nov 19 2026 — supersedes Clips source in all earlier sections; Feed untouched)
- Owner's final Clips direction: the Feed stays EXACTLY as restored (its YouTube videos and the v4 lite-embed player are unchanged — no Feed code, query, or server field for existing posts was modified in behaviour). Clips alone changes source: no YouTube in Clips at all; Clips is sourced from TikTok.
- TikTok clips: canonical permalinks found by web search (browser.search, site:tiktok.com GTA VI queries; full share URLs returned verbatim in the search session) and validated live via TikTok's oEmbed endpoint (`https://www.tiktok.com/oembed?url=<permalink>` answered 200 with an official player iframe for every curated clip at build time — tested with curl from the build sandbox). Curated seed (creator / video id): hiper.ae / 7678864554830351636, cliip2gtavi / 7709121479690046753, gta6creator1 / 7697417842278321421, dr_gta6 / 7708423174878235926, gta6intelroom / 7706347329326124321, gtavi.clips5 / 7702451645417868576, tonysixoficial / 7617965414315773206. Permalinks are stored in the canonical `https://www.tiktok.com/@<handle>/video/<id>` form that oEmbed itself validated.
- Server: `tiktokIdOf` / `tiktokHandleOf` / `tiktokEmbeddable` (oEmbed 200 = exists + embedding allowed; network failure accepts, mirroring the YouTube pattern) / `tiktokDefinitivelyDead` (oEmbed 400/403/404). `ensureTikTokClips` stores missing curated clips as feed_posts rows (authorKey `viceverse-tiktok`, author = creator handle, sourceName/sourceUrl = TikTok permalink) so Like / Comment / Share / Follow work unchanged in Clips. `listClips` (new action) returns ONLY: TikTok rows that currently pass the oEmbed playability gate (checked per call, in parallel) + native MP4/WebM uploads — never YouTube. `refreshTikTokClips` (new action, Clips Refresh button) prunes definitively-dead automatic TikTok rows, re-seeds the curated set, and web-searches for more GTA VI TikTok clips, storing only oEmbed-confirmed ones (leak/download bait skipped). `listFeed` now excludes TikTok-media rows so TikTok clips live only in Clips and the Feed's content is byte-for-byte what it was for every existing post.
- Client Clips: structure kept EXACTLY TikTok-like — vertical full-screen snap slides, swipe up, right rail (creator avatar + Follow, Like, Comment, Share, Save), creator @handle + caption bottom-left, comments sheet. New `TikTokPlayer` renders TikTok's official player endpoint (`https://www.tiktok.com/player/v1/<id>` — the src oEmbed returns) with `autoplay=1&muted=1` for the active slide only; inactive slides unmount and show a ViceSunshine-gradient placeholder (never black); the slide/container background is the ViceSunshine gradient, not black; a 9s load watchdog settles on a gradient card with Reload + Watch on TikTok; "Tap for sound" remounts unmuted. Iframe uses the social-embeds sandbox token set + no-referrer. Caption line credits "Source: TikTok — @creator" with a "Watch on TikTok ↗" link to the original. Native uploads still play via the unchanged NativeClip. Privacy copy updated: Feed = YouTube embeds, Clips = TikTok embeds.
- Sidebar (same edit, owner's follow-up): the paragraph "Unofficial Fan Community — Not affiliated with Rockstar Games or Take-Two Interactive. GTA VI is named only to describe what this community is about." was removed from the desktop sidebar ONLY — the disclaimer remains in the page footer and legal pages (and Settings > Legal). Independent scrolling: at lg the shell row is `h-dvh` + `overflow-hidden`; the main column is its own `overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain` scroller and the sidebar's nav area is `overflow-y-auto overscroll-contain` inside the fixed sidebar, so scrolling either never moves the other (scroll chaining stopped). Mobile All-sections nav and the Database sub-list also gained `overscroll-contain`. The existing scroll-to-top helper already resets `main`, so tab changes still land at the top of the main column.

## Settings modal, sidebar accordion, Database simplification (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Settings is now a MODAL modelled on Muse's own Settings (owner's screenshots): Settings / Premium and everything account-related were removed from the sidebar nav (COMMUNITY_NAV); a Settings button with a gear icon + current-plan label sits at the bottom left of the desktop sidebar directly above the profile row (and at the bottom of the mobile All-sections drawer), opening the modal. Any legacy go("settings") caller (wallet chips, Profile, Premium page) opens the modal instead of a page. Modal: left nav General / Connectors / Wallet / Secure store / Permissions / Messaging channels / Devices / Data controls / Help & support / Legal info with Log out pinned at the bottom of the nav; right panel scrolls inside the modal; Escape / backdrop / X closes. General leads with the ViceSunshine account row (the Meta Account equivalent), then the plan section: NO percentage / usage tracking anywhere — instead a Free plan card and a Premium CA$4.99/month card side by side, Premium benefits listed (250 VC/week, ad-free, badge, clan cap 200, priority LFG, custom emblem), an Upgrade button (one-tap with a saved card, otherwise routes to Wallet), the current plan highlighted with a subtle border + "Current plan" label, and Premium cancel / undo-cancel managed inside the modal. Then Redeem invite code, Refer a Friend, profile fields, email, Language, currency / timezone auto-detect, and Appearance / Mode — all pre-existing Settings features preserved (linked PSN/Xbox/Discord + Discord invite admin in Connectors, saved cards in Wallet, privacy + blocked users in Permissions, notifications in Messaging channels, legal pages via Legal info).
- Professional polish (owner: no childish / gamey / scammy look): inside Settings only, bright accent fills were replaced with Muse-style muted styling — neutral white primary buttons (Upgrade, Save settings, Save card, Log out), subtle bordered "Current plan" / "Connected" / "Active" labels instead of solid neon pills, a muted avatar tile instead of the pink-purple gradient, and a subtle grey active state in the Settings nav. No layout, artwork or palette changes anywhere else; all prices and status labels remain the real stored values, and the unofficial fan-community labelling is unchanged.
- Sidebar accordion: the four sidebar dropdowns (Your agent, Explore, GTA VI Database, Community) now share ONE open-section state — opening any one closes the other three, on desktop and in the mobile drawer. Fresh loads still start with only "Your agent" open.
- Database simplification (owner: page felt complicated, design unchanged): the Group / Sort / class / status controls now sit behind a single "Filters" toggle (same chips and styles, auto-opening when a filter is active, with a Clear filters chip); search moved directly under the section tabs. Recently Updated is categorised by the selected section — Vehicles shows only vehicle updates, Weapons only weapons, Characters only characters, and the combined list appears only in the new All entries view (a fourth tab + the sidebar's All entries link now opens kind "All", grouping entries by kind). Section banners are unique per section: All entries has its own new original illustration (`client/src/assets/db-all.webp`, generated at build time: muscle car + rifle on a stand + two characters together, neon studio style, no text/logos); Vehicles / Weapons / Characters keep their existing distinct banners — no picture is repeated across sections.

## Settings — exact Muse replication (edit, Nov 19 2026 — supersedes the Settings presentation in the section above)
- Design source: Ben's own screenshots of Muse's Settings, supplied in the conversation (the bottom-left menu box, and the centred Settings modal on General). No web source; the layout, sizes and behaviour were copied from those screenshots and recoloured only.
- Three layers, exactly Muse's flow: (1) the sidebar's bottom left (desktop and the mobile All-sections drawer) carries ONLY a two-line account trigger — line 1 avatar + name, line 2 plan · Vice Credits — and nothing else settings-related anywhere in the sidebar; (2) pressing it opens a small popover box anchored bottom-left (Your profile / Wallet with balance / Premium with price-or-Active / Report an issue, a divider, then Settings with a ⌘, hint; outside-click and Escape close it); (3) pressing Settings in that box opens the full modal.
- Modal proportions copied from the screenshot: 880px max width, 24px radius, height min(780px, 94dvh); left nav 248px on a darker panel (#0f0f16) with a 17px semibold "Settings" title, 40px rows with real stroke icons (gear/grid/wallet/shield/hand/chat/monitor/lock/help/shield-check), the selected row filled one step lighter (#232333) with a 1.5px HOT-PINK outline where Muse uses blue, Log out pinned at the nav bottom (on phone the nav is one horizontal scrolling row, Log out at its end); right panel has a 17px semibold section title, an X in a 32px circle top-right, dim 13px section labels, and content on 16px-radius cards (#232333) stacked with 12–16px gaps.
- General panel order mirrors Muse's General exactly: ViceSunshine Account card (gradient initial mark, "ViceSunshine Account", "<name> — Password, security, personal details", ↗ top-right; pressing it scrolls to the account form), the Plan section in Muse's Usage-card layout (plan name left, honest status/price right — NO percentage or usage meter anywhere, sub line, divider, benefits, and the Upgrade link in hot pink where Muse's is blue; Free and Premium as two stacked cards, current plan outlined), Redeem invite code (gift icon, single line, chevron), Refer a Friend card, the wallet summary line, then Profile & account details and Account & security forms, the single-line Language row (current language + chevron; language/currency/timezone controls open underneath), and Appearance: a Mode card with Muse's sun/moon/monitor segmented pill (Dark selected — ViceSunshine is dark-only, the other segments explain that honestly) and the accent colour dots in a row under a divider inside the same card.
- All pre-existing Settings functionality is unchanged underneath (save, linked accounts, Discord invite admin, saved cards, one-tap Premium/packs, cancel/resume Premium, privacy/blocked, notifications, data export/clear, support, legal pages, log out). Deep links that already opened the modal directly (wallet balance chip, Profile/Premium page callers via go("settings")) still open it directly — only the sidebar entry gained Muse's two-step flow.

## Effects removed, Premium-only payments, Premium benefits enforced, sidebar fits (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Effects removed entirely: all emblem-effect catalogue items (fx-*), all profile-effect/accent items, and the two effect Clan Awards (Storm Champion / Champion Gold, plus their Clan Level 5 / 10 award entries) are deleted from `COSMETIC_ITEMS` / `AWARD_ITEMS` / `CLAN_AWARDS` in actions.ts; the Customise "Profile Effects" and "Emblem Effects" sections are gone; `ClanEmblem` no longer accepts or renders an effect; the `.emblem-fx` CSS is deleted; `clanSummaryOf` always returns `emblemEffect: ""` so legacy stored values never render. The `clans.emblem_effect` column stays in the schema (already-applied migration) but is never written or read for display anymore. Points/credits earning is unchanged.
- Premium is the ONLY paid product: `CREDIT_PACKS` and the `purchaseCredits` action are deleted, `getWallet` no longer returns packs, and the Settings Wallet credit-pack grid is removed. All referral/legal/Ask Vice copy now says the 10% referral discount and saved cards apply to Premium only, and that Vice Credits cannot be bought.
- Premium benefits are server-verified (new `premiumActiveFor` helper, built on `applyPremiumWeeklyCredits`, which also lapses cancelled Premium at the paid month's end): (1) Ad-free — `AdSlot` already returned null for Premium; both slots take `premium` from the real profile query. (2) Weekly credits — unchanged lazy grant of 250/week, anchored, no back-pay. (3) Clan cap — `createClan` and `createLfg` no longer accept a client `premium` flag; the clan cap is 200 when the founder's stored Premium is active, else 50; `effectiveClanCap` also upgrades a clan to 200 when its current Leader is Premium and is enforced in both `joinClan` and `reviewApplication` (the latter previously had no cap check). (4) Custom profile badge — new Premium-only catalogue item `badge-premium` ("Premium Member"); `equipCosmetic` rejects it without active Premium; Customise shows it with a Premium chip and an "Included with Premium" disabled state for free members. (5) Priority LFG — `createLfg` derives `priority` from the author's stored Premium; listings still sort priority-first. (6) Early event access — new: for the first 48 hours after an event is created, only Premium members may RSVP Going, enforced in `rsvpEvent`; `listEvents` returns `earlyAccess` / `earlyAccessUntil` / `canRsvpGoing` and the Events UI chips it and disables Going for free members during the window (Maybe stays open). (7) Custom clan emblem — new Premium-only item `emblem-vice-crest` ("vicecrest"), also selectable at clan creation (server rejects it for non-Premium founders; picker tile disabled for free members); `ClanEmblem` renders it with a gold frame + crest diamond. `equipCosmetic` gates it on the equipping Leader/Co-Leader's Premium.
- Sidebar fits with no scrolling: the desktop sidebar's nav wrapper is `overflow-hidden` (was `overflow-y-auto`), the Database sub-list lost its `max-h-[30vh]` scroll, and rows were compacted (`.sidebar-link` min-height 48→39px, padding 11→7px; `.side-head` 44→36px; tighter section/search/logo/footer spacing) so the whole sidebar — header, search, all four section heads, the one open dropdown (accordion: only one open at a time, unchanged), and the pinned two-line account trigger — fits a typical viewport with no scrollbar. The main column remains its own scroller. The phone "All sections" drawer is a menu overlay (not the sidebar) and keeps its own behaviour.

## Premium exclusives + Customise alignment + page scroll lock (edit, Nov 19 2026 — supersedes the "no effects" wording above)
- Premium exclusives (owner reversed the effects removal FOR PREMIUM ONLY): the free catalogue is unchanged and free members still have no effects. New `premiumOnly` catalogue items in `COSMETIC_ITEMS`, all gated server-side in `equipCosmetic` by the existing `premiumActiveFor` check (free members get a clear Premium-exclusive error; clan items gate on the equipping Leader/Co-Leader's Premium), never sold separately: (1) Six Premium avatars — Neon Outlaw, Alien Visitor, Supercar Ace, Sky Pilot (flying car), Vice Royalty, Phantom — added to `AVATAR_DEFS` with original per-avatar specs, so `seedAvatarArt` / the artwork cron generate their original illustrations into owned blobs exactly like the free avatars. (2) Six animated avatar effects gifted to Premium — Real Fire (fast orange flicker), Neon Surge, Lightning, Ice Frost, Gold Aura, Shadow Veil — a new personal cosmetic kind `effect`, stored in the new `profiles.avatar_effect` column (migration 0027), rendered by `CosmeticAvatar`'s `effect` prop as box-shadow glow animations (`.fx-*` in theme.css, reduced-motion safe with static glows). `getProfile` / `listPlayers` / profile friends now return `avatarEffect`, and the effect renders on the profile header, player directory, friends, sidebar account trigger and Customise previews. (3) Designed Premium banners, CSS-painted originals (no copied artwork): six profile banner designs (Supercar Nights, Flying Car Skyway, Alien Encounter, Vice Sunset Deluxe, Neon City Rain, Leonida Galaxy) as `bannerColor` items storing `design:<key>` in `profiles.bannerColor`, rendered via the shared `BANNER_DESIGNS` map + `profileBannerBackground` on the profile header; plus three clan banner designs (Supercar / Flying Car / Alien) as clan `banner` items storing the key in `clans.bannerStyle`, tinting the clan home header via `CLAN_BANNER_BG`. Premium page and Settings plan card now list these exclusives as benefits. Ask Vice / Terms copy updated to match (effects are Premium-only, never for points/credits/separate sale).
- Customise alignment: every card is now structurally identical — `.customise-card` (flex column, full row height), `.customise-stage` (fixed 176px stage), `.customise-stage-visual` (fixed 232×128 centred box), `.customise-card-body` (flex column with `.customise-desc` fixed 60px description height and the action pinned to the bottom via mt-auto) — in one uniform `gap-5` grid with `items-stretch`. All visuals normalised to the same box (avatars/effects/frames/emblems 112px centred; banners and colour panels full-width 112px tall). Premium items get their own sections (Premium Avatars, Avatar Effects — Premium, Premium Banner Designs, Premium Clan Banner Designs) via new `premiumOnly` / `excludePremium` section filters, so free and Premium never mix. The Profile > Customise tab's equipped list is now uniform 72px rows in a fixed kind order, each with a 48px visual thumb and an accurate Free/Premium chip (it previously labelled everything "Free").
- Page scroll lock (owner: the page scrolled sideways and past the bottom into blank space; grabbing the far-right/bottom scrollbar made the app disappear): theme.css pins `html, body` to height 100% with `overflow-x: clip`, `body { overflow-y: hidden }` and `overscroll-behavior: none`, and both shell wrappers (`[data-generated-space-root]`, `.hatch-space-root`) to height 100% / overflow hidden (SDK rules with higher-specificity selectors still win where they set their own sizing). The App root is now a `flex h-dvh flex-col overflow-hidden` column: mobile header and mobile bottom nav are flex-none shell rows (the bottom nav is no longer a fixed overlay), the shell row is `flex-1 min-h-0 overflow-hidden`, and the main column is the ONLY scroller at every width (`overflow-y-auto`, `[overflow-x:clip]`, `overscroll-contain`, min-h-0) — its scroll range is exactly its content, with the old 112px dead bottom padding reduced to normal end padding (40px phone / 48px desktop). Sideways page scroll is impossible; the far-edge scrollbars have nothing beyond the content to drag.

## Presence / online status (Nov 19, 2026)

- effectivePresence (server): self sees their chosen status; Ghost reads as Offline to everyone else; manual Offline reads Offline; last_seen older than 5 minutes (or never) reads Offline. touchPresence refreshes last_seen (60s throttle) on getProfile / getSettings.

## Premium badges + monthly rotating drops (Nov 19, 2026)

- ALL Premium-only items now belong to a monthly drop (dropMonth on CosmeticItemDef): Nov 2026 = "Vice Nights — Launch" (the original 29 Premium items); Dec 2026–Nov 2027 defined in MONTHLY_DROPS with holiday/season themes (Dec Christmas/winter, Jan New Year, Feb Valentine's, Mar Spring Break, Apr Neon Rain, May Golden Hour, Jun Summer Heat, Jul Boardwalk Fireworks, Aug Hurricane Season, Sep Autumn, Oct Halloween, Nov Year One) — each 2 avatars (AVATAR_DEFS specs added for artwork generation), 1 effect (reuses the six animated styles), 1 banner design (client BANNER_DESIGNS), 1 badge.

Owned items stay equippable forever — after the month ends and after Premium lapses. ## Clan Wars removed → Community Events hosting + discovery (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Clan Wars removed completely at the owner's direction: the `ClanWarSection` UI, the live Clan vs Clan scoreboard, and the server actions `getClanWar` / `joinClanWar` / `closeClanWar` are deleted; `recordWarActivity`, `WAR_POINTS`, `WAR_CHALLENGES`, `ensureCurrentWar` and `clanWarsWonMap` are deleted and every call site removed — no war points are counted anywhere anymore. All user-facing copy that mentioned Clan Wars / war points / "Event / War Rewards" was reworded (Rankings, How to Earn, Clan Perks, profile stats — the "Clan Wars won" tile became "Events held" — About, Terms, Ask Vice rules and chips). The `clan_wars` / `clan_war_entries` / `war_activity` tables remain in the schema as unused legacy tables (historical rows are not deleted). Seasonal Rankings (Global/Local/PlayStation/Xbox) and the separate Green vs Purple faction standings were NOT part of this removal and remain. One legacy seeded example event still carries its old "Clan War Showcase Night" title in stored data; its type is presented as "Clan Event" and fresh seeds use a Screenshot Contest example instead.
- Community Events (migration 0030: `events.visibility` Public/Private, `events.hashtags`, `events.host_clan_name`; the host clan is stored in the existing `events.clan_id`): creation is server-gated — `createEvent` requires the creator to be the clan's Leader (role checked via membership) of a clan with at least 10 members (counted server-side), with clear errors for non-Leaders ("you are Recruit…") and small clans ("needs at least 10 members — it has N"). `getMyEventHosting` returns each of the player's clans with member count, role, eligibility and the reason, driving both the Events create area and the Clans-page `CommunityEventsPromo` card that replaced the Clan War section in ClansView. Creating an event increments the clan's `eventsHeld`.
- Discovery: `listEvents` accepts search / hashtag / type / platform / visibility filters. Search by name matches title, type, description, host and host clan; a search starting with `#` (or the hashtag filter) matches hashtags only. Hashtags are normalised (lowercase, no #) and every event auto-carries type tags (Car Meet → #carmeet/#meetup/#cars, Race → #race, Time Trial → #timetrial, Screenshot Contest → #screenshot, Clan Event → #clan, etc.) plus the host's own tags. The Events page is a discovery page: search box, hashtag chips derived from real events with counts, type/platform/Public-Private filters, result count, and cards showing Public/Private badge, date, platform, max players, spots left, host + host clan, and clickable hashtag chips. Private events are filtered out of `listEvents` for everyone except the host clan's members and the host, and `rsvpEvent` re-checks privacy server-side.
- Private-event invites (follow-up, migration 0031: `events.invite_code`): creating a Private event generates a unique 6-character invite code (unambiguous alphabet). The code is returned by `listEvents` only to the host and host-clan members, whose event card shows an invite panel with the code and a Copy invite button. Anyone with the code can view and join that one event: `getEventByInviteCode` returns the event by code, `rsvpEvent` accepts an optional `inviteCode` that admits a non-clan player, and invite links work as `#event=CODE` (parsed on load like the existing `#ref=` referral links, stored on-device, auto-looked-up when the Events page opens; the Events page also has a manual "Joining a private event?" code box). Verified in the sandbox: Leader gate and 10-member gate error correctly; an outsider's list hides the private event; #carmeet search finds car meets without leaking the private one; RSVP without a code is rejected and with the code succeeds.

## Starter Guides library (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- `seedExamples` now also seeds nine starter guides (author "ViceSunshine Guides", idempotent — only while no such guides exist): Beginner first-day guide, Money (honest launch economy + scam warnings), Cars and Weapons (built on the Database's confirmed/reported/unverified/returning statuses — no invented rankings or stats), Missions (spoiler-safe walkthrough approach), Map & Locations (trailer-named regions, official map not released), Tips & Tricks (12 launch tips, including the new event hashtag/invite flows), Settings (PS5/Xbox checklist framed as what to look for), and Fixes (launch-day troubleshooting). Bodies are step-formatted plain text with the official Rockstar URL (supplied verbatim in earlier briefs) where relevant. Guide categories were extended client-side with Missions, Map & Locations and Tips & Tricks, and every guide card now shows its category's own original ViceSunshine illustration (GUIDE_ART map over existing owned assets — no new or external images).

## Database is automatic — no user entries or edits (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- At the owner's direction, users can no longer add or edit Database entries: the `addDbEntry` action is deleted (verified: calling it returns "action is not defined") and the client's Suggest-an-entry form, its state and mutation are removed, replaced by a "Maintained automatically — no user entries" card explaining that the ViceSunshine team maintains the Database from official Rockstar sources and errors can be raised via Ask Vice → support. Community 1–5 ratings remain (an opinion score shown as "Community rating", not an entry edit).
- `seedExamples`' Database section is now a true sync against the team-maintained catalog in code (the two Confirmed protagonists + the weapons/vehicles/supporting-characters catalog): it inserts missing entries (`dbAdded`), updates stored entries whose category/description/status/source/type/manufacturer/featured differ from the catalog (`dbUpdated` — this is what makes status badges automatic from official data), and deletes stored entries that are not in the catalog together with their ratings (`dbRemoved`) — non-catalog rows can only be old user suggestions. The sync runs as the post-submit action, so production is cleaned and kept current on every build; Ask Vice's rules gained the matching Database fact.

## Automatic / self-monitoring systems + automatic moderation (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Automation cadence (artifact-action schedules installed by this build, on top of the existing daily agent jobs for news/feed/artwork): `refreshNews` every 1h (news + patch tracking), `refreshFeedMedia` every 3h (trending media), `automationSweep` every 1h (moderation re-check), `seedExamples` daily (runs the automatic Database sync). "Every second" tracking is not possible or honest for source-bound data — sources are checked on these schedules and the app says so. `getAutomationStatus` reads the meta timestamps each refresh already writes (`last_refreshed_at`, `last_feed_media_refresh_at`, `last_tiktok_clips_refresh_at`, plus new `last_db_sync_at` written by the Database sync and `last_automation_sweep_at` written by the sweep) and powers a live status line on News ("Automatically updated — last checked …") and the full status card in the Owner Dashboard.
- Automatic moderation: `screenText` / `assertClean` / `screenMediaUrl` (server helpers) screen ALL public user content BEFORE it is stored — Feed posts (title/body/media URL), comments, quote-repost comments, clan chat, guides, LFG posts, events, clan names/tags/mottos, profiles (gamertag/display name/bio), bug reports and Easter egg reports. Categories: sexual content involving minors (illegal, always blocked), explicit sexual content / nudity, leaked-pirated-illegal download offers, scams (generators, password/code/card requests), real-world illegal trade, severe hate slurs, personal contact details in public posts, and direct file-download media links (videos are shared via YouTube/TikTok embeds only; users can also upload images/clips to the Feed via `uploadFeedMedia` — JPEG/PNG uploads are vision-screened by the moderation model inside that action BEFORE storage (nudity/sexual content, minors, graphic real-world violence, illegal trade, scam/leak adverts rejected and never stored); WebP/GIF/video uploads cannot be read by the checker and rely on text screening, reports, the sweep and the owner queue, which the copy states honestly). Blocked posts never reach the database; the error names the rule and offers a human review via support. The hourly `automationSweep` re-screens the newest 300 Feed posts / comments / clan messages: failing posts are flagged, failing comments and clan messages are removed; automatic (team) posts are skipped. Reports: the existing report system now has teeth — at 3 reports a Feed post is flagged AND `listFeed` hides flagged posts from everyone except their author; the owner clears or removes them from the Owner Dashboard moderation queue.

## Owner access & Owner Dashboard (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- The Owner is the first / creator account — the earliest profile row in `profiles`. No separate login, website or secret: the owner uses their normal account, `getProfile` returns `isOwner` for that account only, and every owner action (`getOwnerDashboard`, `ownerRemoveFeedPost`, `ownerClearFeedFlag`, `ownerRemoveBug`, `ownerSetSupportStatus`) re-checks server-side via `assertOwner` and throws for anyone else. UI entry points render only when `isOwner`: an "Owner Dashboard" item (with OWNER label) in the bottom-left account popover, an OWNER pill beside the owner's name on the account trigger, and the `owner` tab view itself, which shows a restriction note to non-owners.
- Dashboard contents (all real counts from the database): total players, new players this week + the 12 newest, Premium members, clans, Feed posts, events; Premium & revenue — honest state: payments are NOT connected (Stripe test mode), so revenue taken is CA$0.00 and the card says exactly that rather than estimating sales; automatic-systems status (last run of each refresh/sweep + counts + currently flagged + open support); moderation queue (flagged Feed posts with report counts/reasons and Remove / Clear-flag controls, flagged bugs with Remove); recent reports; support requests with Mark resolved / Reopen. Verified negatively in the sandbox: a non-owner key gets the owner-only error; the positive view can only be exercised from the owner's own device key.

## Premium avatar effects are animated scenes, not glow rings (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- The four Premium effects were rebuilt as layered SVG scenes over the avatar (`AvatarFxScene` in the client, `.fxs-*` keyframes in theme.css; the stored effect values are unchanged, so existing equips keep working): `fire` = Flame Flyby (flame shapes streak left→right across the top, smoke puffs drift right→left), `neon-surge` = Hamster Wheel (a hamster with running legs patrols and bobs on top of the avatar while a dashed neon ring spins beneath it, second ring counter-rotating), `lightning` = Storm Strikes (three bolts flash down at staggered intervals from drifting storm clouds), `gold-aura` = Top Greeter (a little character stands on top of the avatar waving, gold sparkles rising). The heavy box-shadow glow animations for these four were retired to thin quiet rings; scenes are aria-hidden, pointer-transparent, and freeze under prefers-reduced-motion. Catalog item names/descriptions, the Premium page and Ask Vice were renamed to match (Flame Flyby, Hamster Wheel, Storm Strikes, Top Greeter).

## Scope note + automatic glitch / tips coverage (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Scope (owner, final word): ViceSunshine is a GTA VI site. It does NOT expand into a general all-GTA site — an expansion to GTA V / IV / San Andreas / Vice City / III was started and then explicitly cancelled by the owner before any of it was built (no game column, no classic catalog; the empty migration file was deleted unused). GTA V appears only where it helps explain GTA VI: "Returning from GTA V" Database entries and labels, comparisons in guides, and GTA Online context where relevant.
- Glitches / tips / tricks now flow in automatically for GTA VI: `refreshNews` added GTA VI glitch/bug-discovery and tips/tricks searches (hourly schedule), and `refreshFeedMedia` added a "GTA VI glitch bug discovered" Gameplay search (posts labelled as community reports with the source linked, pointing readers to the Bug Tracker for confirmed follow-up) and a "GTA VI tips and tricks" Tutorial search (3-hourly schedule). The existing title filters still block leak/download/piracy results, and nothing is invented when a search finds nothing usable.

## Events rule made explicit + Leader event control; LFG clearly separate (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner restated the split, now enforced and labelled end-to-end: Community Events can ONLY be created by a player who is in a clan AND is that clan's Leader AND the clan has at least 10 members total (server-gated in `createEvent`, unchanged). NEW: the hosting clan's Leader now actually controls the event afterwards — `eventItem.canManage` (Leader of host clan; legacy clan-less rows fall back to host gamertag) drives a "Leader controls" panel on the event card, and two new server actions enforce the same check (`isEventManager` helper): `updateEvent` (edit everything, including switching Public ↔ Private — going Private mints a fresh invite code so old codes die, going Public clears it) and `deleteEvent` (removes the event and all its RSVPs, logged in clan activity). The 10-member minimum is creation-only: a Leader keeps control of an existing event if the clan later dips below 10. `setEventResults` previously had NO auth (anyone could post results) — it now requires the managing Leader; `hostConfirmEvent` likewise accepts the hosting clan's Leader (not just the host gamertag). Managing Leaders also receive their own event's lobby details from `listEvents` / `getEventByInviteCode` so they can edit them (still never sent to anyone else except Going RSVPs).
- Find a Group (LFG) is labelled as the opposite, everywhere it matters: nav renamed "Find a Group", an explicit "No clan needed — open to everyone" panel (no clan, no Leader, no 10-member minimum; a quick, small, instant group), and the Events page opens with a two-panel "Community Events — clan-hosted" vs "Find a Group — open to everyone" explainer with cross-links both ways. Server behaviour for LFG was already open (`createLfg` / `joinLfg` have no clan checks) and is unchanged; `createLfg`'s description and Ask Vice's knowledge now state the contrast explicitly.

## Find a Group redesigned — clean and spacious (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner rejected the old Find a Group layout (screenshot): platform chips, activity chips, the mic toggle and the post button all ran together in one unlabelled wrap, the "No clan needed" explainer was a big text block, and each card crammed author/presence/when/language/skill/mic into one wrapping line. `LfgView` was restructured (client only, no server changes): (1) the explainer is now one slim line ("No clan needed — open to everyone" + a Community Events text link); (2) one big obvious primary CTA "+ Post a quick group" (54px tall) with a "takes 10 seconds" helper sits directly under the header and toggles the form, which itself got a heading, roomier padding and a full-width-on-mobile submit; (3) filters live in their own card as three clearly labelled groups — Platform / Activity / Options — with larger 42px chips, plus a "Clear filters" button that only appears when a filter is active, and a result-count line ("N open groups"); (4) group cards are now a single spacious column (p-5/6): pills row (activity, platform, Priority, "Your listing", Full/closed), large title, its own author + presence row, details as a labelled 2/4-column definition grid (When / Language / Skill / Mic — nothing shares a line), author account links on their own row, then a separated footer with a spots-filled progress bar and a big 48px Join group / Close listing button, and a one-line Discord note. Empty state points back to the top CTA.

## Customise trimmed to exactly 5 free + 3 Premium per section (edit, Nov 19 2026 — supersedes the 3–5 trim and monthly drops notes above)
- Owner's rule: EVERY Customise section holds exactly 5 free items and exactly 3 Premium items — no more. The server `COSMETIC_ITEMS` catalogue was rebuilt to exactly 104 items: 13 sections (scope + kind groups) × 8. Sections: Avatars, Avatar Frames, Avatar Effects, Profile Banners, Name Colours, Badges, Calling Cards, Themes (personal) and Clan Emblems, Clan Colours, Clan Banners, Clan Name Colours, Clan Calling Cards (clan) — each verified programmatically at 5 free + 3 Premium.
- Removed: all overflow free items (extra avatars Mechanic/DJ/Detective, extra frames, themed-set badges/banners/colours), all overflow Premium items (Vice Royalty, Golden Vice, Premium Founder, Inferno personal card, Alien profile banner, Ember Sparks as a Premium effect), and the ENTIRE monthly Premium drop system (60 drop items, `MONTHLY_DROPS`, `DROP_THEMES`, `dropMonth` on the item def, drop availability/claim logic in `getCustomise`/`equipCosmetic`, and the client's expired/upcoming drop UI). Premium items are now evergreen: equippable with an active Premium, no monthly rotation. `AVATAR_DEFS` trimmed to the 8 kept avatars.
- Added only where a section was UNDER the rule, to reach exactly 5+3: Premium frames (Royal Gold, Void, Prism — new frame styles in `CosmeticAvatar`), 5 free effects (Ember Glow `gold-aura`, Frost Ring `ice`, Shadow Veil `shadow`, Neon Pulse and Sunset Haze — new `.fx-neon-pulse`/`.fx-sunset-haze` rings in theme.css; the 3 Premium effects stay the photorealistic Real Fire / Real Lightning / Real Smoke), Premium name colours (personal + clan), Premium clan colours, extra clan calling cards (reusing existing `CALLING_CARDS` designs), a Neon Strip clan banner (`CLAN_BANNER_BG`), Wave/Eclipse/Royal Palm emblems, and a whole new Themes section (kind `theme`, stored in the existing `profiles.themeAccent`; 5 free accents mirroring Settings + 3 Premium).
- Client: the split free/Premium subsections (Premium Avatars, Premium Banner Designs, etc.) were merged into one section per type; each section header shows "5 free · 3 Premium". Premium page, Settings plan card, Customise intro and Ask Vice copy updated to the kept sets. Clan Awards (`AWARD_ITEMS`) are a separate earned system, not a Customise catalogue section, and are unchanged. Client design maps for previously equippable values (old banner designs, calling cards) were left intact so profiles/clans already wearing a removed item still render.

## Premium avatar effects are PHOTOREALISTIC now — cartoon scenes removed (edit, Nov 19 2026 — supersedes the SVG scenes note above)
- Owner rejected the animated SVG scenes (hamster, greeter, drawn flames) as childish/cartoon and demanded REAL effects. The four Premium effects are now photorealistic photographic overlays: generated photographs of a real ring of fire, real lightning arcs, real smoke wisps and real golden embers/sparks on a pure black background (build-time assets `client/src/assets/fx-fire.webp`, `fx-lightning.webp`, `fx-smoke.webp`, `fx-embers.webp`, generated with the media tool for this purpose), rendered by `AvatarFxScene` as an `<img>` over the avatar with `mix-blend-mode: screen` (black disappears, only the real element rings the avatar) plus quiet realistic motion in theme.css (`.avatar-fx-photo`, `vv-fxp-*`: irregular flame flicker, sharp lightning double-flash, 48–64s smoke swirl, ember shimmer; still under prefers-reduced-motion). The wrap's old glow animations/box-shadows are switched off for photo effects. Stored values unchanged (`fire`, `lightning`, `neon-surge`, `gold-aura`, `shadow`), so existing equips keep working; display names are now Real Fire, Real Lightning, Real Smoke (value `neon-surge`; `shadow` drops render the same real smoke, darker/slower) and Ember Sparks (value `gold-aura`). `ice` has no photo and keeps its frost ring. Catalogue names/descriptions (incl. monthly-drop effect items reusing these values), the Premium page list and Ask Vice copy were renamed to match. The hamster / greeter / flyby scenes are deleted from code and CSS entirely.

## Avatar Frames & Name Colours removed — Avatar Effects kept (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner's final direction: Avatar Frames are removed completely and Name Colours are removed completely; Avatar Effects STAY (the earlier "remove avatar effects" ask was corrected twice — effects, including the photorealistic Premium Real Fire / Real Lightning / Real Smoke, are untouched).
- Server catalogue (`COSMETIC_ITEMS`): all 8 `frame` items and all 16 `nameColor` items (personal + clan) deleted; the `frame` / `nameColor` kinds are gone from the item type, and the equip/unequip handlers no longer write `avatar_frame` / `name_color`. Customise now has 10 sections (Avatars, Avatar Effects, Profile Banners, Badges, Calling Cards, Themes, Clan Emblems, Clan Colours, Clan Banners, Clan Calling Cards), each still 5 free + 3 Premium.
- Names are white only: every server response now returns `nameColor: ""` / `authorNameColor: ""` / `avatarFrame: ""` / `authorFrame: ""` regardless of any legacy stored value, and the client no longer applies any name-colour style anywhere — profile names, clan names, clan roster names, Feed/clan-post authors, friends and the player directory all render in the default white text colour. Legacy `profiles.name_color` / `profiles.avatar_frame` / `clans.name_color` columns remain in the schema but are never written or read for display anymore.
- Client: `CosmeticAvatar` no longer takes a `frame` prop (plain thin border only; the effect wrapper/FX_PHOTO system is unchanged), no `frame={...}` props remain, the Avatar Frames / Name Colours / Clan Name Colours sections, KIND_LABEL entries, CosmeticVisual and equipped-item branches for those kinds are deleted, and the dead `.frame-anim` CSS was removed. All user-facing copy (Customise, Premium page, profile Customise tab, Ask Vice rules, action descriptions) no longer mentions frames or name colours.

## Avatar Effects split — free glow frames, Premium realistic layered (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner's direction: the 5 free avatar effects must have NO real effects — each is just a glowing frame (CSS glow ring) around the avatar. The 3 Premium effects must be animated, realistic and impressive (the previous single flat photo overlay looked fake).
- Free effects (Ember Glow / Frost Ring / Shadow Veil / Neon Pulse / Sunset Haze): `FX_PHOTO` no longer maps their values, so no photograph renders for them at all — only the `.fx-*` CSS glow-ring animations in theme.css. Catalogue descriptions now say "just a soft glow ring, nothing more".
- Premium effects (Real Fire / Real Lightning / Real Smoke): `AvatarFxScene` now renders TWO layers of the same real photograph — a blurred ambient layer underneath (the light the element throws off, on its own slower cycle) and the sharp main layer on top — giving genuine depth instead of one flat picture. Motion rebuilt for realism: fire = fast irregular 7-step flicker with rotation/saturation shifts + slow ambient breathing; lightning = long quiet stretches then a sharp double flash with an ambient light burst; smoke = two layers counter-rotating at 52s/78s with scale/opacity breathing. Reduced-motion still freezes all of it.

## Integrated avatar effects, custom avatar upload, Discord-style status (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Avatar Effects redesigned AGAIN at the owner's direction: the photorealistic photo overlays (Real Fire / Lightning / Smoke) read as a SEPARATE thing floating around the circle, so they are gone — the fx-fire/fx-lightning/fx-smoke photo assets are no longer rendered and all photo-overlay CSS is deleted. Effects are now INTEGRATED accessories drawn as original inline SVG in the same 100×100 box as the avatar circle, bases overlapping / growing out of the circle, with the circle's rim taking the effect colour — effect + rim + avatar are one piece. The 8 catalogue value keys are unchanged (equipped effects survive), only names/descriptions/rendering changed. Free 5: Vice Crown (gold-aura), Frost Horns (ice), Bat Mask (shadow — the owner's own example), Neon Halo (neon-pulse, resting ON the circle), Sunset Cap (sunset-haze). Premium 3 (animated): Inferno Mohawk (fire — flame tongues rooted in the circle's top arc, flickering in place), Storm Helmet (lightning — metal dome following the circle's arc, lightning crest, sparking rivets), Dragon Horns (neon-surge — horns rooted into the circle, gold bands, centre gem). Section counts stay exactly 5 free + 3 Premium.
- Custom avatar pictures (migration 0032, profiles.custom_avatar_blob_key): new `uploadCustomAvatar` action — JPG/PNG only (the formats the moderation model can read, so no upload can skip the check), ≤4 MB, profile required. The image is screened by `ctx.inference` vision moderation (shared `assertImageAllowed` helper, also now used by uploadFeedMedia) BEFORE the blob is stored: nudity/sexual content, anything sexualising a minor, graphic gore, real-world illegal trade and scam/leak/piracy adverts are rejected and never stored. On success the blob key is stored on the profile and the picture takes precedence over the catalogue avatar everywhere avatars resolve (getProfile + friends, listPlayers, clan roster, LFG, Feed — via profileAvatarUrl / pickAvatar + customAvatarUrls helpers). Equipping a catalogue avatar clears the custom picture; `removeCustomAvatar` clears it directly. UI: "Your avatar picture" block at the top of the Profile edit form with Upload / Replace / Remove, the moderation rule stated up front, and errors shown next to the control.
- Discord-style presence (migration 0032, profiles.custom_status): internal presence values unchanged (Online / Away / Do Not Disturb / Ghost / Offline) but displayed Discord's way — Away = "Idle" (yellow crescent-moon icon), Ghost = "Invisible" (grey hollow ring; still Offline to everyone else), Do Not Disturb = red circle with white minus, Online = green dot. New `setOnlineStatus` action sets presence and/or a custom status text (≤80 chars, text-screened by assertClean before storing). Profile header gains Discord's speech-bubble "Add Status" button next to the avatar (shows the custom status when set) opening a "Change Online Status" sheet: title, "Online Status" label, one radio card with Online / Idle / Do Not Disturb / Invisible (filled ViceSunshine-pink radio on the selected row), a separate "Set a custom status" card below that expands to an editor with Save / Clear, and the invisible/inactivity note. Settings' Status picker now offers the same 4 labelled choices and shows the current custom status.
- Slim footer (same edit): the full-width footer block (disclaimer + two legal paragraphs + 9 pill buttons + big "New to ViceSunshine?" CTA, ~450px on every page) is replaced by a slim collapsed bar — the one-line unofficial-fan-community disclaimer (always visible), a "Legal & About" expand toggle, and a quiet "Create Account — free" text link for visitors. Expanding reveals the full legal text verbatim and all 9 legal links (Privacy Policy, Terms, About & Contact, Cookies, Refunds, Community Guidelines, Copyright / DMCA, Contact & Support, Ads Disclosure) as compact text links, plus the New-to-ViceSunshine line. All legal content stays reachable in one tap (and unchanged in Settings / the Legal pages); the big gradient CTA block is gone from the footer (Create Account still lives in the sidebar, phone header and Sign Up page).

## Calling Cards / Banners merge + COD-style redesign (Nov 19, 2026)
- Banner colours REMOVED entirely: the `bannerColor` catalogue (5 free colours + 3 Premium designs) and the clan `banner` styles (5 free + 3 Premium) are deleted from the server catalogue and from Customise. Stored legacy values are no longer surfaced (getProfile/listPlayers return bannerColor "", clan summaries return bannerStyle "").
- MERGED: the Calling Card IS the banner. Profile header renders the equipped card's design (background + scene art + title) as the banner; the clan home header does the same. The separate card strips under the profile/clan headers were removed (one thing, not two). The Founders Clan Award is now a gold "founders" calling card (kind callingCard) instead of a banner style.
- Merged sections keep exactly 5 free + 3 Premium: personal cards Vice Nights / Street Heat / Palm Reaper / Keys Runner / Skull Strip (free, STATIC) + Vice Haze / Hydro Vice / Magic Palms (Premium, ANIMATED); clan cards mirror that (Vice Haze / Dead Palms / Inferno Premium animated).
- COD-style original art: CALLING_CARDS designs now carry a `scene` + `animated` flag; CardSceneArt renders original inline SVG motifs (cannabis-style leaves, skulls, flame tongues, smoke) — inspired by COD weed / Dark Ops cards, never copied art. Animation classes (cc-*) apply only to animated designs; free cards are static. Reduced-motion disables all of it.
- Challenge calling cards (separate from the 5+3 catalogue, like Clan Awards): Dark Vice (reach Level 5), Skull Harvest (10 Feed posts), Vicestronaut (RSVP Going to 5 events) — progress computed server-side from in-app activity only (challengeProgress), shown in Customise with Locked/Unlocked state, and equipCosmetic refuses locked cards server-side. All three are animated once earned.

## Season Challenges, XP levels + auto-generated season calling cards (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- XP = Rank Points (one automatic count, in-app activity only). Rates now exactly the owner's table: mission ticked +10 (was +5), attend event (RSVP Going) +15, Feed post +5 (NEW — createFeedPost now records points), post reaching 10 likes +10, recruit +15, form LFG +10, join LFG +10 (was +5), event win +50/+30/+20, verified egg +25, confirmed bug +20, clan post +2. Daily caps adjusted (mission 100, feedpost 25, lfg 50).
- Player level thresholds rebuilt to the owner's ladder: Level 2 at 200 XP, 3 at 500, 4 at 1,000, 5 at 2,000, then the widening steps [3500, 5500, 8000, 11000, 14500] and growing +4,000/+4,500/… steps beyond (xpThresholdForLevel / playerLevelFromPoints now returns levelStart too; getWallet exposes viceLevelStartPoints). The old sqrt formula (L2 at 50) is gone. Clan rank level already used the same ladder and is unchanged. Level milestone credit payouts (L5/L10/…) unchanged.
- Season Challenges (new Challenges tab + getSeasonChallenges action): seasons are calendar months, Season 1 = Nov 2026 (seasonNumberOfKey / seasonKeyForNumber). Seven web-based-only challenges per season, progress counted server-side from the player's own rows in that season's month (seasonMetricValues): RSVP-Going events, done mission ticks (updatedAt), original Feed posts, distinct LFG teams formed/joined, likes received on own posts (excluding self-likes), members recruited into the player's clan (excluding self), and lifetime XP level. Targets scale every season: events 5·s, feed posts 5·s, likes 25·s, missions 3+2(s−1), LFG 3+2(s−1), recruits 2+2(s−1), level target 3/5/7/10/12/15/18/20 then +3/season. Past seasons remain viewable with their historical progress.
- Unlocks are automatic: grantSeasonUnlocks inserts the owned_items row when a completed challenge's board (or Customise) is read — no claim step. equipCosmetic re-checks season challenge progress server-side and refuses locked season cards with the challenge + progress named. Season cards also appear in Customise (current season set with progress, plus owned past-season cards) and stay equippable forever once unlocked.
- Auto-generated season calling cards: definitions are generated deterministically from the season number (theme list Vice Nights → Neon Storm → Haze Empire → …, per-metric card names/scenes), item ids `card-season-<n>-<metric>`, design values `s<n>-<metric>`. The ARTWORK is genuinely generated: generateSeasonCardArtFor paints an original wide banner per card via ctx.tool.generate_media with a brief that escalates by design tier (tier = season number: T1 bold flat neon; T2 + smoke/leaves/depth; T3 + flames/glowing eyes/contrast; T4 + shimmer/cinematic light; T5+ maximum detail, "strictly richer than tier N−1"), stored as owned blobs under meta `season-card-art:<value>`. Generation is driven automatically by the existing artwork schedule (seedArtwork paints one missing current-season card per run) and by the refreshSeasonCards action (up to 2 per call); a new season therefore generates its own set with no manual step. Client rendering mirrors the escalation even before art lands: seasonCardDesignOf derives palette/scene from the key, adds light layers at tiers 2/3/5, animation from Season 2, and the shimmer sweep from Tier 4; cardDesignOf is now the single lookup for profile/clan banners so season cards render everywhere catalogue cards do. Style kept: the loved COD-style dark cards with skulls, neon-green leaves, fire and smoke — original ViceSunshine art only.

## Sign-up Step 1 back to email-only + simplified egg detail tab (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Owner reversed the Discord sign-in / 2FA direction: Create Account Step 1 is email sign-up ONLY again (username + email + platform + "Sign up with email"). The half-wired "Continue with Discord" OAuth flow and the email-code 2FA phase were removed from `SignUpView` (the `createAccount` mutation is restored: saveProfile creates the account with its 500 starting credits + referral, then saveSettings stores the contact email, then Step 2). No Discord button, no OAuth return handling, no code entry anywhere on Step 1. The Discord OAuth + email-code machinery was then removed entirely (audit correction): `completeDiscordSignUp` / `getDiscordSignInStatus` / `setDiscordOAuthConfig` / `discordOAuthConfig` / `discordAuthorizeUrl`, `requestEmailCode` / `verifyEmailCodeAndSignUp` and the email-code helpers are deleted from actions.ts, the Settings `DiscordOAuthSettings` panel is deleted, the `email_verification_codes` table is removed from schema.ts, and migration 0039 drops the table (0038 itself is untouched, as applied migrations are never edited).
- Step 2 "One more step / Link & join" unchanged and confirmed as the owner asked: linked accounts (PSN / Xbox / Discord username) side by side with the Discord server card on desktop, card stacked directly after the fields on phone, Join opens https://discord.gg/NDH9XxyVkW (via the stored invite), and "Save & finish" sits all the way at the bottom after the Discord card.
- Egg detail tab simplified (owner's direction): ONE best-matching video about that exact egg playing inline (first Video from the automatically gathered egg_media), OR one picture (the egg's own Ideas illustration) when no video exists — the old "Videos about this egg" list, "Guides & coverage" list, "From ViceSunshine" related posts/guides and "More eggs to try" sections are gone from the tab. New "Is it real?" verdict block: Confirmed → "Real — confirmed", Reported → "Unverified — reported, not yet confirmed", Rumor → "Rumor — not confirmed", each with a short plain explanation, plus ONE check link — labelled official Rockstar when the egg's source URL is rockstargames.com, otherwise the original source report (falling back to the first gathered coverage). Automatic refresh on open (stale > 6h) is unchanged. Ideas page untouched; the Easter Eggs exploring list stays text-only (no pictures on egg entries).

## First idea whole-card tap + egg Recommendations + no-repeat content pictures (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- Ideas: ONLY the first card under "Trending ideas to try" (Trending #1, idx 0) is whole-card tappable — the article itself carries role=button/tabIndex/onClick/onKeyDown (Enter/Space) calling the existing openEgg(id), which switches to the eggs tab and renders that egg's simplified EggDetailView at the top. Inner picture/title/Try-it controls keep working (stopPropagation so the tap fires once); the Save button is wrapped in a stopPropagation span so saving never navigates. Other idea cards are byte-for-byte as before. Ideas hero and layout unchanged in structure.
- Egg detail Recommendations: at the VERY bottom of EggDetailView, a "More Easter eggs to try" section. Data = detail.moreEggs (server, other eggs) merged with listEggs, deduped, current egg excluded, capped at 12. Cards are SMALL (148px wide, 84px picture, truncated title, StatusBadge only). The row is a snap-x overflow-x-auto flex row: swipe/touch on phone, scroll arrows (desktop header + mobile footer) scrolling 340px smoothly, and pointer-drag scrolling on desktop (a drag suppresses the click so dragging never opens a card). Tapping a card calls openEgg(rec.id) — that egg's own detail tab, scrolled to top.
- No-repeat content pictures (Customise explicitly OUT of scope — avatars/calling cards/emblems/effects/badges untouched): audited every static content image in App.tsx; each content image variable is now used exactly once. New original illustrations generated at build time with media.generate_image into client/src/assets/ (painted Vice neon style, no text/logos): ideas-hero.webp (Ideas hero, was sec-eggs), saved-hero.webp (Saved header, was hero), release-hero.webp (Release Times background, was hero), news-featured.webp (News featured story, was hero), goals-wallet.webp (Goals wallet card, was clan-arena), guide-beginner/money/cars/weapons/missions/map/tips/settings/fixes/help.webp (GUIDE_ART per category, were hero/clan-arena/db-vehicles/db-weapons/sec-missions/sec-map/sec-guides/sec-profile/sec-bugs/sec-news), egg-fallback-map/door/reef/sky/swamp.webp (ideaArtFor fallbacks). ideaArtFor(name, id) picks a fallback from a hash of name+id; IdeasView then de-duplicates the assignments across the rendered grid — the used set is seeded with the Ideas hero, and a card whose picture is already on the page gets the next unused fallback instead — so no two cards on the Ideas page ever show the same picture. EggDetail recommendations de-duplicate art inside the row the same way (never the current egg's picture, never repeated in the row). hero.webp now appears only as the Home hero; sec-eggs.webp only as the Easter Eggs section banner; clan-arena.webp only as the Crews list hero (ClanHome's no-calling-card fallback is now a crew-colour gradient with no picture, not a repeat); sec-events.webp only as the Events banner, and the Community Events promo uses its own dedicated events-promo.webp (a beachfront car meet at dusk, generated for this slot — audit correction: it previously reused shop-racer.webp, which is now referenced nowhere; the Shop itself was removed earlier, so its leftover shop-*.webp files are orphaned assets, not displayed pictures). Database entries keep their per-entry server illustrations (imageUrl) with per-entry monogram tiles as the no-image state; Feed/News editorial media stays per-post source media. Remaining same-file reuse is only dynamic per-item media (post mediaUrl, user avatars), which is the item's own picture, not a shared stock image.

## Egg detail origin-aware Back + sidebar (edit, Nov 19 2026)
- The egg detail tab now remembers WHERE it was opened from (eggOrigin state in the app shell): openEgg(id, origin) is called with "ideas" from IdeasView, "eggs" from EggsView and "saved" from SavedView; recommendation taps inside EggDetailView pass no origin, so the original origin is kept while browsing recommendations. The detail's back button is labelled for the origin — "← Ideas", "← Saved" or "← All Easter eggs" (both the main and the not-found back buttons) — and onBack returns there: go("ideas") / go("saved") for those origins, or clearing the open egg back to the list for the eggs origin. The bottom of the detail tab (Recommendations) and everything else in it are unchanged.
- Sidebar follows the origin, not the render tab: a derived navTab (the origin while an egg detail is open, otherwise the current tab) drives aria-pressed/aria-current highlighting in the desktop sidebar, the mobile drawer and the bottom nav, and openEgg opens the origin's sidebar section (Your Agent for ideas/saved, Explore for eggs). So a detail opened from Ideas keeps Ideas highlighted with Your Agent open; only a detail opened from the Easter Eggs list itself highlights Easter Eggs.

- Ideas hero: the "Start with one good idea" hero card at the top of Ideas is now whole-card tappable — role=button, tabIndex, Enter/Space (guarded so keys on inner buttons don't double-fire), cursor-pointer — and pressing it goes DIRECTLY to the Easter Eggs section via in-app navigation (go("eggs"); same tab, no external link, no new browser tab). Its three inner buttons keep their own actions with stopPropagation ("Try an idea now" still opens the first egg's detail tab, "Map status & known regions" still opens the Map, "All Easter eggs" still goes to Easter Eggs). The idea cards below the hero are unchanged.

The paragraph, count line and the three buttons sit on a deep navy strip (.home-navy) under the art inside the same frame. It is an original ViceSunshine mark: it does not copy the GTA VI "VI" logo, the Grand Theft Auto logo, or the Rockstar "R" logo. Used as the site logo beside the ViceSunshine wordmark in the desktop sidebar brand button and in the mobile header, and shown as the server icon on the Create Profile Discord card with a download link (download="vicesunshine-vs-logo.webp") so the same file can be used as the Discord server icon or a profile avatar.
