- **Updates removed from the sidebar ONLY**: the `updates` entry is removed from `EXPLORE_NAV`, so there is no Updates item in the Explore dropdown on desktop or in the phone drawer (both render `EXPLORE_NAV`). `navSectionForTab("updates")` now returns `""` (the tab lives in no dropdown). The `updates` tab itself is NOT deleted, and nothing else was removed — the search recommendation card and all other entry points are untouched.
- **Latest Update box → full Updates tab**: Home's minimised Latest Update box (with its Live dot) no longer sets an update focus / opens the single-patch detail view. It now clears any focus (`setUpdateFocus(null)`) and navigates to the `updates` tab, which opens `UpdatesView` on its list view — banner, "Updates & patch notes", verify card, Track an update, and the full table of all updates with official links — exactly the view the old sidebar item opened. A single patch's detail is still reachable by tapping its version inside the Updates tab. Home itself is otherwise exactly as it was (Leonida hero, minimised aligned Latest Update / Your Profile / Service Status cards, carousel, gateway buttons, Latest & trending).
- **Home bottom part kept whole**: the bottom of Home is present in full and was NOT removed by the clans→leaderboard switch — hub stats (News posts / Updates tracked / Bugs reported / Open LFG teams / Events), the Crew Leaderboard card under them, Latest & trending, Find your people, Refresh news from the web with its last-checked stamp, the visitor "Set your gamertag to join in" button, the Live Server Status block, and the shell footer below the page. The only bottom change remains the earlier one: the plain "clans" stat tile was switched to the Crew Leaderboard card.
- **All sidebar dropdowns share the Database guide line**: a shared `.side-drop` class in theme.css now wraps the items of ALL FOUR dropdowns (Your Agent, Explore, GTA VI Database, Community) in both the desktop sidebar and the phone drawer — the same clean vertical line on the left the Database dropdown had (`border-left: 1px solid var(--border)`), the same indent (12px margin to the line, 12px from line to items, 4px right padding), the same 4px row gap and 4px top gap, so every dropdown lines up identically. The drawer's extra 8px bottom spacing (`mb-2`) is preserved.

## Profile analytics graphs, visual Crew Leaderboard Top 200, Back + scroll restore, Home theming, carousel visuals, Clips rework (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Profile analytics — mini chart + Spend-insights graph (owner's direction)**: Home's Your Profile card no longer shows a plain "0 followers" line: it carries a mini smooth green SVG sparkline (the last 14 days of cumulative followers from the same real `getProfileAnalytics` data, flat along the bottom at 0) plus BOTH counts on the outside — "{followers} followers · {following} following". Inside, Profile insights keeps the Wealthsimple Spend-insights layout (round back button, centred title, gear opening Settings, Trends / Categories tabs, big monthly total with "3 month avg.", month dropdown, Activity list) and the graph is now a hand-built SVG `GrowthChart` — the Recharts library is GONE (it was the lag): Catmull-Rom-smoothed path, downsampled to ≤120 points, no animation loop, dotted grid, end dot, hover/touch readout with the FULL date ("Nov 19, 2026"). The category picker is exactly Followers / Following / Likes / Views / Posts / Comments / Saves, and a range picker slices the server's long cumulative daily `history` (Jan 1 of the current year — or the first tracked event if earlier — through today, real stored events only): 1M / 3M / YTD / All, with the delta + % for the chosen range under the graph. Server: `getProfileAnalytics` gained the `following` metric everywhere (totals, days, months, compare, recent — "Followed a player") and the `history` array. Analytics refetch relaxed 15s → 30s.
- **Crew Leaderboard — visual, Top 200, Compare (owner's direction)**: Home's Crew Leaderboard card now shows crew emblems, trend arrows (▲/▼/—/New) and rank bars (points vs the leader's) on the top-3 rows, plus a compact season-points line graph (top 3). Pressing it opens Crews at the Rankings section, which is now the full Top 200 view: each board (Global / Local / PlayStation / Xbox — the split lives only here) caps at its own Top 200 with a "Top N" pill and a name/tag search; a season graph (top 5 crews' cumulative season points at the 7/14/21/28-day checkpoints + Today, from real `rankPointEvents`) sits above the list; every row carries a rank bar; and a Compare block lets the player pick any two crews on the board and see them side by side (rank, season points, lifetime points, members, Crew Level, event wins, member-level lift, league — the stronger number highlighted). Server: `clanRankings` gained a `graphs` field ({global, local, playstation, xbox} → labels + top-5 crews with per-checkpoint effective points, raw points × that crew's member-level multiplier). Visuals are calm: thin lines, no fill on season lines, no glow — the clearest chart per job (bars for points, lines for points-over-time, rank bars in rows).
- **Back buttons + scroll restore for detail views (owner's direction)**: the shell keeps a back stack. Opening a detail view from a box/card — Latest Update box → Updates, Your Profile → Profile insights, Service Status → full status page, Crew Leaderboard card → Rankings, an Easter egg → its detail tab — goes through `goDetail` / `openEgg`, which first push {tab, scrollY, openEggId}. Each detail view shows a clear "← Back" button wired to `goBack`, which restores the SAME page at the SAME scroll position (re-asserted over ~1s while the view reloads; the shell's `<main>` is the scroller) — no refresh, no jump to the top. Plain navigation (`go`: sidebar, bottom nav, in-page links) clears the stack so a stale Back never fires. In-view sub-details keep their own backs (a patch's "← All updates", a crew's "← All crews", an egg detail's origin label).
- **Home boxes match the theme (owner's direction)**: a `.home-themed` scope on Home's root gives EVERY Home box — stat tiles, Crew Leaderboard, Latest & trending, Find your people, the three minimised cards, Live Server Status — the same treatment: a faint neon-purple → hot-pink → teal wash over the dark navy surface, purple-tinted borders instead of plain grey, and teal-edged ghost gateway buttons. One theme across the whole main page; no other page's cards change.
- **Carousel slides are visual-first (owner's direction)**: slides carry NO paragraph text anymore — just the kicker chip, a big bold title (24/30px, text shadow) and a time · source line over a stronger dark gradient overlay; the full story is one tap away (news slides link out, the release slide keeps its official-site verify pill, now top-right so it never collides with the title). Pictures are TOPIC-MATCHED per story via `NEWS_SLIDE_ART` rules scanned against the story's title + category (car meet → events-promo, racing/cars → guide-cars, money/heist → guide-money, mission → guide-missions, weapons → guide-weapons, Lucia/Jason/characters → db-characters, map/Leonida → guide-map, glitch/bug → sec-bugs, Easter egg → sec-eggs, trailer/video → sec-clips, tips/guide → guide-tips, settings → guide-settings, patch/update → sec-updates), first matching picture not already used wins, so a slide's picture always matches its topic and never repeats. Slide height 264 → 288px; the calm crossfade is unchanged.
- **Clips — scroll fix, sound on, no pause, cleaner boxes, For You / Following (owner's direction)**: (1) SCROLL: an embedded player's frame swallows touch/wheel gestures, so a swipe starting on the video never scrolled the snap container — the picture stayed stuck. Each slide now has a transparent swipe layer over the player (below caption z-10 and rail z-20, `touch-action: pan-y`), so swipes land on a normal in-page element and the container scrolls + snaps TikTok-style. (2) SOUND: there is no pause and no unmute button in Clips — clips autoplay and keep playing. Audio is a single Clips-level state: before the viewer's first interaction browsers forbid audible autoplay, so players start muted ONLY until the first pointer-down anywhere flips `audioOn`, after which every clip (TikTok embed remounts with muted=0; native clips unmute) plays with sound and keeps it. TikTokPlayer's per-clip "Tap for sound" button and NativeClip's tap-to-pause / sound button are removed in Clips mode (NativeClip keeps tap-for-sound in the Feed, where it is not passed `soundOn`). (3) BOXES: the stage is framed with a purple-tinted border, and each caption lays out creator + Follow + source chips, title, caption, then a clear stats row (likes · comments · shares with icons, matching the rail) above the time/views/source line. (4) TABS: For You / Following tabs sit at the top of Clips — For You is the recommendation mix (discoveries from creators you don't follow blended with followed creators' clips); Following shows ONLY followed creators' clips, same full-screen style. Following nobody yet → a clean empty state listing the creators currently posting clips, each with a Follow button; following people with no clips in the list → an empty state with Refresh / Back to For You. Follows remain the existing on-device follow list used by the rail's Follow buttons. Feed videos are unchanged in design (v4 lite-embed YouTube player: thumbnail facade with a real play button, muted autoplay in view, tap for sound, fullscreen via YouTube's controls, 8s watchdog → alternate host → thumbnail with Reload / Watch on YouTube; native uploads play with real controls) — never a bare black box.

## Stories order + Profile Suggestions (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Stories row order (owner's direction)**: the Feed Stories row keeps "Your story" first, then OTHER people's story circles (round profile picture + name, slideable left-to-right) ordered: people you follow FIRST, then unseen, then most recent (recommended). Server: `listStories` groups gained `followedByMe` (from real `profile_follows` rows) and the sort is followed → unseen → latest. Stories still only live 24 hours and still open the full-screen viewer in row order.
- **Profile Suggestions (owner's direction, Instagram-style, kept simple)**: new `getProfileSuggestions` action + a `ProfileSuggestionsRow` (round avatar, name, @handle, the plain reason, Follow/Following button) shown in the Feed under Stories and on another player's profile (where tapping a suggestion opens that profile). Ranking uses ONLY real stored data about the viewer: same crew (+6, reason names the crew), followed by people you follow (+4), authors whose posts you liked or commented on (+3), same region (+1), same platform (+1); remaining slots fill with the most-followed profiles ("Popular in the community"). Yourself, people you already follow and people you blocked are never suggested. Per-user search history is not stored anywhere in ViceSunshine, so searches honestly play no part in the ranking — the reasons shown are the real signals above.

## Rockstar service status + personal graph ranges + new hero (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Status surfaces track ROCKSTAR now (owner's direction)**: the Home "Rockstar status" card, the bottom-of-Home status block and the Service Status page no longer track this website (no more uptime / online players / News / Feed / Database / Moderation pipeline states there). They show the latest stored check of Rockstar's official service status page — overall state (operational / issues / down / unknown), per-service lines exactly as read, and the last-checked time — with a "Check now / Check again" button and a link to check it directly. Source (verbatim, the page Rockstar's own support articles link to): https://support.rockstargames.com/servicestatus — read by `refreshRockstarStatus`, which starts a background checker task that opens Rockstar's page itself and stores exactly what it says via `saveRockstarStatusCheck` (the server search index does not reliably surface that page, and only Rockstar's own page may drive the status — an early search-based version read a third-party summary and reported a false "down"; that path was removed). A silent schedule (`vicesunshine-rockstar-status`, every 30 minutes) plus an on-open stale check (over 5 minutes old) keep it live; the display shows Online / Maintenance / Offline with the universal status light (green steady = online, orange pulsing = maintenance, red flashing = down). Stored in `rockstar_status_checks` (migration 0046). Verified live Nov 19, 2026: the checker read the page and stored "operational" with all 16 service lines (GTA Online per platform, Red Dead Online, Online Services, Cfx). `getServerStatus` still exists and now only drives the Latest Update card's Live dot (update-check freshness).
- **Profile insights graph — personal tracking only, extended (owner's direction)**: the graph stays the player's own followers / following / likes / views / posts / comments / saves — no GTA VI data in it. Ranges are now 1Y / 2Y / 3Y / YTD / All (default 1Y, Followers the default category); the month dropdown lists EVERY month from the creation month through the current month; history extends to 3 years and starts on the day the ACCOUNT WAS CREATED — tracking starts on day one, no dates or backfilled history from before the account existed (anything logged earlier still counts into totals and the signed series' opening balance, it just never gets a graph date before creation day). Followers / Following are now SIGNED series built from a new `follow_events` log (migration 0046; `setFollow` writes +1 on follow, −1 on unfollow), so the line can go DOWN on unfollows — and when a range ends lower than it started the line turns RED (green when it grew or held). The chart's value domain is padded so the line starts in the MIDDLE of the graph with room to move either way; grid labels stay on the true data values, and dates/counts are labelled in full. Months and the 7-day compare for Followers / Following are NET change; the Activity list gains "Lost a follower" / "Unfollowed a player" entries. The "3 month avg." is the average of the last 3 months exactly.
- **New Home hero (owner's direction)**: `client/src/assets/hero-vice.webp` — a new original ViceSunshine painterly illustration (Vice City neon skyline at sunset: art-deco hotels in hot pink / neon purple, teal bay reflections, causeway, palm silhouettes, classic sports car on a wet street; 2352×1008, generated in-house, no text / logos / copied Rockstar art) is now the big "LEONIDA IS OPEN" hero picture on Home, replacing the previous dusk skyline (`hero.webp`, no longer imported anywhere; the file remains in assets).

## Home cards, live chart movement, X-style follow lists (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Your Profile card — followers only (owner's direction)**: the Home "Your profile" card no longer shows a following count — just "{n} followers" under the mini sparkline. (Following still exists on the profile itself and in Profile insights; only this card changed.)
- **Rockstar Status — never "Unknown" / "Not checked" (owner's direction)**: `getRockstarStatus` now falls back to the most recent check that actually READ Rockstar's page (overall ≠ "unknown"), with that check's own time and service lines, so a later unreadable check can no longer blank the status to "Unknown" — the Home card, the bottom-of-Home block and the full status page (all fed by this one action) show the real Online / Maintenance / Offline state with the universal light (green steady = online, orange pulsing = maintenance, red flashing = down). "unknown" is returned only when no check has ever been read; then `rockstarOverallMeta` words it "Checking…", both the Home card and the block auto-start a fresh `refreshRockstarStatus` check on sight (stale > 5 min also re-checks), and the block's "Not checked yet" line is gone ("Checking now…"). The checker's spawned-task instructions now tell it to wait for the service list to render and re-open the page once before reporting unreadable. The 30-minute `vicesunshine-rockstar-status` schedule is unchanged.
- **Insights graph moves on the FIRST follower, live (owner's direction)**: the bug was the GrowthChart value domain — padded around the data only, so a brand-new account's single day of history (min = max = the value) re-centred the dot mid-graph whatever the count, and a 0 → 1 step changed the total but not the line. The domain is now anchored at 0 (`domMin = min(minV, 0) − pad`, `domMax = max(maxV, 0) + pad`), so the first follower visibly lifts the line/dot and losing one drops it (red). Liveness: Profile insights and the Home Your Profile card now poll `getProfileAnalytics` every 15s, and `FollowButton` also invalidates `profile-analytics`, so line, dot, total and % change update from real follow activity with no manual refresh. Verified in the sandbox: one real `setFollow` moved the target's totals.followers 0 → 1 with the history's last day at 1 (earlier days 0), and an unfollow returned it to 0.
- **Status lights on EVERY status, never removed (owner's direction)**: every status display carries its universal light — green steady = online/live, orange pulsing = maintenance/delayed, red flashing = offline/down. Verified across all surfaces in this edit: the Home Rockstar Status card, the Rockstar Service Status block (header light + one light per service line + the green Live badge) and the full status page it feeds, the Latest Update card and Updates detail (`UpdateLiveDot`), the Profile insights Live badge, and every profile presence dot (Online / Idle / Do Not Disturb / Invisible / Offline). The brief "checking" state keeps a gently pulsing grey light — the checking dots in `UpdateLiveDot`, the Home Rockstar card and the status block no longer force `animation: none` — so no status ever renders as bare text without a light.
- **Profile follow lists — X design, fully working (owner's direction)**: `FollowListSheet` is now one sheet with three tabs — Verified Followers / Followers / Following — switchable in place, opened from the profile's Followers / Following counts (which already tapped through). All three tabs are real `listFollowList` data; Verified Followers is the followers list filtered to Premium members (the verified accounts here), with its own honest empty state. Rows keep avatar, name, @handle, Level and a working Follow / Following / Follow back button (`setFollow`, which invalidates the lists, the profile and the analytics), and tapping a row opens that person's profile. No dead tabs, no fake lists.

## Profile insights — direction + whole-profile sums, stated on the page (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Owner's rule, confirmed and now written on the page itself**: Profile insights tracks YOUR profile only — who interacted with YOUR profile and YOUR posts, never the other way around. Likes / Views / Comments / Saves / Reposts count only other people's actions on the player's own posts (the player's own likes/views/comments on their own posts are excluded server-side, and what the player likes or watches on OTHER people's posts never counts here). Every number is ONE total for the whole profile, summed across ALL of the player's posts — two videos' views add into a single Views total; nothing is shown per-post. This was already how `getProfileAnalytics` counted (verified in this edit — `feed_reactions` / `feed_views` / `feed_comments` / `feed_saves` / `feed_reposts` filtered to the player's own post ids, actor ≠ the player); the change in this edit is that the page now SAYS so: an explainer line under the Profile insights header, per-metric total captions under the graph ("likes received · all your posts added together", "views received · all your posts and videos added together", etc. via `ANALYTICS_TOTAL_CAPTIONS`), a whole-profile note in the Categories view, the Growth details subtitle reworded to "what other people did on your profile and your posts, all added together", and the no-profile banner reworded the same way. The `getProfileAnalytics` action description records the rule for future builders. No counting logic, ranges, or graph behaviour changed.

## Workable profile — X-style Edit profile, real Highlights, graph opening point (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Edit profile is one X-style sheet that actually saves (owner's direction)**: the Profile page's Edit profile button now opens a modal sheet modelled on X's Edit profile — banner on top with a Change banner picker (calling cards, equipped live via `equipCosmetic`), avatar with Upload your own picture (existing moderated upload), Choose an avatar (catalogue picker, equipping clears the uploaded photo server-side) and Remove picture, then Name, Bio (with character count), Location, Website, Username (gamertag), Platform, linked accounts with visibility, and Profile settings (Private profile, Show my crew). ONE Save runs `updateProfile` (new action: display name, username, bio, location, website, platform, profilePrivate, showClan — moderation-screened, creates the profile with the 500 starting credits when missing) then `saveProfile` (linked accounts); the sheet only closes when the save landed, and errors stay visible in the sheet. Location / website are new `profiles` columns (migration 0047), returned by `getProfile` / `getSocialProfile` / `getSettings` and shown on the profile under the bio (location line + real website link; blanked for private profiles viewed by others). `saveSettings` accepts location / website only as optional fields and never overwrites them when a caller doesn't send them, so saving Settings can't wipe what Edit profile saved.
- **Stories + Highlights actually work end to end (owner's direction)**: stories already posted via `createStory` (Feed Stories row + the profile's New story circle, photo/video upload through the moderated `uploadFeedMedia`, or a clip link). Highlights are now REAL saved collections — new `story_highlights` + `story_highlight_items` tables (migration 0047) and `listHighlights` / `createHighlight` / `updateHighlight` / `deleteHighlight` actions (own stories only, enforced server-side). The profile highlights row shows the live story first, then the real highlights with their own covers and titles; tapping one plays its stories in the full-screen viewer (highlight stories stay viewable past the 24-hour story window — the point of a highlight). The + Highlight circle opens an editor: name + a multi-select grid of the player's own stories from the story archive (live and expired alike, tap order = play order), with Post a story inside it when there's nothing to pick yet; after watching one of your own highlights, an Edit highlight bar offers rename / re-pick / delete (deleting a highlight never deletes the stories). The old sticker-derived pseudo-highlights that just opened the archive are gone.
- **Uploading pictures to posts — the real bug found and fixed**: uploads themselves stored fine (`uploadFeedMedia`, JPG/PNG/WebP/GIF to 4 MB, moderated before storage), but the returned blob URL (`./blobs/<token>`) carries no file extension, so `feedMediaOf` classified every uploaded picture as a plain "link" (posts rendered an "Open attached link" button instead of the picture) and every uploaded story photo as an "embed". `feedMediaOf` now decodes the blob token (base64url of the blob key, which ends with the original filename) and classifies uploads by their real type; story read paths (`listStories`, `listStoryArchive`, `listHighlights`) re-derive the type at read time via `storyMediaTypeOf`, so already-stored uploads heal too. Verified in this build's sandbox test: upload → story/post → both come back as mediaType "image".
- **Insights graph — the line now rises on a first-day +1 (owner's bug report)**: the previous fix anchored the chart domain at 0, which covers a follow landing on a LATER day, but when the follow lands on the account's FIRST tracked day (create profile, get followed the same day) every history point already contains it — the total went up while the line, dot, range delta and % change stayed flat. `getProfileAnalytics` now prepends an OPENING point to the history series: the true cumulative value at the start of the first tracked day, before that day's events (signed series: `valueAt(historyStart)`; counted metrics: events before `historyStart`). A +1 on any day — including day one — now steps the line up visibly, and a −1 steps it down (red), with the range delta and % reading the same series. The Home sparkline (`days` = the history tail) inherits the same opening point.

## Crew Leaderboard bar chart — always five bars, profile-graph vibe (Nov 19, 2026)

- **Home's Crew Leaderboard card is a bar graph like the owner's hand drawing (owner's direction)**: a clean SVG bar chart in the Profile insights graph's style — dark plot, dotted gridlines labelled with the true point values, a baseline, green bars (leader full-strength, fading slightly by rank). It ALWAYS shows exactly five bars: the current Top 5 crews with their points on top of each bar and rank / trend / crew NAME under the baseline. Spots no crew has taken render as flat stubs at 0 labelled "Open spot" — with nobody ranked, all five bars sit at 0 and the card says all five spots are open. The chart reads the live `clanRankings` standings (Home card re-reads every 15s; point-earning actions invalidate `clanRankings` immediately), so movement is automatic both ways: a crew in 6th that earns past 5th appears on the chart and the dropped crew's bar falls off, and bars resize as points land — no refresh, no pressing anything. The card still taps through to the full Crew Leaderboard page (Global / Local / PlayStation / Xbox, Top 1000 each), unchanged.

## Banner + log-out fixes, referral credits, bots, accounts hardening (Nov 19–20, 2026)

- **Profile banner fix (owner's bug report)**: the equip itself always saved server-side (`equipCosmetic` → `profiles.callingCard`, verified by action round-trip); the profile header just didn't reflect it reliably. The Edit profile sheet's equip, the Customise page equip/unequip, and the Challenges equip now write the new calling card straight into the cached `["profile", playerKey]` and `["social-profile", playerKey, playerKey]` query data the moment the equip lands (plus the existing invalidations), the banner picker stays open with the new banner outlined, and a "Banner updated — <name>" confirmation names the live card.
- **Log out fix (owner's bug report)**: the old log-out removed `leonida-player-key` and reloaded instantly; the removal did not survive the reload in the webview and the session returned (reproduced in the probe). Log out now writes a `"signed-out"` tombstone to `leonida-player-key` (twice, ~450 ms apart), clears the on-device caches, shows a "Signing you out…" state, and then resets IN PLACE via the App shell's new `onSignedOut` callback: the shell's key (now React state, was a one-shot memo) swaps to `freshGuestKey()` — a session-only guest key minted without consulting storage — the query cache is cleared, Settings closes and the app goes home, so signing out never depends on a reload or on storage flushing. `playerKey()` also honours the tombstone on later loads (session-only in-memory guest key, never overwritten); signing in / creating an account overwrites it with the real key. Verified in the mobile probe: signed-in → Log out → guest state (Create Account back, Profile locked), then Gmail + password sign-in restored the account including its banner. Note: the probe harness restores its session-start localStorage snapshot on reload, so no client storage write can survive a probe reload there; on a real phone the tombstone persists normally. A hooks-order crash in SettingsView found during this verification (a `useState` placed after the component's early returns — Settings crashed into the error boundary once data loaded) was fixed by moving the state up with the other hooks.
- **Refer a Friend pays Vice Credits, not a discount (owner's direction, supersedes the 10% discount)**: when a friend joins with your referral code, the REFERRER earns 200 Vice Credits (`creditPersonalWallet`, reason "Referral bonus — …") plus 15 XP via `recordPoints` (source "recruit" — the recruit row of the owner's XP table). Credits still only spend on the two allowed things (crew creation 500; an extra community event 200) and can never buy rank — the XP comes from the recruiting action itself. `getSettings`' referral block now reports `creditsEarned` (sum of referral-bonus transactions) instead of `discountAvailable`; the Settings UI, the share text and Premium checkout carry no discount wording. The legacy `profiles.referral_discount` column remains in the schema, unused.
- **Extra community event for 200 Vice Credits (owner's credits rule, made real)**: `createEvent` accepts `useCredits`; when a Leader is past the one-free-event-per-week limit and opts in, 200 credits are deducted from their personal wallet (reason "Extra community event — <title>") and the event is created; the paid event restarts the weekly clock. `getEventCreationStatus` returns `extraEventCost` (200), `creditBalance` and `canAffordExtra`; the Events page shows the balance + opt-in when weekly-blocked. Creating an event and Find a Group were also simplified (owner's direction): essentials only up front (title / type / platform / time / visibility; LFG: what / activity / platform / when), everything else under "More options"; private-event invite codes stay automatic.
- **Community seed — 200 members, 20 crews (owner's explicit direction)**: new idempotent `seedBots` action (run once as the post-submit action; a re-run changes nothing once ≥150 seed accounts exist, and it never touches real players' rows). It creates 200 accounts with keys `bot-<slug>` — every one a DIFFERENT realistic name (mixed gaming names and real names, checked against existing gamertags), every one with a catalogue avatar, most with a bio and an equipped calling card — spread across 20 Public crews of 9–13 members (varied names/tags/colours/emblems/platforms/regions, joinable straight away and listed as crew recommendations). Season Rank Points are chunked into realistic in-app actions: 95% low (5–180 XP, Level 1), 5% medium (220–900 XP) so real players overtake them easily. Feed life: ~67 posts across the Feed kinds, ~48 comments, likes on their own and on pre-existing posts. The seeded members also follow each other (~140 follows each) so profiles carry follower counts in the low hundreds. Per the owner's instruction these accounts are NOT labelled as bots anywhere in the UI. Note: the repeated "lol @lol" profiles in the owner's screenshot are duplicate UI sign-ups (the same-Gmail bug below), not seed data — no seed code ever created them.
- **Accounts hardened (owner's critical report)**: (1) ONE account per Gmail — `createAccount` now enforces `assertEmailAvailable`, the sign-up Gmail step checks live via the new `checkEmail` action and blocks Continue when taken, and `saveSettings` email changes enforce it too. (2) One account per username (already enforced; now also surfaced as live username SUGGESTIONS — when a name is taken, up to 4 checked-available alternatives appear as one-tap chips). (3) One account per phone number — optional `profiles.phone` (migration 0052), enforced at sign-up and in Settings; log-in accepts Gmail OR phone via `signInWithPassword`'s renamed `identifier` field. (4) Email confirmation — 6-digit codes (SHA-256 hash only, 10-minute TTL, 5 attempts, 60-second resend limit; `email_codes` table, migration 0052) issued by `requestEmailCode`, confirmed at sign-up (code carried into `createAccount`, sets `profiles.email_verified`) or later in Settings via `verifyEmailCode`; skippable at sign-up by design so a mail hiccup never strands a new player. (5) Forgot password — `requestPasswordReset` (generic response, never reveals which emails have accounts) + `resetPassword` (code + new password twice). Codes are delivered by a spawned agent task that hands the send to the mail worker (Muse Mail `mail.handoff`) — the artifact server has no direct mail channel; if delivery fails the sign-up flow still completes (verify later) and reset can be re-requested.
- **Leaderboard sidebar + season countdown (owner's direction)**: `navSectionForTab("leaderboard")` now returns "" — opening the Leaderboard leaves the sidebar on whatever section the player was in instead of jumping to Community. A compact live countdown chip under the Leaderboard title ticks every second to the end of the current season (`clanRankings.seasonEndsAt`); the page otherwise stays list-only with the reward chart at the bottom.
- **Feed — X-style (owner's direction)**: the Create post composer and the hero banner picture are REMOVED from the Feed (posting still lives on profiles; the "Share a GTA post from YouTube / X instead" link stays). The top is now For you / Following tabs (X-style, accent underline); Following is served by `listFeed`'s new `followingOnly` flag (own posts + followed players' posts; Automatic posts excluded). Category / source / sort filters unchanged.
- **Latest & trending carousel (owner's direction)**: Home's Latest & trending is a horizontal auto-sliding carousel (advances every 4 s, loops, pauses on touch/hover, respects prefers-reduced-motion) with up to 8 stories, each with a small picture — original ViceSunshine art only (publisher images are never hotlinked; news rows carry no image of their own) — and tapping a card opens the News page where the story carries its official source link. Home's pale-pink pill buttons (`.btn-pale`) are now white with dark text, per the owner's screenshot.
- **Counts abbreviate from 10,000 (owner's rule)**: `fmtCount` now renders full numbers under 10,000 and 10k / 100k / 1m at and above it, and the remaining raw `toLocaleString()` count spots (profile Posts/Followers/Following, account-menu counts, trending-search counts) route through it — likes, comments, saves, views, followers, following, posts all follow the one rule.
- **Ad placements extended (owner's direction)**: the existing honest AdSlot system (placeholders only until Ben's own AdSense publisher ID exists; Premium members see nothing) gains a `vertical` format and two new placements — a TikTok-style ad slide after every 5 clips in Clips, and a skippable ad break between story groups in the Story viewer (after every second person, auto-continues in 5 s) — alongside the existing Home banner and in-Feed slot (every 4 posts). Frequency stays capped so the breaks never run back-to-back.

## Leaderboard platform locking, crew picture/banner, Feed visual-first, Crew Leaderboard removed-for-now (Nov 19, 2026)

- **PlayStation / Xbox Leaderboard locking (owner's rule)**: on the Leaderboard page, the PlayStation board unlocks only when the viewer's profile has a PSN id linked (`profiles.psn_id` non-empty) and the Xbox board only with an Xbox gamertag linked; both directions lock the same way, neither linked = both locked, both linked = both open, Global/Local never lock. A locked tab shows a padlock + "Locked" and opens a locked panel naming the account to link, with a button to the profile. The board defaults once per visit to the platform the player linked/chose at account creation (PS -> PlayStation, Xbox -> Xbox, both -> their chosen platform, neither -> Global).
- **Crew picture & banner (owner's direction)**: a crew's emblem is its profile picture and its calling card is its banner. Crew home shows the banner header with the emblem over it; every Leaderboard row carries the crew's banner strip (its calling card, or a fallback strip in the crew's own colour with its name until a card is equipped). Leader/Co-Leader get a "Crew picture & banner" editor on the crew home (emblem + calling-card pickers over `getCustomise`/`equipCosmetic` with `clanId`; server enforces Leader/Co-Leader).
- **Feed visual-first (owner's direction)**: post media now renders directly under the header at full column width (pictures 4:3 on phone), the post text runs underneath as the caption, posts get more vertical air, and Discuss/comments stays present but smaller/secondary (comments still open in the Instagram-style sheet). Quote Reposts keep their take on top of the quoted original.
- **Crew Leaderboard REMOVED for now — BACKUP / RESTORE**: hidden behind the single client switch `CREW_LEADERBOARD_ENABLED = false` in `client/src/App.tsx` (next to `RANK_BOARDS`). Nothing was deleted: the `clanRankings` server action, all Rank Points/standings data, the Home bar-chart card, the Crews entry card and the full `LeaderboardView` (including the platform locking above) are all intact, just not rendered (their queries are `enabled`-gated off too). The Leaderboard page shows an "off for now" note meanwhile. TO RESTORE: set `CREW_LEADERBOARD_ENABLED = true` and rebuild — the leaderboard returns exactly as it was.

## Codes & Unlocks, Missions removal, banner fix, daily tracker, ads everywhere, Community Discord (Nov 20, 2026)

- **GTA 6 Codes & Unlocks (owner's brief)**: a dedicated special tab inside Guides ("Codes & Unlocks", gradient-bordered, separate from the guide categories). All 61 codes live in ONE easy-to-edit list, `client/src/codes-data.ts` (`GTA6_CODES`): each row is name / category / description / unlock requirement / `code` (a D-pad direction array, or `null`). A row with `code: null` renders grey "Code not yet known — not out yet" with no code buttons and a "Not out yet" pill; setting a direction array flips it to "Verified in-game" automatically — that is the whole update mechanism, no page changes needed. Counts: PLAYER 10, COMBAT 11, VEHICLES 21, ENVIRONMENT 3, SCREEN FILTERS 16; 17 known / 44 not yet known. Page carries the exact title "CODES & UNLOCKS - 61 codes for GTA 6", the amber warning "Using codes will disable all Achievement/Trophy progression and disable saving!", and the how-to (phone via Up on the D-pad, hold L1/LB, press R1/RB, enter the combo; codes appear in the Progress menu's Codes List after the story — rendered "Codes List", never "Cheats", per the owner's naming rule). Search covers name, description, unlock requirement and category; category chips carry counts; a "17 of 61 codes verified" progress line updates itself from the data. Text / Symbols toggle at the top: Text (default) shows UP / RIGHT / DOWN / LEFT word chips, Symbols shows ↑ → ↓ ←; the choice persists in localStorage (`vicesunshine-codes-display-v1`), switches every code instantly with no reload, and Symbols mode carries an aria-label spelling the full text sequence.
- **Codes data provenance (Nov 20, 2026 research)**: the brief named tracker.gg as the only source, but no tracker.gg codes page could be found. The published 61-code lists from KeenGamer (https://www.keengamer.com/articles/guides/gta-6-all-cheat-codes-for-ps5-and-xbox-and-how-to-unlock-them/), space4games (https://space4games.com/en/games-en/gta-6-cheat-codes/), Dexerto (https://www.dexerto.com/wikis/gta-6/cheat-codes/) and Polygon (https://www.polygon.com/gta-6-cheat-codes-list-unlock/) agree with each other and match the brief's 17 known codes exactly (names, unlock requirements and D-pad sequences, including Attack Force's 9 inputs). Two sequence conflicts were resolved by the two full-table sources agreeing: Cycle Weather is the 7-input version (Dexerto lists an 8-input variant) and Unlimited Focus follows KeenGamer/space4games at input 8. DIVERGENCE FROM THE BRIEF, kept visible on purpose: the brief's placeholder names for the 44 unknown codes (Lower Heat Level, Unlimited Health, Unlimited Stamina, Slow Motion, Super Jump, Fast Run; Exploding Fists, One Hit Kill, No Wanted, Super Punch, Weapon Spawn; Change Time, Clear Weather) appear in NO published list — the published unknown names (Drunk, Raise Wanted Level, No Fall Damage, Parachute, Repair Vehicle, Unlimited Nitrous; All Explosive Weapons, Incendiary Ammo, Exploding Bottles, Explosive Shotgun, Hauners H-AT; Move Time Forward, Pause Time of Day; the 18 vehicle spawns and 13 filter names in the data file) are what the page shows, with the brief's per-category unknown counts (6 / 5 / 18 / 2 / 13) matching exactly. The brief's invented names were not presented as real codes.
- **Missions Database REMOVED, mission guides moved to Guides (owner's direction)**: the Missions tab, nav entry, search group, Saved section, profile stat tiles and the server actions `listMissions` / `addMission` / `toggleMission` are gone, along with the mission XP rate (+10), the daily cap, the "Story Hunter" season challenge / missions season metric, `missionsDone` on profiles, the "mission" account-save kind, and Ask Vice's Missions knowledge (it now points at Guides). Migration 0053 first copies every `missions` row into `guides` as category "Missions" (title = mission name; body composed of kind / region / characters / walkthrough / rewards / tips / official source link / video-guide link; author "ViceSunshine Guides", deduped by title+category), remaps `account_saves` rows of kind "mission" to the matching new guide rows, deletes unmapped leftovers, then drops `mission_progress` and `missions`; the schema exports are removed. Earned XP history (`rank_point_events`) is untouched. The profile's tracked-goals card keeps its news/events/posts list (renamed `TrackedGoalsCard`). Guides gains the "Missions" category content through the normal library + search (global search now has a Guides group instead of a Missions group).
- **Feed / Clips banner bug (owner's report)**: the Clips top banner looked fixed because it sat ABOVE the full-screen snap scroller, which swallowed every scroll gesture while the banner never moved. The banner, title/actions and For You / Following tabs now live INSIDE the scroller as its first snap-start block (the reel container grew to fill the freed space): scrolling down moves the banner up and out of view and lands on the first clip; scrolling back to the top brings it back. The Feed itself has no hero banner and nothing sticky at its top — its tabs and Stories row are normal flow inside the shell's single `main` scroller, so they scroll away with the page.
- **Home stats row is a daily tracker + full history (owner's direction)**: new `getDailyStats` action counts rows created since the viewer's local midnight (passed in as `dayStart`; UTC-midnight fallback) for news / updates / bugs / open LFG teams / events, alongside all-time totals. The Home row shows today's count big, the label "… today", and the all-time total beneath; it refetches every 30 s and resets at midnight. Every tile is a button opening the new History tab (a detail view — Back returns to Home at the same spot) with category chips (News / Updates / Bugs / LFG teams / Events, each with its all-time total), a search box, and every item ever posted grouped by day (Today / Yesterday / full date), newest first, each row linking onward to its home section. News groups by its published date; events by their start date.
- **Ads — high-traffic placements, theme-matched (owner's direction)**: the AdSlot placeholder was restyled from a dashed box to a theme panel (dark navy, soft hot-pink → neon-purple → teal gradient via the `.ad-slot` class in theme.css, a gradient "Ad" pill, honest "Ad space — Reserved for ads when ViceSunshine is published" copy) — still never a fake advert, still no script and no impressions until Ben's own AdSense publisher ID is set, and Premium members still see NOTHING anywhere (AdSlot returns null). Placements now: Home top banner + a mid-page slot after the Latest & trending carousel, Feed in-feed (every 4 posts), Clips interstitial slides (every 5 clips), Stories ad breaks, News after the featured story, Profile at the bottom of the page, and the SIDEBAR: three thin banner strips stacked directly above Create Account — each spans the full sidebar width edge to edge at 36px tall (about one sidebar row, the smallest sensible strip), clearly labelled, hiding automatically on viewports under 760px tall so the pinned account trigger always fits (`.sidebar-ad-stack`). Plus occasional TRANSITION ads: every 5th page move (tab taps, detail opens, idea opens — funnelled through `go` / `goDetail` / `openEgg`), at most once per 3 minutes, a small labelled ad card slides in at the bottom of the screen; the destination page loads underneath immediately, the card never blocks the page, it has a Close button and dismisses itself after 5 seconds. Premium never sees any of these.
- **Community Discord section (owner's direction)**: the Community sidebar dropdown gains a "Discord" entry opening a permanent Discord page in Community (banner art, the server benefits, and a Join button to the live invite https://discord.gg/NDH9XxyVkW — read from `getDiscordInvite`, with the owner's invite as the fallback). This is a destination page, separate from the Create Account card (which still appears once, only pre-join, on the sign-up page); the Community Feed itself still carries no Discord card. The benefits list no longer mentions crew wars (removed feature) — it now says "Crew channels".
- **Correction — Discord lives ONLY in Community > Discord (owner's correction, supersedes the Create Account card rule)**: the Join Discord card was removed from Create Account Step 2 entirely (the step is now just the linked-accounts fields, single column, Save & continue at the bottom) and the `DiscordServerCard` component is deleted. The Community > Discord tab (`DiscordView`) is the server's only home in the app; the Settings → Connectors admin that stores the invite link stays (it feeds `getDiscordInvite`, which the Discord tab reads).

## Messages — X Chat layout, group chats, crew chats, event chats (Nov 20, 2026)

- Messages restyled after Ben's X Chat screenshot: "Chat" title, All/Requests filter pill, pill Search, empty-inbox state ("Your inbox is empty — Conversations you have will show up here"), a Suggested section (avatar, name, relation line — "Crewmate" / "Following" / "Followed by people you follow" from real follow data — and a Chat button), and a right pane "Start Conversation" empty state with a "New chat" button.
- Two categories in Messages: **Personal** (DMs, player-made group chats, and a Following box listing everyone the player follows, newest follow first, each with a Chat button) and **Crews & Events** (crew chats + event chats).
- Group chats (`chat_groups` kind "group", migration 0055): the creator can add **anyone they follow** — any crew or none; following is the only rule. Owner can remove members; leaving transfers ownership; the last leaver deletes the group.
- Crew chats (kind "crew") are **automatic**: `ensureCrewChats` runs on every Messages read and syncs each crew chat's membership exactly to the crew's member list. Creating a crew creates its chat with the Leader in first; joining a crew joins its chat; leaving a crew leaves its chat. The chat carries the crew's identity — name, emblem (tag/colour/emblem), banner (the crew's calling card) and settings (platform, privacy, region) — all read live from the crew row, so leader edits on the crew page flow into the chat.
- **One crew at a time** is enforced server-side (`assertNotInAnotherCrew`) in create crew, join crew, and application approval — the check runs before any Vice Credits are charged. Leaving a crew (`leaveClan`: leadership passes to the highest-ranked longest-standing member, or a one-member crew dissolves including its chat) is the way out.
- Event chats (kind "event", migration 0057): `ensureEventChats` creates one chat per event for everyone RSVP'd Going plus the host, who leads it. The host can **mute** a member (they can read but not send — `chat_group_members.muted`) and remove someone from the event entirely, which also removes them from the chat and blocks rejoining that event.

## Crews — hype, My crew / Discover, crew page as profile (Nov 20, 2026)

- The Crews tab now opens on **your crew's profile** when you are in one (My crew / Discover switcher), and on Discover otherwise. Discover leads with a live hero (crew/member/recruiting counts), a "Live in crews right now" activity pulse (`listCrewActivityFeed`, real `clan_activity` rows), a Suggested/Trending strip (top crews by members then trophies, with banner, emblem, member counts, spots left, View + Join), and crew cards that show their calling-card banner.
- The crew page works like a profile: the Leader gets an **Edit crew** form on the page (name, tag, motto, description, platform, playstyle, privacy, region, language, colour — `updateClan`), member management (promote/demote/remove, application review) was already on the page, and every member gets a **Leave crew** control with role-aware consequences.

## Community Events — pictures, kick, countdown, 32 cap (Nov 20, 2026)

- Every event card shows, **before joining**: a picture header, a live **countdown** chip to the start ("Starts in …", ticking every second), the **creator** ("Hosted by …"), and **how many people are participating** ("{going}/{maxPlayers} going" chip + going/maybe counts + spots left).
- **32-player cap**: create/edit forms and the server schemas cap `maxPlayers` at 32 (a full GTA lobby); every card states "max {n} players (GTA lobby limit: 32)". Pre-existing example events created with 30 players are a host choice under the cap, not the limit itself.
- **Event pictures**: the default picture follows the event **type** (`eventArtForType` — original ViceSunshine illustrations generated for this build: racing→neon street race, off-road→swamp buggies, crew event→rival crews with a tank, cruise→convoy, Easter egg hunt→treasure map, fashion→neon runway, watch party→rooftop cinema; Car Meet keeps the existing promo art). The leader can **upload their own picture** (JPG/PNG, ≤ 4 MB, moderated like avatar uploads, migration 0056 `events.image_blob_key`) which replaces the theme art everywhere; removing it restores the theme art.
- **Kick**: the host/leader can remove an attendee (`removeEventAttendee`); the removal is recorded (`event_kicked`) and that player cannot rejoin the event, and drops out of the event chat automatically.
- **Discord**: the optional Discord link/code is the event's lobby link — it is only returned by the server to players who RSVP'd Going (and the host), so non-joiners never see it. The Events page intro now points to the Community > Discord tab for the server invite (the old "join card on the Create Account page" copy was removed with the card).

## Bug tracker goes automatic, Guides/Codes visual-first, Database expansion + realism art, Tracker-style map & filters, Eggs alignment (Nov 20, 2026)

- **Bug & glitch tracker — automatic + community (owner's brief)**: `bugs` gained `source_name` / `source_url` / `auto` (migration 0058). The new `refreshBugs` action (throttled to one real check per 10 minutes via meta `last_bugs_refresh_at`) web-searches GTA VI bug/glitch reports from Rockstar/GTA sources, news and creators/influencers (YouTube included), extracts ONLY specific bugs a result explicitly describes via `ctx.inference` (title, platform only when stated, description, workaround only when stated, `sourceUrl` copied exactly from the result), dedupes by source URL and by title, caps 6 per run, and stores them as Reported with `auto = 1` and the source host as `sourceName`. Inference failure falls back to clear video/article results whose titles name a bug/glitch (cap 3) — still grounded in real results, never invented. Download/crack/mod-menu results are excluded. Schedule: silent `space_action` cron `vicesunshine-auto-bugs-refresh` every 3h (in `space.json` managedCronJobs), plus a throttled trigger when the Bugs page opens. Community reporting is unchanged and stays open to everyone (`reportBug`, `auto = 0`); the page separates the two with origin badges (Community report / Official — Rockstar / Automatic), a source filter (All / Automatic / From players / Official — Rockstar), per-bug "View source" links for automatic finds, and a live line ("bugs & glitches …" in the automation status + owner dashboard row). A bug is still only Confirmed after multiple players reproduce it ("I have this too" votes).
- **Guides / Codes — visual-first, less text (owner's brief)**: five new original ViceSunshine illustrations generated for the Codes categories (`code-player.webp`, `code-combat.webp`, `code-vehicles.webp`, `code-environment.webp`, `code-filters.webp` in `client/src/assets/`, all original, no Rockstar art/stills/logos, visually checked). Every Codes category section now leads with its topic-matched picture banner (shown once per category), the Codes intro collapsed to a 4-step visual strip (phone up → hold LB/L1 → tap RB/R1 → enter the code) + one short warning line, code cards and the code detail page were trimmed to the essentials, and guide cards show their category's topic-matched illustration as a banner with guide-tile art enlarged. Less text everywhere; pictures carry the meaning.
- **Database — full catalog, Tracker-style structure (owner's briefs)**: the team catalog in `refreshDbEntries` grew by 200 entries to 155 vehicles (every Tracker-style class: Muscle 24, Off-Road, Sports, Super, Sports Classics, Coupes, Sedans, SUVs, Pickups, Vans, Motorcycles, Scooter, Cycles, Boats, Planes, Helicopters, Emergency, Industrial, Service, Utility), 58 weapons (Assault Rifles, Handguns, SMGs, Shotguns, LMGs, Sniper Rifles, Heavy, Melee), 12 characters (Protagonist / Supporting / Associate — Brian Heder, Cal Hampton, Raul Bautista, Roxy added) and a new **Locations** section (15 entries grouped by Kind: Barber, Clothing, Gun store, Mod shop, Other, Safehouse, Storage facility, Tattoo shop — One Eyed Willies, Rideout Customs, Big Shots Wholesale Weapons, Island Dreams, Jack of Hearts, Key Lento Fire Department, Mami's Grocery Market, Megamundo, The Rusty Anchor, Three Sheets Liquor, Venture Apartments, Jason's Safehouse, Lucia's Apartment, Paradise Garage, Electric Fang Tattoo). Names/classes mirror the structure of public GTA trackers, but every status stays honest: GTA V items are "Returning from GTA V" (never confirmed for GTA VI), rumoured names are "Unverified", trailer-spotted locations/characters are "Reported" with the official Rockstar GTA VI page as the verify link. Vehicles gained `type` and `drivetrain` (migration 0059; drivetrain only where the GTA V layout is known). "Cars" is renamed "Vehicles" everywhere (Database, Saved carousel, guide category — the DB sync rewrites stored "Cars" guides to "Vehicles").
- **Database art — realism v3 + distinct per entry (owner-confirmed)**: `DB_ART_STYLE` now demands near-photorealistic AAA game-render detail (realistic proportions/materials/reflections) while staying an original illustration — not a photograph, screenshot, or copy of Rockstar art, no text/logos. Every entry has its own distinct spec in `DB_ART_SPECS` (unique body shape, era, colour and signature details per vehicle — the fix for "same car, different angle": all vehicles now share ONE three-quarter-front studio framing, so the only difference between two pictures is the vehicle itself). `seedArtwork` runs a one-time v3 regeneration pass (meta `db_art_v3_ids`, vehicles first, 6 per run on the existing artwork schedule) repainting all existing art once in the new style; new entries paint with v3 prompts from the start. Entries still waiting show the per-entry monogram tile — never a reused picture. Detail pages keep the "See original picture" external verify link.
- **Database UI — Tracker-style, better (owner's briefs)**: "All entries" is now a Database home (big GTA VI DATABASE hero, Released Nov 19, 2026 line, Featured Interactive Map card with "Open the Map" — the fan-made concept schematic, labelled not official — four section cards with live counts, Featured entries, Recently Updated, Latest News). Sections use grid cards with the entry's own picture, status badge, class/manufacturer/drivetrain chips and community rating, grouped by Class / Role / Kind with counts. Filters work like the reference: dimension buttons (Class, Type, Manufacturer, Drivetrain for Vehicles; Class for Weapons; Role for Characters; Kind for Locations) open a Filters panel with multi-select chips, Reset All and Save Changes (draft → applied). Status chips stay visible (they mirror the automatic badges). Sidebar GTA VI Database gains Locations.
- **Map — Tracker-style layout, own schematic only (owner's brief)**: the Map page now has a left panel (stacks on top on phones) with "Search the map", a Day / Night toggle (schematic dims to a night palette), and pin categories with counts + Hide/Show toggles (Shops, Food and drink, Activities, Racing and stunts, Travel, Collectibles, Places) over the existing zoom / cluster / measure / notes tools. Pins are ViceSunshine's own only: the five trailer-known regions (Places) and reported Easter egg finds (Collectibles, amber pins); shop/food/activity counts are honestly 0 until players verify locations after launch. The label stands everywhere: "Map not released yet — fan-made concept schematic only, not official, not to scale", locations Unverified / Reported. Tracker.gg's map image and pin data were NOT copied — only the tools/layout pattern.
- **Easter Eggs — alignment + in-grid ad (owner's brief)**: egg cards are now equal height/width flex columns — fixed-height badges row, two-line title, three-line description, note, and the Open / Save / Verify action row pinned to the card bottom (mt-auto) so buttons share one baseline across each row. One theme-matched house ad sits inside the grid as a card of its own after the second egg (`.ad-slot` styling, "Ad" pill, ViceSunshine Premium creative in site colours, same fixed rows so the grid stays aligned); Premium members never see it.
- **Map moved (owner's direction)**: Map is out of the sidebar (Explore nav entry removed; the tab itself is unchanged). Home gains an "Interactive Map" card under the Today stats in `HomeStatsRow` (sec-map art, fan-made/not-official chip, "Open the Map"), and the Database home's Featured Interactive Map card also opens it.

## Follow-up round 3 (2026-11-20) — Database art completion, full map, Stories row, Create tab/creator, Feed refresh, Rockstar in Feed, hero, memes, moderation, player reports
- **Database art — no letter tiles anywhere (owner's brief)**: the 82 entries with no picture (all 15 Locations, 4 Characters, most Weapons, 12 Vehicles) are painted by the existing per-entry generation pipeline (`DB_ART_SPECS` + `generateDbArtFor`, original near-photoreal game-render illustrations, never Rockstar art/stills/logos). `seedDbArt` now interleaves missing entries round-robin across kinds (Weapons first) and `seedArtwork` paints 12 per run, missing-first (repaints wait). The client fallback tile is no longer a monogram: entries still painting show a full-frame dark-studio scene with a recognisable per-kind/category silhouette and a "Painting the {name}…" caption — no letters, no "?", no reused picture. The Database view self-heals: while any loaded entry lacks a picture it triggers `seedDbArt` in small batches and refreshes until real pictures land.
- **Full interactive map (owner's brief, Tracker-style layout, own data only)**: left panel "GTA 6 MAP / Interactive map of Leonida" with search, pins-shown count + Show all, and the owner's exact category groups with counts and Show/Hide — Services (Safehouses, Mod shops, Pawn shops, High-end jewelry, Big banks, Towable ATMs, Gun stores, Convenience, Jewelry, Banks, Payday loans), Food and Drink (Fast food, Liquor stores), Activities (Golf), Racing and Stunts (Stunt jumps, Base jumps, Knife flights, Under the bridge), Travel (Standout vehicles, Randy vehicles, Moses vehicles, Ferry terminals, Trains), Collectibles (Collectible cards, Safes) — 224 ViceSunshine schematic pins over an original SVG Leonida landmass (blue sea, green landmass, Keys chain, Vice City grid hint), Day/Night toggle, zoom +/− and fullscreen bottom right. Labelled fan-made concept schematic, not official, locations unverified; Tracker.gg's map image and pin data were NOT copied.
- **Stories row (owner's brief, Instagram layout)**: one tight horizontal row of round circles — Your story first (+ badge opens the composer), then people you follow, then recommended people, all as circles in the same scrollable row. `listStories` now also returns `people` (followed first, then recommended by crew/follow graph), circles without a live story open that profile.
- **Create tab + creator (owner's brief, Instagram flow)**: the Feed's tab bar is Create / For you / Following (Create leftmost). Create opens a camera creator: live camera preview (flip, photo capture, up-to-60s clip recording), gallery thumbnail + "Upload from your device instead", then a bottom destination picker — Post / Story / Clips — chosen before publishing, plus a Post category picker (Post, Memes, Funny, Gameplay, Car Meet, Tutorial, Discussion, Screenshot). Publishing routes through `createFeedPost` / `createStory` / `uploadFeedMedia`; guests get a create-account prompt.
- **Feed refresh (owner's brief)**: the Refresh button is gone. Re-pressing the For you / Following tab you are already on scrolls to the top and reloads the latest posts (or latest in the chosen category) every time, without leaving the tab; re-pressing Create restarts the creator fresh.
- **Rockstar in the Feed (owner's brief)**: `refreshFeedMedia` gained a Rockstar official pass — web searches restricted to rockstargames.com (GTA VI + Newswire bonus/money posts); only results actually hosted on rockstargames.com become automatic Feed posts authored "Rockstar Games" (kind News, short snippet summary, source link to Rockstar's original post, editorial image when fetchable), deduped by URL/title. `refreshNews` labels rockstargames.com items Official / "Rockstar Games". Automatic Feed cards now show a clickable "Original post ↗" source link.
- **Home hero (owner's brief)**: `hero-vice.webp` regenerated as a near-photoreal AAA game-render Vice City sunset skyline — art-deco neon hotels in hot pink/purple/teal, palms, causeway over the bay, classic muscle car on wet reflective streets. Original ViceSunshine illustration only; no Rockstar art, stills or logos.
- **GTA memes (owner's brief)**: Memes was already a Feed category/filter with automatic meme-video posts; the Create tab's category picker now lets users post directly into Memes (kind "Meme") alongside the automatic GTA meme posts.
- **Moderation (owner's brief)**: public content only. `screenText` (existing) screens all public posts/comments/stories/crew & group spaces for illegal content (sexual content/minors, leaks/piracy, scams, real-world illegal trade, hate slurs, public contact details) and uploaded images are AI-checked before storage. NEW: `screenProfileName` — a stricter screen for usernames/display names (everything above + profanity + Rockstar/Take-Two impersonation), applied at account creation and every profile save; bios were already screened at both save points. The hourly `automationSweep` also re-screens recent profile names/bios (counted as `profilesFlagged`, listed by name in the owner dashboard — the sweep never edits a profile). **Private one-to-one DMs are explicitly NOT screened or monitored** — the `assertClean` call was removed from `sendDm`; group/crew chats stay screened as shared community spaces.
- **Player reports (owner's brief, standard flow)**: new `player_reports` table (migration 0061) + `reportPlayer` action (one report per reporter per player, details screened). Profiles gain a Report button next to Follow/Message/Block opening the standard flow: reason chips (Illegal content, Bad name, Bad words, Harassment, Hate speech, Spam, False information, Breaking the rules, Something else), optional detail, submit, confirmation that the player went to moderation review and won't be told who reported them. Post reporting was upgraded to the same standard flow (reason chips: Spam, Harassment, Hate speech, Illegal content, False information, Something else + optional detail + existing confirmation). `getOwnerDashboard` now returns `reportedPlayers` (grouped counts, reasons, latest detail) and `flaggedProfiles` (name/bio screen failures with reasons), rendered in the dashboard's Moderation queue.

## Follow-up round 4 (2026-11-20, later) — detail pages, character redo (final direction), Database tabs/filters, quiet ads
- **Database detail pages (owner's brief, Tracker-style layout, own data only)**: tapping any entry now opens a full detail page (replacing the inline expanding card): breadcrumb (GTA VI » Section » Manufacturer/Group » Name), Back button, See original picture + Save, a hero with the entry's own big picture beside name / manufacturer · class / status badge / description, PERFORMANCE bars (Speed, Acceleration, Handling, Strength for vehicles; Damage, Fire rate, Accuracy, Range for weapons) and a RATING bar from the real community rating. Tabs: Overview / Stats / Gallery / Locations / Variants. Overview holds the INFO table (Manufacturer, Class, Type, Drivetrain, real-life counterpart, Seats, Doors, Storage — real fields where stored, TBD where not), AVAILABILITY & PRICING (Content pack / Patch TBD, Game release Nov 19, 2026, prices TBD), a LOCATIONS panel ("Spawn locations coming soon" + View on map; locations link to the concept map as Unverified) and the RATE card (signed-in 1–5 rating, unchanged `rateDbEntry`). Gallery shows the entry picture full-size plus the section illustration; Variants lists real same-manufacturer / same-class entries, tappable. NO stats or prices are invented — everything unknown is TBD / Unverified. Share on X / Copy link buttons from the reference were NOT copied: the app shell owns sharing and the page URL is never read.
- **Character redo — final direction (owner's correction)**: all 12 character specs now demand the same face, facial features, hair AND signature outfit as the game character, in GTA VI's graphics style, as original fan-made ViceSunshine illustrations — not copies of Rockstar artwork, trailer stills or screenshots, labelled fan-made/not official everywhere they render. A one-time repaint pass (`db_art_char2_ids` in meta) repaints every character portrait once with the new specs, after missing pictures and before the general v3 repaint, 12 per `seedArtwork` run.
- **Database tabs & filters (owner's brief)**: the status filter row (All statuses / Confirmed / Reported-Rumor / Unverified / Returning) is REMOVED from the Database page, and the four status links are removed from the sidebar's GTA VI Database dropdown — status badges on entries themselves stay (they are generated from the data). "All entries" now sits on its own full-width row at the very top, above the Vehicles / Weapons / Characters / Locations pills.
- **Ads — many but quiet (owner's correction)**: placements multiplied while keeping the existing subtle, theme-matched slim-strip design (labelled "Ad", honest "reserved for ads" copy until an AdSense ID exists, Premium sees nothing anywhere): sidebar ×3 thin strips, Home top/mid/bottom, Feed in-feed every 4 posts + bottom, Clips interstitial, Stories interstitial, News after-featured + bottom, Database top + detail page, Profile bottom. No loud boxed takeovers — the Tracker-style boxed ad with an Upgrade pitch was dropped in favour of blending in.

## Full map rebuild (Nov 20, 2026) — "the map is so bad"
- **Reference (owner-supplied, layout/look only)**: Ben sent the exact TRN map URL to mirror — https://tracker.gg/gta6/map?hide=fast-food.liquor-store.golf.stunt-jump.base-jump.knife-flight.under-the-bridge.standout-vehicle.randy-vehicle.moses-vehicle.ferry.train.collectible-card.safe.curiosity&at=-702_707_2.8 — the page is JS-rendered and would not load in the builder's browser tools, so the rebuild follows Ben's own screenshot + written brief from the earlier round (same panel, same category groups/counts, Day/Night, zoom, fullscreen). Only the layout/feel pattern is followed: Tracker.gg's map image, coastline and pin data were NOT copied, and no asset was fetched from that page.
- **New landmass (the actual fix)**: the old 21-point straight-line polygon is replaced by ViceSunshine's own original Leonida coastline — ~44 hand-placed control points (`LAND_POINTS`) smoothed in code (Catmull-Rom → Bézier, `smoothClosedPath`) into a Florida-shaped state with bays and inlets, drawn with a sandy beach rim over shallow-water shelf rings on the blue sea. Detail layers: Lake Leonida (centre) + a northern pond, two rivers, terrain (northern forest + Mount Kalaga ridge triangles, Ambrosia farm-belt patchwork, south-west wetlands), urban areas (Vice City blob + downtown core + glow, Port Gellhorn, Ambrosia, five small towns), street grids (Vice City, its beach barrier island, Port Gellhorn — clipped to land), a five-route highway network with cream centre lines, and causeways to the beach island and along the six-island Keys chain. Night style darkens the whole palette and lights glowing city dots over Vice City / Port Gellhorn / Ambrosia. All of it is original schematic art — fan-made, not official, not to scale, locations unverified, labelled as before.
- **Pins, cleaned up**: pins are no longer 224 overlapping rounded-square chips. Each pin is now a proper teardrop map marker (category colour, white ring, category glyph, drop shadow, tip anchored on its spot), and pins cluster the way big interactive maps do: at low zoom nearby pins fold into one count bubble in the dominant category's colour that zooms in on tap and splits as you zoom (cell 15/zoom schematic units). Pin placement is now land-aware: `mapOnLand` (mainland/beach-island polygon test, Keys island ellipses, lake exclusion) re-samples every generated pin until it sits on land, so no pin floats in the sea or the lake. All groups now start visible (Show all state) with the panel's counts unchanged; the schematic grid now starts OFF (toggle still in Setup) so the map reads as a map, not graph paper.
- **Chrome polish**: the floating toolbars are slim glass icon rails (line icon + 8px label, dark blur, soft shadow) instead of chunky text blocks; the viewport is taller (560px phone / 700px desktop); fullscreen now actually fills the screen (`.map-card:fullscreen` CSS stretches the viewport; 62vh on phones where the panel stacks). Region label positions were re-based to the new geography with per-region offsets so labels sit on land. The panel's category rows are now a single-column list (TRN-style) at 340px wide so no category name truncates ("High-end jewelry", "Towable ATMs", "Convenience" all read in full). A first probe pass showed the smoothed coast still read as an oval with a heavy rim, so the coastline points were re-shaped (flatter wider north, straighter west coast, Vice City bay notch, jagged south-west wetlands) and the beach rim + road widths were thinned to real-map weight. The two-step Featured card, left panel contents (GTA 6 MAP / Interactive map of Leonida, Search the map..., pins-shown + Show all, the owner's exact Services / Food and Drink / Activities / Racing and Stunts / Travel / Collectibles groups with counts + Show/Hide), Day/Night toggle, zoom +/−, fullscreen, measure/notes tools and the five trailer-known region pins are all unchanged in behaviour.

## Full map rebuild, round 2 (Nov 20, 2026) — exact TRN layout study

Only the layout/look pattern was followed — no TRN map image, coastline, pin positions or pin data were copied or fetched; the landmass, roads and labels are the same original ViceSunshine schematic from round 1, extended (airfield, Grassrivers channels, minor roads, minor labels: Hamlet, Grassrivers, Waning Sands, Bayside, Ocean Beach, Starfish, Key Lento, Vice City International Airport).
- **Counts**: the category list, order and per-category pin counts are the owner's own dictated spec for this schematic (Services 252 incl. Towable ATMs 99, Food and Drink 35, Activities 2, Racing and Stunts 397, Travel 105, Collectibles 130, Places 99 — 1,020 schematic pins total). The pins are still ViceSunshine's own, generated onto our landmass with the land-aware sampler; the counts are targets for our schematic, not imported pin data. The map opens at "316 of 1020 pins shown": the categories the owner's reference link hides (Food and Drink, Golf, all Racing and Stunts, all Travel, Collectible cards, Safes, Curiosities) start hidden, and Towable ATMs count as shown but only draw at zoom ≥ 1.8 with a "Zoom in to see these pins" note under their row. A new Places group (Landmarks 31, Curiosities 35, Sculptures 23, Contacts 10) joins the panel; Sculptures pins are a bare black eight-pointed asterisk with no tile, as in the reference.

Mark done is stored on-device (`viceverse-map-done-v1`) and dims the pin; Copy link copies a generated text reference for the pin (the page's own URL is never read — the shell owns sharing). The floating icon toolbars were removed from the map face; every tool (Pins/Art/Grid/Labels toggles, 3D tilt, Measure, Note, Undo, Reset, Clear) moved into a Map tools section at the bottom of the panel, and the region list remains below the map. The two-step Featured card, fan-made/not-official labelling (panel note + dismissible centre pill), known-regions cards and notes list are unchanged.

## Map — YANIS V18 tiled base map (edit, Nov 20 2026, owner's option 1)
- **Decision (owner, supersedes rounds 1–2 above)**: the flat original schematic landmass is GONE from the Map tab. The full interactive map now renders the openly licensed community base map — **YANIS V18 by Yanis and the GTA VI Mapping Community, via GTADB, CC BY 4.0** — as a real tile pyramid, exactly like TRN and the other fan sites that use it with credit. The TRN-matching panel/search/counts/pins/controls from round 2 are unchanged (385px floating dark panel, "316 of 1020 pins shown" + Show all, exact groups/counts, square pin tiles, Day/Night, zoom, Show whole map, Fullscreen, search that pans to a pin, pin card with Mark done + Copy link, Towable ATMs only at street-level zoom — now zoom ≥ 5.5 in the new 0.55–16 zoom range).
- **Tile source (verified live at build time)**: GTADB's own tile host `https://maps.gtadb.org/tiles/6/yanis,18/{z}/{z},{y},{x}.jpg` refuses hotlinking (403 unless the Referer is map.gtadb.org — tested with curl), so tiles load from the community's public CDN that fan sites use: `https://tiles.gtamap.io/gta6/community-v18-4x/{z}/{x}/{y}.webp` (512px WebP, zoom 0–8, XYZ scheme; pattern and placement config — nativeZoom 8, tileSize 512, offset [21504, 21504], image 59392×80896, gameToPixel a=4 c=44032 e=-4 f=44032, background #2c68a6 — taken from GTAMap.io's own published map config on https://gtamap.io/ , fetched Nov 20 2026; tiles verified 200 with no/foreign referer at z0/z2/z4/z6, and a full z4 stitch was assembled at build time to calibrate geography). GTAMap.io builds these tiles from the GTADB.ORG community map (Yanis v18) under CC BY 4.0, per its disclaimer https://gtamap.io/disclaimer/ .
- **Credit (CC BY 4.0 requirement), shown on the Map page** (panel footer + an always-visible bar under the map): "GTA VI Map Tiles (CC BY 4.0) — https://map.gtadb.org/" with links to https://map.gtadb.org/ and the licence https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/ , crediting YANIS / the GTA VI Mapping Community and GTADB, plus GTAMap.io as tile host. GTADB credits page: https://gtadb.net/gta-6-map/credits/ . Fan-made / not official / locations unverified labelling is kept everywhere (banner, panel, notice pill, pin card).
- **Engine**: hand-rolled tile layer in MapView (no external map library): ResizeObserver stage size → contain-fit base scale over a native-px crop [14400,16000]–[84800,109600] of the 131,072px world; per-frame tile level chosen so tiles render near-native (screen tile ≈ 360–720px); only tiles intersecting the YANIS art rect are requested; sea background is the tiles' own sea colour #2c68a6 (sampled from a blank tile); Night is the same tiles under a dark filter. Pan/zoom are multiplicative (0.55–16×) so street grids, Grassrivers channels, roads and labels stay sharp at deep zoom. The coordinates readout and the pin card now show GTADB-grid game coordinates (X/Y in metres, origin at world centre); Measure reports metres on that grid.
- **Pins on the new geography**: ViceSunshine's 1,020 schematic pins were re-based onto the YANIS V18 coastline — a traced land polygon + island ellipses (Vice Beach barrier island, NE barrier chain, west islets, Port Gellhorn island, Keys chain) and pin zones re-centred (Vice City SE u71/v41, Ambrosia centre, Grassrivers S-central, Port Gellhorn W, Kalaga N, Keys SW) with the same land-aware sampler and the same counts. Region pins/labels moved to the real YANIS positions (Vice City 70.9/41.2, Leonida Keys 40.1/73.0, Port Gellhorn 20.3/28.6, Ambrosia 47.6/57.1, Mount Kalaga 51.7/21.2). Pins remain ViceSunshine's own schematic placements — no GTADB/TRN pin data was imported — and every pin stays labelled unverified. Easter-egg pins only draw where their stored position falls on the new landmass.
